Method: 以基础流模型 Wan2.1 为骨干构建 VoRTeC：(1) 将视频潜在表示紧凑编码；(2) 预测压缩潜在表示沿流轨迹的位置（即单步采样所需的流时间位置）；(3) 集成多尺度先验以增强生成质量。整个框架无需访问流匹配网络的参数或梯度；同时通过尾帧复用与先验缓存策略维持帧组间的一致性。

Result: 实验表明，相比此前的扩散式压缩方法，VoRTeC 将比特消耗降低 58%；解码速度提升 3 至 197 倍，达到 720p 下 13 FPS、480p 下 32 FPS 的实时解码性能，同时重建结果具有较高的感知保真度。

Conclusion: VoRTeC 验证了将生成式流先验与紧凑潜在编码、单步轨迹预测相结合，可在超低码率下同时兼顾高感知质量、低解码延迟和时序一致性，为生成式视频压缩提供了一条高效可行路径。

Abstract: Ultra-low bitrate video compression still faces critical challenges: traditional neural video compression inevitably introduces blurring artifacts, while diffusion-based generative video compression suffers from excessive decoding latency and poor temporal consistency. To address these issues, we propose $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$, a Video Compression framework built upon a foundational flow model (Wan2.1). By compactly encoding latent video representations, predicting the positions of compressed representations along flow trajectories, and integrating multi-scale priors, $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$ enables the compressor to harness generative video flow priors effectively. Without accessing the parameters or gradients of flow matching networks, our framework achieves one-step decoding and reconstructions with high perceptual fidelity. Meanwhile, we maintain consistency across frame groups via tail-frame reuse and prior caching. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method reduces bit consumption by 58\% compared to prior diffusion-based approaches, with decoding speed boosted by 3 to 197 times: $\mathtt{VoRTeC}$ achieves a decoding speed of 13 FPS at 720p and 32 FPS at 480p.

</details>


### [64] [Domain shift-robust object detection with GenAI image editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02299)
*Isabel D. Stein,Thijs A. Eker,Sebastiaan P. Snel,Ella P. Fokkinga,Klamer Schutte,Luca Ambrogioni,Friso G. Heslinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用扩散模型（Diffusion）进行生成式图像编辑，通过向训练数据合成添加伪装（如树叶、网罩、多光谱伪装）来模拟域偏移，从而提升目标检测器在真实域偏移场景下的鲁棒性。在迷彩军用车辆检测任务中，生成式伪装增强显著提升了GroundingDINO在树叶（+20.1 mAP）和网罩（+14.4 mAP）伪装上的性能，LoRA微调进一步改善多光谱伪装的生成效果（+4.4 mAP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测器在域偏移（如光照、天气、遮挡变化）下性能严重退化，因为它们依赖从训练分布中学到的视觉捷径（visual shortcuts），难以跨域泛化。在低数据量的专业场景中，采集覆盖域变化的真实样本尤其困难。虽然扩散模型已被用于提升检测器的域内性能，但其改善跨域鲁棒性的潜力尚未被充分探索。作者假设生成式图像编辑可以模拟受控的域偏移，从而弥合源域与目标域之间的差距。

Method: 以迷彩军用车辆检测作为具有挑战性的域偏移场景（15类车辆、地面近距离图像）。使用两种扩散编辑模型（Qwen Image Edit 2509和Flux.2 Dev）以及LoRA微调版Qwen，将伪装合成添加到未伪装训练数据中；以非生成式的黑条遮挡（black-bar occlusion）作为增强质量的下限基线；使用GroundingDINO检测器在真实与合成数据混合的训练集上进行评估。

Result: 生成式伪装增强在树叶伪装上带来+20.1 mAP、网罩伪装上+14.4 mAP的显著提升；多光谱伪装的生成更具挑战性，但LoRA微调使性能比未伪装基线提升4.4 mAP。生成式方法明显优于非生成式黑条遮挡基线。

Conclusion: 扩散驱动的生成式图像编辑能够模拟可控的域偏移（如伪装），作为合成数据增强手段可以有效弥合源域与目标域差距，增强目标检测器在低数据专业场景下的跨域鲁棒性；LoRA微调可针对难以生成的域偏移类型（如多光谱伪装）进一步优化，为生成式数据增强用于跨域鲁棒性提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Object detectors often degrade under domain shifts such as changes in lighting, weather, or occlusion. These shifts alter object appearance and expose a reliance on visual shortcuts learned from the training distribution that do not generalize across domains. Acquiring sufficient real-world samples to capture such domain variation is particularly difficult in specialized, low-data settings. Recent advances in diffusion-based generative image editing have shown promise for improving the in-domain performance of object detectors through synthetic data augmentation. However, their potential to improve out-of-domain robustness remains largely unexplored. We hypothesize that generative image editing can simulate a controlled domain shift in training data, effectively bridging the gap between source and target domains. To test this, we studied camouflaged military vehicle detection as a challenging domain shift scenario. Detectors trained on uncamouflaged data demonstrate substantial degradation on real test imagery containing foliage, netting, and multi-spectral camouflage across 15 vehicle classes in close-up, ground-level imagery. We used two diffusion-based editing models, Qwen Image Edit 2509 and Flux.2 Dev, to synthetically add camouflage to the training data, alongside a LoRA fine-tuned version of Qwen. A non-generative black-bar occlusion baseline served as a lower bound on augmentation quality. Using a GroundingDINO detector trained on real and synthetic data, generative camouflage augmentation yielded substantial mAP improvements for foliage (+20.1) and netting (+14.4) camouflage. Generating multi-spectral camouflage proved more challenging, but LoRA fine-tuning improved performance by 4.4 mAP over the uncamouflaged baseline.

</details>


### [65] [YesTrack: Referring Multi-Object Tracking via MLLM-based Yes/No Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02318)
*Quansheng Hu,Qin Sun,Qiansen Dai,Jin Ding,Wan Zhang,Xue Zhou,Jianxiao Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YesTrack 是一种新颖的两阶段指代多目标跟踪（RMOT）方法，将指代任务重构为判别式任务，直接利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）进行 Yes/No 验证，无需显式文本生成或外部决策模块，并引入两个轻量级时序一致性约束（TCP 与 TRP）来提升可靠性与效率；同时提出 YesTrack-MOT 验证其范式对通用 MOT 的泛化性，在 Refer-KITTI 与 Refer-KITTI-V2 上显著超越现有 SOTA 方法，即便使用最小版 Qwen3-VL 也保持高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RMOT 方法虽引入 MLLM 以增强泛化能力，但大多仅将其用作“描述生成器”，最终决策仍需外部模块完成，这不仅引入了额外延迟，也严重低估了 MLLM 固有的视觉-语言对齐能力。作者旨在打破这一范式，充分挖掘 MLLM 在指代任务中的判别式能力。

Method: 提出 YesTrack 两阶段 RMOT 方法：将指代任务重构为判别式任务，直接利用 MLLM 进行 Yes/No 验证而不做显式文本生成；为避免“验证哪些目标”的问题并提升可靠性与效率，引入两个轻量级时序一致性约束——时序置信度先验（TCP）和时序参考传播（TRP）；此外提出 YesTrack-MOT，作为该判别式范式在通用多目标跟踪（MOT）上的直接且高效的实例化。

Result: 在 Refer-KITTI 与 Refer-KITTI-V2 数据集上的实验表明，YesTrack 显著优于现有最先进方法，同时保持高推理效率，即便以 Qwen3-VL 的最小变体实现也能取得领先性能；代码已开源。

Conclusion: YesTrack 证明了“判别式验证”比“生成式描述+外部决策”更适合将 MLLM 用于指代跟踪任务，在性能与效率上实现双赢，且该范式可泛化至通用 MOT，为后续基于 MLLM 的跟踪方法提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking (RMOT) aims to track every instance in a video that matches a given language expression. Despite the recent integration of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to enhance generalization, existing methods predominantly relegate them to the role of caption generators, necessitating external modules for final decision-making. This paradigm not only introduces extra latency but also severely underutilizes the inherent vision-language alignment capabilities of MLLMs. To address these limitations, we propose YesTrack, a novel two-stage RMOT method that reformulates referring as a discriminative task, directly leveraging MLLMs for Yes/No verification without explicit text generation. To further enhance the reliability and efficiency of this MLLM-based verification, we introduce two lightweight temporal consistency constraints: Temporal Confidence Prior (TCP) and Temporal Reference Propagation (TRP). We further validate the generality of this discriminative paradigm by proposing YesTrack-MOT, a straightforward yet highly effective instantiation for generic multi-object tracking (MOT). Experiments on Refer-KITTI and Refer-KITTI-V2 show that YesTrack significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods while maintaining high efficiency, even when implemented with the smallest variant of Qwen3-VL. Code is released at https://github.com/ggbondrighthere24/YesTrack.

</details>


### [66] [ORB-SVM : An Innovative Hybrid Framework for Efficient Brain Tumor Detection from MRI Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02333)
*Amirhosein Azarpour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合框架，利用ORB算法进行特征提取并结合SVM分类器用于脑癌MRI图像的早期诊断。该方法实现了约99.5%的数据压缩，在Br35H数据集上达到97.5%的分类准确率，为高参数深度学习模型提供了一种资源高效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑癌早期诊断的准确性对患者生存率和治疗效果至关重要；MRI作为金标准影像手段，其判读受主观差异和图像噪声影响；现有深度学习方法参数规模大、计算成本高且需要大量训练数据，难以在资源受限场景下应用。

Method: 构建基于ORB（Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF）局部特征提取与SVM核分类器相结合的混合框架：ORB负责精确提取关键诊断特征并实现约99.5%的数据降维，去除无关背景信息；SVM利用核函数对稀疏特征进行鲁棒分类，平衡特征稀疏性与分类性能。

Result: 在Br35H数据集上的实验评估表明，该框架达到97.5%的分类准确率，同时实现显著的数据压缩（约99.5%），有效降低了非信息背景数据的影响并保留了肿瘤识别的关键诊断模式。

Conclusion: 局部特征表示与优化分类器的结合为医学图像分析提供了可靠且资源高效的选择：该方法在无大量计算开销的前提下保持高诊断性能，缓解了过参数化模型的局限，适用于临床诊断场景中的结构化解决方案。

Abstract: Brain cancer remains one of the most significant challenges in modern medicine, where the accuracy of early stage diagnosis is a decisive factor in patient survival and treatment efficacy. Although Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is the established gold standard for visualizing neurological structures, the interpretation of these high dimensional scans is often complicated by subjective variability among practitioners and the inherent noise present in complex medical images. While contemporary approaches frequently rely on high parameter deep learning architectures, such models often involve significant computational costs and require extensive data for effective training. This study introduces a hybrid framework that utilizes the Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF (ORB) algorithm for precise feature extraction and a Support Vector Machine (SVM) for classification [1], [2]. The proposed approach achieves a sub- stantial data reduction of approximately 99.5%, which effectively minimizes the influence of non informative background data while preserving critical diagnostic patterns essential for tumor identification. By balancing feature sparsity with a robust kernel based classifier, this methodology addresses the limitations of over parameterized systems while maintaining high diagnostic integrity. Experimental evaluations conducted on the Br35H dataset demonstrate that the framework attains a classification accuracy of 97.5%. The findings suggest that the integration of localized feature representation and optimized classification provides a reliable and resource efficient alternative for medical image analysis, offering a structured solution that maintains per- formance without the need for extensive computational overhead.

</details>


### [67] [Towards Zero-Shot Transfer Across Embodiments For Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02341)
*Caio Azevedo,Stefano Sabatini,Sascha Hornauer,Fabien Moutarde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶中VLA模型的多数据集训练问题，提出BEV-Forcing辅助目标函数，将专用鸟瞰图模型的地面物体布局信息注入VLA骨干网络，以提升跨数据集的零样本迁移能力；实验表明在小规模相机配置训练时该辅助任务能提升分布内与分布外性能，但随训练本体（数据多样性）增加其收益会减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中已通过跨本体统一表征扩展微调获得收益，但在自动驾驶中仍主要基于单一数据集训练，缺乏对未见数据集与相机rig的零样本迁移评估，且简单增加训练数据并不必然提升已知本体内的性能；需要研究多数据集训练及有效的跨数据集泛化方法。

Method: 提出BEV-Forcing辅助目标：将专用Bird's-Eye-View（BEV）模型输出的地面平面物体布局信息作为辅助监督信号注入VLA骨干网络，通过共享的BEV空间接口鼓励模型显式表征物体位置；并在多个数据集、多种相机rig配置下进行多数据集联合训练与零样本迁移评估，同时考察训练集规模（embodiment数量）对辅助任务收益的影响。

Result: 在少量相机rig训练时，BEV-Forcing能同时提升分布内（in-distribution）与分布外（out-of-distribution）性能；但随着训练embodiment（数据多样性）数量增加，BEV-Forcing带来的收益逐渐减小，表明当训练多样性扩大时，类似新技术的效益可能减弱。

Conclusion: 辅助任务（如BEV-Forcing）在数据规模有限时是有效的泛化手段，但其收益会随训练多样性扩展而递减；因此研究应纳入数据规模因素来评估新技术，避免在扩大训练多样性后高估或误判其实际价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have shown strong potential in autonomous driving by leveraging multimodal pretraining for instruction following, visual reasoning, and scene-level generalization. In robotic manipulation, scaling VLA fine-tuning across multiple robot setups--especially when unifying representations across embodiments--has been shown to improve in-dataset performance and cross-embodiment generalization; in autonomous driving, however, VLAs remain largely trained on individual datasets and are rarely evaluated for zero-shot transfer to unseen datasets and camera rigs; furthermore naively adding more datasets to the training data does not necessarily lead to better performance within seen embodiments. To address these problems, we study multi-dataset training for the driving task and BEV-Forcing, an auxiliary objective that transfers ground-plane object-layout information from a specialized Bird's-Eye-View model into the VLA backbone. By encouraging the model to represent object position through a shared BEV spatial interface, we show that an auxiliary task such as BEV-Forcing can improve both in-distribution and out-of-distribution performance when training on a small number of camera rigs. As the number of training embodiments increases, however, the benefits of the auxiliary task are reduced; we present this as evidence that new techniques in the literature may see their benefits diminish when simply scaling up training diversity, which motivates presenting results taking into account data scaling.

</details>


### [68] [Structured-Prior-Guided Diffusion Inpainting with Physical Consistency for Traffic Sign Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02348)
*Luo Li,Chongchong Huang,Jun Jia,Qiang Gao,Xinlong Liu,Gui Yang,Liang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对交通标志检测中的长尾数据分布问题，提出一种结构化先验引导的扩散修复（inpainting）框架，通过三条正交先验通路（JSON文本提示、IP-Adapter颜色渲染的前视矢量模板、ControlNet仿射对齐的矢量模板）注入语义、外观和几何先验，并加入CIELAB色度L1与Sobel梯度两项物理一致性损失，在AMAP自采数据上自监督训练后零样本迁移到TT100K-2021。基于约1.4B参数的Stable Diffusion 1.5，在重建保真度、物理一致性和语义可控性上全面超越七个基线，OCR精确匹配率达91.1%（对比12B的FLUX.1 Fill的44.2%），推理时间仅为其1/14；下游检测中稀有类分组池化AP50相对仅用真实数据提升1.23×–7.40×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志检测面临长尾数据分布，许多稀有标志在法规层面与常见标志同等重要，但样本极少。生成式数据增强是一种解决途径，然而通用图像修复模型直接作用于标志区域时会扭曲数字、变形几何与透视、偏移颜色。作者将问题根源归结为单一缺陷：条件信号对标志的物理构成而言过于抽象，因此需要将标志的物理组成（语义、外观、几何）显式注入生成过程。

Method: 提出结构化先验引导的扩散修复框架，通过三条正交通路注入三类先验：1) JSON格式文本提示注入语义先验；2) 用实测主色渲染的前视矢量模板经IP-Adapter注入外观（颜色）先验；3) 仿射对齐的矢量模板经ControlNet注入几何（透视/结构）先验。同时设计两项物理一致性损失：CIELAB色度空间L1项约束颜色，Sobel梯度项约束边缘结构。在AMAP自采大规模图像上以自监督重建方式训练，之后在异源公开数据集TT100K-2021上零样本评估。骨干为约1.4B参数的Stable Diffusion 1.5，并提供消融（leave-one-out）验证每个通路与损失项的独立贡献。

Result: 该方法在重建保真度、物理一致性和语义可控性的全部指标上胜过七个代表性竞品。OCR精确匹配率达到91.1%，远超12B工业模型FLUX.1 Fill [dev]的44.2%，且推理时间仅为其1/14。逐项消融证实三条先验通路与两项损失各有独立贡献。在下游检测任务中，合成数据使稀有类别的分组池化AP50相对仅用真实数据的基线提升1.23×至7.40×。代码与预训练模型已开源。

Conclusion: 通用修复模型失效的根源在于条件信号过于抽象，而将标志物理构成的语义、外观、几何先验通过正交通路显式注入扩散修复过程，可显著提升生成标志的物理一致性与语义可控性。所提出的框架在数据效率（1.4B参数 vs 12B）和推理速度上均大幅优于大型工业模型，并能有效缓解长尾稀有类的数据稀缺问题，增强下游检测性能。

Abstract: Traffic sign detection faces a long-tailed data distribution. Many rare signs matter as much as common ones from a regulatory standpoint, yet they have very few samples. Generative data augmentation is one way out. General-purpose inpainting models, however, distort digits, deform geometry and perspective, and shift colours when applied directly to sign regions. We trace this to a single gap: the conditioning signal is too abstract for the physical composition of a sign. We propose a structured-prior-guided diffusion inpainting framework with physical consistency. It injects the semantic, appearance and geometric priors of a sign through three orthogonal pathways: a JSON-formatted text prompt, a front-view vector template rendered with measured dominant colours (via IP-Adapter), and an affine-aligned vector template (via ControlNet). Two physical consistency losses constrain colour with a CIELAB chromaticity $L_1$ term and edge structure with a Sobel gradient term. We train by self-supervised reconstruction on a large set of images collected in-house at AMAP, then evaluate zero-shot on the public TT100K-2021 dataset, a different source. Our method uses a Stable Diffusion 1.5 backbone of about 1.4B parameters. It beats seven representative competitors on every metric of reconstruction fidelity, physical consistency and semantic controllability. Its OCR exact-match rate reaches 91.1\%, against 44.2\% for the 12B industrial model FLUX.1 Fill [dev], and it needs only $1/14$ of that model's inference time. Leave-one-out ablations confirm that each of the three prior pathways and both loss terms contribute on their own. In downstream detection, the synthetic data raises the group-pooled AP50 of rare classes by $1.23\times$ to $7.40\times$ over a real-data-only baseline. Code and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/52hz-whale/TrafficSignInpaint.

</details>


### [69] [GlyphAnchor: Enhancing Visual Text Rendering via Position-Anchored Glyph Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02349)
*Qiang Xiang,Shuang Sun,Binglei Li,Yibo Chen,Xu Tang,Yao Hu,Junping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlyphAnchor 是一种面向文本到图像和图像编辑扩散 transformer 模型的文本渲染增强方法，通过轻量级字形补丁条件（其位置由模型原生位置编码锚定到目标图像）提升文本保真度，并引入 InfoTextBench 基准来评估复杂文本渲染场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成与编辑模型在渲染长文本、复杂排列文本及罕见字符时仍不准确。已有方法要么依赖更强骨干网络和数据驱动训练但缺乏显式字形先验，要么引入字形先验但设计不够精确鲁棒，难以应对挑战性场景。

Method: GlyphAnchor 为扩散 transformer 骨干模型添加轻量级字形补丁条件，并利用模型自身的位置编码将这些补丁锚定到目标图像；通过分阶段监督微调训练该能力，再以文本感知的后训练步骤提升鲁棒性。同时构建 InfoTextBench 基准，用于评估生成与编辑设置下的文本丰富视觉渲染。

Result: 在多个骨干网络和基准（包括长文本、复杂密集排列文本及罕见字符场景）上的实验表明，GlyphAnchor 在保持整体图像质量的同时持续提升文本保真度。

Conclusion: GlyphAnchor 通过将字形先验以轻量、锚定位置的方式融入扩散 transformer，兼顾了训练效率与文本渲染准确性，为文本丰富的图像生成与编辑提供了稳健且通用的增强方案。

Abstract: Rendering accurate text remains difficult for image generation and editing models, especially when the target contains long, complex, and densely arranged text or rare characters. Existing approaches either improve native text rendering through stronger backbones and data-centric training without explicit glyph priors, or incorporate glyph priors through specialized designs that remain insufficiently accurate and robust under challenging scenarios. We introduce GlyphAnchor, a novel text-rendering enhancement method for both text-to-image and image-editing diffusion transformer models. GlyphAnchor enhances the backbone with lightweight glyph patch conditions whose positions are anchored to the target image through the model's native positional encoding. We train this capability with staged supervised finetuning and further refine it with text-aware post-training to improve robustness. We also introduce InfoTextBench, a benchmark for evaluating text-rich visual text rendering in both generation and editing settings. Experiments across multiple backbones and benchmarks, including long, complex, and densely arranged text and rare character scenarios, show that GlyphAnchor consistently improves text fidelity while preserving overall image quality.

</details>


### [70] [LookStep: Efficient Vision-Language Navigation with Linguistic Foresight and Event Driven Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02350)
*Kun-Yang Yu,Yingzhe Li,Hongyu Xu,Shi-Yu Tian,Zhi Zhou,Yang Chen,Ming Yang,Sheng Wang,Qing Yu,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookStep 是一个面向资源高效视觉-语言导航（VLN）的统一端到端框架，通过语言中心未来状态建模（用语言标签为每个候选动作生成粗粒度导航进度和未来状态）与事件驱动滚动记忆（自主决定是否将观测写入带语义角色的有界滚动记忆）相结合，摆脱了传统"仅监督专家动作"的下一动作预测范式和认知地图/历史帧/外部3D工具带来的高计算与内存开销；在 VLN-CE 任务上（R2R-CE Val-Unseen）以更少数据和更高内存效率取得 49.7% 的成功率，超越同训练设置下的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的 VLN 方法存在两大瓶颈：一是采用"下一步动作预测"范式，仅以专家动作作为监督信号，导致训练需要大量数据；二是依赖认知地图、累积历史帧或外部3D工具来维护状态，造成高昂的计算与内存开销。因此论文旨在实现资源高效的 VLN，在降低数据与算力需求的同时保持甚至提升导航性能。

Method: 提出 LookStep 统一端到端框架，包含两大核心组件：(1) Language Centric Future State Modeling——以语言标签生成每个候选动作对应的粗粒度导航进度与未来状态，从而提供更丰富的监督信号，减少对海量数据的依赖；(2) Event Driven Rolling Memory——自主判定哪些观测值得写入一个语义角色标注的有界滚动记忆，既保留关键历史信息又限制内存占用。整体采用端到端训练，无需外部3D工具。

Result: 在 VLN-CE 任务上，LookStep 在与现有方法相同的训练设置下取得更优性能：R2R-CE Val-Unseen 上成功率（SR）达到 49.7%，同时具备更好的内存效率和更低的数据使用量。代码与模型已开源（https://github.com/kunyang-YU/LookStep）。

Conclusion: 通过语言中心的未来状态建模替代仅专家动作的监督范式，并结合事件驱动的有界滚动记忆来维护状态，LookStep 验证了在不依赖认知地图、历史帧堆叠或外部3D工具的前提下，也能以更少数据和更低内存开销实现高性能 VLN，为资源受限场景下的具身导航提供了可行的统一端到端方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to follow natural-language instructions in unseen environments. Recent progress has been largely driven by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing methods follow a next-step action prediction paradigm, supervising only the expert action, which requires a high quantity of data for training. They also rely on cognitive maps, accumulated historical frames, or external 3D tools to maintain states, leading to high computational and memory overhead. To realize resource efficiency VLN, we propose LookStep, a unified end-to-end framework that combines Language Centric Future State Modeling and Event Driven Rolling Memory that uses language labels to generate coarse-grained navigation progress and future states for each candidate action, while autonomously deciding whether to write each observation into a bounded rolling memory with a semantic role. We validate LookStep empirically. On VLN-CE tasks, LookStep outperforms existing methods under the same training settings, achieving a 49.7\% success rate on R2R-CE Val-Unseen with better memory efficiency and less data usage. Code and model is available at https://github.com/kunyang-YU/LookStep.

</details>


### [71] [TempoGround: State-Aware Streaming Visual Grounding with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02359)
*Leqian Ding,Junning Qiu,Manwen Yang,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TempoGround 是一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的流式视觉定位框架，通过状态感知的跨帧对应关系显式建模物体进入、持续与离开等存在状态，并结合流式定位强化（SGR）与三阶段训练，在流式输入下同时提升2D与3D定位的精度、身份一致性与跨帧稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉定位方法在单帧和视频场景已取得显著进展，但在流式输入下仍面临身份漂移、跨帧不一致以及部分遮挡下的定位脆弱等问题。仅依靠 token 级监督无法刻画流式定位的几何目标，因此需要一种能够显式建模物体对应关系与存在状态的 VLM 原生框架。

Method: 提出 VLM 原生的 TempoGround 框架，核心是状态感知跨帧对应的课程预测机制：先解决2D实例关联，预测每个物体是新建、持续还是离开视野，解码2D框并提升为相机坐标系下的3D框；同时引入流式定位强化（SGR），以可验证的 Grounding、Identity、Consistency 三类奖励联合优化持久定位与时序一致的预测；采用精心设计的三阶段训练策略并在大规模数据上训练。

Result: 在多个因果流式输入基准上，TempoGround 使 F1_2D@0.5 和 F1_2D@0.95 平均提升 4.4 和 0.5，F1_3D@0.25 和 AP_3D 分别提升 6.2 和 7.5。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明 TempoGround 为流式输入下的视觉定位提供了实用且有效的基础，显著改善了定位精度、身份保持与跨帧一致性。

Abstract: Visual grounding maps language referents to spatial targets and is central to open-vocabulary perception with vision-language models. Existing methods have made substantial progress on single-frame and video-based visual grounding, yet under streaming inputs they still suffer from identity drift, cross-frame inconsistency, and fragile localization under partial occlusion. To address these issues, we present TempoGround, a VLM-native framework that detects cross-frame object correspondence and explicitly models object presence states, thereby enabling accurate and consistent visual grounding under streaming inputs. The key is a curriculum prediction mechanism guided by state-aware cross-frame correspondence: TempoGround resolves 2D instance association, predicts whether each object newly enters, continues in, or leaves the view, decodes the 2D box, and then lifts it to a camera-frame 3D box. As token-level supervision alone cannot capture the geometric objectives of streaming grounding, we further introduce Streaming Grounding Reinforcement (SGR), which optimizes TempoGround with verifiable Grounding, Identity, and Consistency rewards, jointly reinforcing persistent localization and temporally consistent predictions. We carefully design a three-stage training strategy and train TempoGround on large-scale data. We evaluate visual grounding under causally streaming inputs on multiple challenging benchmarks: TempoGround improves F1_2D@0.5 and F1_2D@0.95 by 4.4 and 0.5 on average, and F1_3D@0.25 and AP_3D by 6.2 and 7.5, respectively. These results demonstrate that TempoGround provides a practical foundation for visual grounding under streaming inputs.

</details>


### [72] [Information Density Imbalance in Visual Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02369)
*Ziwei Zhao,Yanxi Lu,Yuwei Hu,Shiyang Su,Mingxuan Wang,Chenyue Zhou,Jiayi Chen,Hehan Li,Xiaoshuai Hao,Andi Zhang,Yanbiao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息密度"这一新概念来度量类别偏差，发现在实例数量均衡的数据集中，类别信息密度与其检测精度存在显著负相关，并据此对三种先进目标检测损失函数进行简单改进，在Pascal VOC、COCO-LT和LVIS数据集上显著降低了模型偏差并提升了整体性能，为理解目标检测中的广义偏差现象提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究以实例数量判断长尾分布并假设实例少的类别性能差，但实例数量相对均衡的数据集中模型仍存在类别偏差，说明实例数量无法完全解释该现象。作者希望找到类别偏差的更深层来源，并提出新的度量工具。

Method: 首先引入并定义"信息密度"的概念及度量方法；通过实验观察类别信息密度与精度的显著负相关关系，并分析训练过程对这一关系的影响；基于信息密度对三种先进目标检测损失函数（如Focal Loss等）进行简单改进，在Pascal VOC、COCO-LT和LVIS数据集上验证其有效性。

Result: 实验表明，类别信息密度与检测精度呈显著负相关；将信息密度融入三种先进损失函数后，能显著降低模型偏差并有效提升整体检测性能，在Pascal VOC、COCO-LT和LVIS数据集上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 信息密度不平衡可能是类别偏差的潜在来源，为理解目标检测模型中的广义偏差现象提供了新视角，为设计更公平的损失函数与训练策略提供了新工具。

Abstract: In object detection, the number of instances is typically used to determine whether a dataset exhibits a long-tailed distribution, implicitly assuming that the model will perform poorly on categories with fewer instances. This assumption has led to extensive research on category bias in datasets with imbalanced instance numbers. However, even in datasets where instance numbers are relatively balanced, models still exhibit category bias, indicating that instance count alone cannot explain this phenomenon. In this work, we first introduce the concept and measurement of information density. We then observe a significant negative correlation between a category's information density and its accuracy, and we investigate how the training process impacts this relationship. Empirical studies suggest that information density imbalance may be a potential source of category bias. To preliminarily validate the potential of information density, we made simple improvements to three advanced object detection loss functions using this concept. Experiments on the Pascal VOC, COCO-LT, and LVIS datasets demonstrate that information density can significantly reduce model bias while effectively enhancing the overall performance of existing loss functions. This study provides a new perspective for understanding the generalized bias phenomenon in object detection models and offers new tools for designing fairer loss functions and training strategies.

</details>


### [73] [ProSR: Semantic-Prototype-Guided Discrete Modeling for Physically Consistent SAR Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02377)
*Byoungwoo Kim,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProSR（语义原型引导的超分辨率方法），将 SAR 图像超分辨率重构为量化潜空间中的语义引导离散令牌预测任务，通过自监督学习提取无标签语义先验，并借助原型图引导注意力保留 SAR 的脉冲散射特性，在 Umbra 公开数据集构建的 0.25m 分辨率基准上取得更优的视觉质量与散射保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 SAR 图像对自动目标识别等精细分析至关重要，但获取成本高昂；虽然生成式超分辨率模型是有前景的替代方案，但现有基于平滑近似的扩散框架难以保持 SAR 信号连贯的散射统计特性，容易产生与真实 SAR 物理规律不一致的随机结构畸变。

Method: ProSR 将 SAR 超分辨率建模为量化潜空间中的语义引导离散令牌预测任务：1) 将信号特征映射为离散散射基元，避免过度平滑从而保留 SAR 的脉冲性；2) 引入自监督学习骨干提取无标签语义先验，缓解标签稀缺问题；3) 提出语义对齐细节编码，将高频信号解耦为离散散射基元；4) 通过语义原型图生成器显式构建原型图，并利用原型图引导注意力在同一类别内路由信息流、抑制类间干扰。

Result: 基于 Umbra 公开数据集构建了大规模 0.25m 分辨率基准，实验表明 ProSR 在视觉质量上表现优异，同时保留了实际 SAR 应用所需的关键散射特征。

Conclusion: ProSR 验证了将 SAR 超分辨率视为离散语义引导令牌预测的可行性，能够兼顾视觉清晰度与物理散射特性的一致性，为低成本获取高质量 SAR 图像提供了有效方案，也展示了自监督语义先验在遥感图像复原中的价值。

Abstract: High-resolution Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery is critical for precision analysis such as automatic target recognition, yet its acquisition is costly. Although generative image super-resolution (ISR) models offer a promising alternative, current smooth-approximation based diffusion frameworks often struggle to preserve the coherent scattering statistics, causing stochastic structural distortions that are less consistent with real SAR physics. To address this, we propose Semantic Prototype-Guided Super-Resolution (ProSR), reformulating SAR ISR as a semantically-guided discrete token prediction task within a quantized latent space. By mapping signal features to discrete scattering primitives, ProSR preserves the impulsive nature of SAR without over-smoothing. Furthermore, we integrate a Self-Supervised Learning backbone into SAR ISR to extract label-free semantic priors, overcoming label scarcity. Guided by these priors, we introduce Semantic-Aligned Detail Encoding to decouple high-frequency signals into discrete scattering primitives. In parallel, the Semantic Prototype Map Generator explicitly constructs semantic prototype maps, allowing Prototype-Map-Guided Attention to route the information flows within identical categories and mitigate inter-class interference. To validate our approach, we present a large-scale 0.25m resolution benchmark from the Umbra Open Dataset. Experimental results show ProSR achieves superior visual quality while preserving essential scattering characteristics required for practical SAR applications.

</details>


### [74] [CA-OPD: Confidence-Aware On-Policy Distillation for Structured Visual Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02401)
*Menghao Li,Linjie Mu,Yin Wang,Haotian Hu,Yannian Gu,Lujiayi Xue,Fanyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出置信度感知的在线蒸馏框架（CA-OPD），用于自回归视觉语言模型：利用教师模型的精确置信度选择性纠正学生不可靠的转移（transition），并通过由严到松的调度逐步将轨迹控制权移交给学生；同时将知识蒸馏与干预决策对齐——被纠正位置使用教师预测的直接交叉熵监督，保留位置利用教师完整预测分布。在多教师设置下的GUI grounding与OCR任务中，显著提升Qwen3.5-0.8B在六个基准上的表现（ScreenSpot-Pro +9.50，OCRBench-v2英文 +6.72），消融实验证明增益来自干预位置、渐进式轨迹控制和干预对齐监督，而非单纯干预频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视觉语言模型统一了异构感知任务，但极易受累积误差影响。在线蒸馏（OPD）通过让学生在自己的轨迹（rollout）上训练来弥合训练与推理的不一致，然而学生早期不可靠的预测会误导轨迹并降低教师监督质量。现有的交错蒸馏方法虽允许教师验证并替换学生token，但主要依赖刚性排序指标而非教师精确置信度，且忽略了干预决策如何指导token级监督。

Method: 提出Confidence-Aware On-Policy Distillation (CA-OPD)：1) 利用教师模型的置信度选择性纠正学生不可靠的转移，通过严格到宽松（strict-to-relaxed）的调度逐步将轨迹控制权转移给学生，构建可靠轨迹；2) 将知识迁移与干预决策对齐——被纠正的位置接受来自教师预测的直接交叉熵监督，保留（未被干预）的位置则从教师完整预测分布中获益，实现自适应监督。

Result: 在多教师设置下评估GUI grounding与光学字符识别（OCR）任务，CA-OPD在全部六个目标基准上显著提升Qwen3.5-0.8B基线性能，包括ScreenSpot-Pro提升9.50分、OCRBench-v2英文提升6.72分。受控研究表明，性能增益依赖于干预位置、渐进式轨迹控制以及干预对齐的监督，而非仅仅干预频率。

Conclusion: CA-OPD通过教师置信度驱动的选择性干预与干预感知的监督设计，有效缓解了在线蒸馏中不可靠学生轨迹对教师监督的损害，同时揭示干预决策与token级监督对齐是提升蒸馏质量的关键因素。该框架验证了"在哪里干预"与"如何监督"的协同设计优于单纯增加干预次数。

Abstract: Autoregressive vision language models unify heterogeneous perception tasks but are highly susceptible to compounding errors. On-policy distillation (OPD) bridges the training-inference mismatch by training students on their own rollouts. However, unreliable student predictions, especially early in training, can derail the trajectory and degrade the quality of teacher supervision. While recent interleaved distillation methods allow the teacher to verify and replace student tokens, they primarily rely on rigid ranking metrics rather than exact teacher confidence, and they overlook how intervention decisions can inform token-level supervision. To address this, we introduce Confidence-Aware On-Policy Distillation (CA-OPD), a framework that couples reliable rollout construction with adaptive supervision. CA-OPD utilizes teacher confidence to selectively correct unreliable student transitions, gradually transferring rollout control to the student via a strict-to-relaxed schedule. Crucially, CA-OPD aligns knowledge transfer with these intervention decisions: corrected positions receive direct cross-entropy supervision from the teacher's prediction, while retained positions benefit from the teacher's full predictive distribution. Evaluated in a multi-teacher setting for GUI grounding and optical character recognition, CA-OPD substantially improves the Qwen3.5-0.8B baseline across all six target benchmarks, including gains of $9.50$ points on ScreenSpot-Pro and $6.72$ points on OCRBench-v2 English. Controlled studies further show that the gains depend on intervention placement, progressive rollout control, and intervention-aligned supervision, rather than intervention frequency alone.

</details>


### [75] [The Diagnosis a Reporter Leaves Unspoken: Surfacing Frozen Tumor Features for Brain-Tumor MRI Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02411)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Ruqiyya Adil,Adil Qayyum,Rida Hassan,Asad Mansoor Khan,Muhammad Usman Akram,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 NeuroFusion，一种面向脑部 MRI 报告的辅助生成系统。针对多链思维链（CoT）报告模型"诊断性沉默"问题——即将脑膜瘤和转移瘤误报为胶质瘤（诊断召回率仅 0.44/0.07）——作者发现病灶分割特征中其实潜藏可线性探针恢复的诊断信息（5 折交叉验证 macro-F1 0.82）。NeuroFusion 不覆盖该潜在信号，而是在每病灶特征上训练判别式字段分类头，以其承诺输出条件化一个快速单遍"草稿-审阅"解码器，将诊断召回率恢复至脑膜瘤 0.92、转移瘤 0.75，并在 9 项散文内容比较中赢得 8 项（RaTEScore、RadGraph-F1、GREEN，Holm 校正配对 BCa），延迟降低 5–6 倍（约 80 vs 457 秒/例）。受控阴性实验证明机制：若用学习到的诊断"引脚"覆盖解码器而非仅告知它，分布外转移瘤召回率骤降至 0.03。语法约束解码使 92.3% 记录符合模式（矛盾句 7.5% vs 基线 36.8%）。盲法九例试点中两位神经科医生在所有肿瘤类型上将 NeuroFusion 评为最高，且它是唯一零严重错误的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强大的脑 MRI 报告生成器在诊断层面可能"沉默"：基于医疗 Mistral-7B 骨干的多链思维链报告器在留出队列上把多数脑膜瘤和几乎所有转移瘤命名为"胶质瘤"，诊断召回率分别仅为 0.44 和 0.07。然而答案并非从模型缺失——监督线性探针可从冻结的分割特征中恢复三类肿瘤（macro-F1 0.82，机会水平约 0.33）。这揭示模型内部已编码诊断信息但未在文本中表达，作者旨在将这一潜在信号显式化，而不是简单覆盖或强制覆盖。

Method: NeuroFusion 采用"浮现而非覆写"潜伏信号的架构：1) 在每病灶分割特征之上训练判别式字段分类头（field-classifier heads），形成病灶级诊断判断；2) 以这些分类头的承诺输出（committed outputs）条件化一个快速、单遍的"草稿-再审阅"（draft-then-review）解码器，使诊断信息作为条件输入参与文本生成；3) 采用语法约束解码保证输出符合报告模式；4) 使用受控阴性实验（learned diagnosis pin 覆盖解码器而非仅告知）验证机制有效性。实验在相同的 Mistral 骨干上进行，保证了与基线骨干一致的可比性。

Result: NeuroFusion 恢复了诊断性能（脑膜瘤 0.92、转移瘤 0.75），在三个留出队列的 9 项散文内容比较中赢得 8 项（RaTEScore、RadGraph-F1、GREEN；Holm 校正配对 BCa），第九项无显著损失；延迟降低 5–6 倍（约 80 vs 457 秒/例）。受控阴性实验显示覆盖解码器的诊断引脚使分布外转移瘤召回率从 0.75 崩至 0.03，印证"告知而非覆写"的机制。语法约束解码使 92.3% 记录模式合法，矛盾句比例 7.5% vs 直接基线 36.8%。盲法九例试点中，两位专科认证神经科医生独立将 NeuroFusion 评为每类肿瘤最高分，是唯一零严重错误系统，并在 9 例中的 8 例给出最高签核评价（6 例独占、2 例并列）。

Conclusion: NeuroFusion 证明：大模型生成的报告中表面诊断错误并不等于模型缺乏相关信息，将潜伏于中间特征中的诊断信号以轻量判别头显式提取并条件化解码，能以更低延迟、更高模式合法性和更强的临床可信度恢复诊断能力。受控阴性实验进一步阐明了机制边界——仅"告知"解码器而非强制覆盖才能保持分布外鲁棒性。结果表明"特征探针引导的条件生成"是提升医学报告可靠性的有效范式，并为可审计、可核验（entailment-checkable）的辅助报告系统提供了依据。

Abstract: A capable brain-MRI report generator can still be, in effect, diagnostically silent. When a multi-chain chain-of-thought (CoT) reporter built on a medical Mistral-7B backbone is evaluated on held-out cohorts, it names most meningiomas and almost all metastases "glioma" (diagnosis recall 0.44/0.07). Yet the answer is not absent from the model: a supervised linear probe applied to its frozen segmentation features recovers the three tumour cohorts at 0.82 macro-F$_1$ (5-fold cross-validation; chance $\approx$0.33). We introduce NeuroFusion, an assistive reporter that surfaces this latent signal rather than overriding it: discriminative field-classifier heads over per-lesion features condition a fast, single-pass draft-then-review decoder on their committed outputs. Built on the identical Mistral backbone, this restores the diagnosis (meningioma 0.92, metastasis 0.75) and wins 8 of 9 prose-content comparisons across three held-out cohorts (RaTEScore, RadGraph-F$_1$, GREEN; Holm-corrected paired BCa), with no significant loss on the ninth, at 5-6x lower latency ($\approx$80 vs. 457 s/case). A controlled negative result sharpens the mechanism: a learned diagnosis pin that overrides the decoder instead of merely informing it collapses out-of-distribution metastasis recall to 0.03. Grammar-constrained decoding keeps 92.3% of records schema-valid, making every sentence entailment-checkable (7.5% contradicted vs. 36.8% for the direct baseline). In a blinded nine-case pilot, two board-certified neurologists independently rated NeuroFusion highest in every tumour type, the only system with zero critical errors, and gave it the top-rated sign-off in eight of nine cases (six outright, two ties).

</details>


### [76] [Uncertainty-Guided Adverse Weather Restoration via Gated Transformer Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02434)
*Zheke Jin,Yuning Cui,Tianle Jin,Alois Knoll,Hu Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对恶劣天气下图像退化空间异质性强、现有模型依赖全局聚合与朴素跨尺度融合等问题，本文提出不确定度引导的恶劣天气恢复网络 UAR-Net，集成门控双尺度Transformer块（GDTB）、渐进式均衡多尺度跳跃连接（BMSC）与不确定度感知细化头（URH），并以亮度感知能量损失（BAE-Loss）监督，在多个恶劣天气基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气造成的图像退化在空间上具有高度异质性（如雨、雾、雪在不同区域退化程度不同）。已有模型多采用天气无关的全局聚合、朴素的跨尺度融合和确定性目标函数，难以在all-in-one恶劣天气设定下同时处理多种异质退化，亟需一种能联合建模选择性全局交互与多尺度局部结构、并输出可靠不确定性估计的恢复框架。

Method: 提出UAR-Net：1）Gated Dual-scale Transformer Blocks（GDTB）联合建模选择性全局交互与多尺度局部结构；2）渐进式Balanced Multi-scale Skip Connection（BMSC）实现均衡的多尺度特征集成；3）Uncertainty-Aware Refinement Head（URH）完成伪影去除、细节增强并输出预测不确定度；4）以Brightness-Aware Energy Loss（BAE-Loss）监督，促进准确重建与校准良好的不确定度估计。

Result: 在多个恶劣天气图像恢复基准（如去雨、去雾、去雪及all-in-one恶劣天气任务）上进行大量实验，UAR-Net取得最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: UAR-Net通过门控Transformer、均衡多尺度跳跃连接与不确定度感知目标相结合，有效解决了恶劣天气恢复中异质退化难处理的问题，验证了天气特定（weather-specific）框架在all-in-one设定下的优越性；代码将在论文录用后开源。

Abstract: Restoring images degraded by adverse weather remains challenging due to spatially heterogeneous degradations. Many existing weather-specific restoration models rely on weather-agnostic global aggregation, naive cross-scale fusion, and deterministic objectives, which struggle to handle heterogeneous degradations in all-in-one adverse-weather settings. To address these limitations, we propose an Uncertainty-guided Adverse-weather Restoration Network (UAR-Net), a weather-specific AiO framework that integrates a gated transformer with balanced multi-scale skip connections. Specifically, we employ Gated Dual-scale Transformer Blocks (GDTB) to jointly model selective global interactions and multi-scale local structures, a progressive Balanced Multi-scale Skip Connection (BMSC) for balanced multi-scale feature integration, and an Uncertainty-Aware Refinement Head (URH) that performs artifact removal, detail enhancement, and predictive uncertainty estimation. The model is supervised by a Brightness-Aware Energy Loss (BAE-Loss) to encourage accurate reconstruction with well-calibrated uncertainty. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple adverse-weather benchmarks. The codes will open source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [77] [Adapting a Foundation Model for Lunar Surface Height Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02448)
*Patrick Bauer,Marius Schwinning,Melanie Siegel,Andreas Weinmann,Hichem Snoussi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度估计模型 Depth Anything V2（DAV2）的月球表面相对高度估计方法，利用公开的立体摄影测量（SPG）生成的数字高程模型（DEM）数据对 DAV2 进行微调，使其从零样本相对深度估计模型转变为可靠的月球表面相对高度估计器，显著提升了性能，可为未来 ESA 月球着陆任务中的危险地形定位提供额外的高度信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球表面 DEM 能提供精确的高度信息，对分析月面及保障未来着陆任务至关重要。传统 DEM 生成方法（如明暗恢复形状 SfS 和立体摄影测量 SPG）虽有效，但深度学习时代的单目深度估计成为新方向。现有研究仅将 DAV2 作为零样本基线直接用于月面 DEM 估计，未针对目标域自适应，因而可能性能不足。同时，经典危险检测仅依赖 2D 图像，缺少高度信息，因此需要开发相对月面高度估计器以辅助危险定位。

Method: 提出一种针对月球表面域的微调策略：以零样本相对深度估计模型 Depth Anything V2（DAV2）为基础，使用公开可获取的、由 SPG 生成的月球表面 DEM 数据进行微调，使模型适应月球地形域，从而输出可靠的相对月面高度估计。

Result: 实验结果表明，经过微调后的 DAV2 相比零样本模型性能有显著提升，有效地将 DAV2 转化为可靠的月球表面相对高度估计器。

Conclusion: 所提微调方法能够显著改善深度估计模型在月球表面的适配性，验证了利用域内 SPG-DEM 数据微调通用单目深度模型的有效性，为月球危险地形识别与着陆安全提供了有价值的高度信息来源。

Abstract: Digital elevation models (DEMs) can provide accurate height information, making it invaluable for analyzing the lunar surface. As the European Space Agency (ESA) prepares for future lunar missions that aim to land on the Moon, a precise method for height estimation will be essential for hazardous terrain that could endanger the landing approach. Traditional approaches to generate DEMs from imagery, such as shape from shading (SfS) and stereophotogrammetry (SPG) have been proven highly valuable for this task. However, due to advancements in machine learning, especially computer vision, the focus has shifted towards monocular depth estimation via deep learning. The lunar surface is covered by rocks and craters, and classic hazard detection methods rely solely on 2D image data. Our goal is to address this issue by developing a relative lunar surface height estimator that can provide additional information for hazard localization. In this letter, we present a methodology that builds on the well-known zero-shot relative depth estimation model Depth Anything V2 (DAV2). Other works have been using it as a state-of-the-art comparison for their proposed lunar DEM estimation method, but without adaptations to the target domain. Thus, it may underperform. Therefore, we propose a fine-tuning strategy with publicly available SPG-derived DEM data of the lunar surface. Our results demonstrate a significant improvement in performance compared to the zero-shot model, effectively transforming DAV2 into a reliable relative depth estimator of the lunar surface.

</details>


### [78] [WiFlow: Estimating Optical Flow using WiFi Channel State Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02452)
*Thomas Weigel,Simon Kiefhaber,Fabian Portner,Matthias Hollick,Simone Schaub-Meyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 WiFlow，一个基于 WiFi 信道状态信息（CSI）的光流估计框架，用于在无需摄像头的情况下估计场景中物体的运动。作者还构建了首个用于 CSI 光流估计的训练与评估数据集，并实验验证了关键设计要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景中物体运动的速度与位置估计在许多领域都很重要。传统方法依赖摄像头，但摄像头会引发隐私担忧，且画质受光照条件影响严重。因此作者探索用 WiFi CSI 替代摄像头帧进行光流估计，以规避隐私与光照问题。

Method: 提出 WiFlow，一种基于 CSI 的光流估计器；包含一个 CSI 预处理评估模块；并设计了三种模型架构，在精度与复杂度之间提供不同权衡。同时创建了首个用于训练和评估 CSI 光流估计器的数据集。

Result: 实验验证了 WiFlow 的有效性，并提供了关于该任务关键设计要素（如 CSI 预处理与模型架构选择）的见解；代码与数据已公开。

Conclusion: WiFi CSI 可作为一种可行的、保护隐私且不受光照影响的光流估计替代传感模态；本文贡献的框架、数据集与实验分析为该方向的研究奠定了基础，并为后续设计提供指导。

Abstract: Knowing where and how fast objects are moving within a scene is important across various domains. Usually, cameras are used to capture the data necessary for this task, but adding cameras often raises privacy concerns, and the quality of captured frames is heavily influenced by lighting conditions. In this work, we explore using WiFi channel state information (CSI) instead of camera frames for optical flow estimation. We propose WiFlow, a CSI based flow estimator, a preprocessor evaluation for CSI, and three model architectures that offer different trade-offs between accuracy and complexity. Further, we create the first dataset for training and evaluating CSI-based optical flow estimators, and our experiments provide insights into key design elements for this task. Code and data are available at https://visinf.github.io/wiflow.

</details>


### [79] [VIPS: Vehicle-Infrastructure Cooperative Planning Benchmark via Pseudo-Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02462)
*Hoonhee Cho,Jae-Young Kang,Giwon Lee,Hyemin Yang,Heejun Park,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIPS，一个基于伪仿真的车路协同（V2I）自动驾驶评测基准，通过整合车辆与基础设施观测实现可扩展且逼真的鲁棒性与误差传播评估；同时提出CoS-V2X，一个基于稀疏表示的协作规划框架，利用紧凑特征建模车路交互，实现异构观测下的高效通信与稳健决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境下的端到端自动驾驶面临部分可观测性与复杂多智能体交互问题，严重遮挡和路口密集车流限制了单车感知能力，因此需要V2I协作。但现有评测协议存在根本性权衡：开环评测无法捕捉误差累积与偏差恢复，而闭环评测成本高、难以扩展且依赖可能存在域差距的仿真环境。作者旨在弥合这一差距，提供可扩展且真实的评测方案。

Method: 方法由两部分组成：(1) VIPS基准——扩展伪仿真（pseudo-simulation）范式，整合车辆与基础设施观测，在不进行完整仿真的前提下实现可扩展且逼真的鲁棒性和误差传播评估；(2) CoS-V2X框架——基于稀疏表示的协作规划，用紧凑特征建模车-路交互，以实现高效的通信开销和异构观测下的稳健决策。

Result: 论文提供了VIPS基准与CoS-V2X规划框架，并公开了代码与数据集（https://vips2026.github.io），用于支持协作自动驾驶的可复现评测与验证。

Conclusion: VIPS以伪仿真方式弥合了开环与闭环评测之间的权衡，CoS-V2X则通过稀疏表示实现高效、鲁棒的V2I协作规划，二者共同为V2I条件下的协作自动驾驶提供更实用、可扩展的评测与决策方案。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving in urban environments requires robust decision-making under partial observability and complex multi-agent interactions. Severe occlusions and dense traffic at intersections limit the perception capability of single-agent systems, motivating recent efforts on Vehicle-to-Infrastructure (V2I) cooperation for perception and planning. However, existing evaluation protocols face a fundamental trade-off: open-loop evaluation fails to capture error accumulation and recovery from deviations, while closed-loop evaluation is costly, difficult to scale, and often relies on simulated environments that may suffer from domain gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose VIPS, a benchmark for cooperative autonomous driving in V2I settings based on pseudo-simulation. VIPS extends pseudo-simulation by integrating vehicle and infrastructure observations. This enables scalable yet realistic evaluation of robustness and error propagation without full simulation. We further present CoS-V2X, a cooperative planning framework based on sparse representations. CoS-V2X models vehicle-infrastructure interactions using compact features for efficient communication and robust decision-making under heterogeneous observations. Code and dataset are available at https://vips2026.github.io.

</details>


### [80] [Learning to Track from Privileged Target Appearances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02471)
*Xin Chen,Jiao Xu,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu,Kede Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PATT（Privileged Appearance Transfer for Tracking），一种教师-学生训练框架，用于解决跟踪器中目标模板在定位确定性与外观新鲜度之间的权衡瓶颈。教师模型可获取过去、当前及未来帧的精确目标裁剪（特权信息），学生模型仅依赖过去帧模板，通过学习预测教师的搜索表示来转移这些特权外观；转移过程按教师的相对定位优势和绝对定位精度加权。训练后所有特权组件被移除，推理时保持标准的学生-only流程。在七个基准、两种模型规模下均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟踪器使用的目标模板存在固有瓶颈：初始ground-truth模板定位精确但随时间变得陈旧，而近期模板虽能反映当前外观却来自不确定的预测裁剪结果。作者通过一个不可部署的oracle（提供精确的当前帧目标裁剪）量化了这一瓶颈：在LaSOT上AUC提升了15.2个百分点。这一巨大差距表明，帧级ground truth可在训练时提供精确的当前帧和未来帧目标裁剪（部署时不可用），从而构成一个仅训练阶段可利用的机会，驱动作者设计特权学习框架。

Method: PATT采用教师-学生训练框架：特权教师观察过去、当前和未来帧的精确目标裁剪，学生仅接收过去帧模板，并学习预测教师在搜索区域上的表示（多层级表示预测）。为避免转移不可靠的教师信号，PATT依据教师相对于学生的定位优势以及教师自身的绝对定位精度对转移损失进行加权。训练完成后，教师、潜在预测器、可靠性权重和特权裁剪全部移除，仅保留标准的学生-only推理流程。

Result: Orcale实验显示，精确当前帧裁剪可使LaSOT上的AUC提升15.2个百分点，证实模板瓶颈的存在。PATT在七个基准（涵盖长时与短时跟踪协议）和两种模型规模下均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: PATT验证了利用训练期特权外观信息（当前/未来帧的精确裁剪）可以显著缓解模板陈旧与定位不确定性之间的权衡，且不增加任何部署时的推理开销或输入。通过教师-学生表示预测与可靠性加权，特权知识得以有效且可靠地转移，为视觉跟踪提供了一种通用的训练范式。

Abstract: Target templates define what a visual tracker searches for, yet the templates available at inference trade off localization certainty with appearance freshness: the initial ground-truth template is exact but becomes stale, whereas recent templates better reflect the current appearance but are cropped from uncertain predictions. We quantify this bottleneck with a non-deployable oracle that supplies an exact current-frame target crop, improving AUC on LaSOT by 15.2 percentage points. This gap reveals a training-only opportunity: frame-level ground truths provide exact current- and future-frame target crops, although such crops are unavailable at deployment. We introduce Privileged Appearance Transfer for Tracking (PATT), a teacher-student training framework that transfers these privileged appearances to a deployable tracker through multi-level representation prediction. The privileged teacher observes exact target crops from past, current, and future frames, whereas the student receives only past-frame templates and learns to predict the teacher's search representations. To avoid transferring unreliable teacher signals, PATT weights this transfer by the teacher's relative localization advantage over the student and its absolute localization accuracy. After training, the teacher, latent predictor, reliability weights, and privileged crops are removed, leaving standard student-only inference. Across seven benchmarks at two model scales, PATT achieves consistent gains under both long- and short-term tracking protocols.

</details>


### [81] [UnCapsTSR: An Unsupervised Transformer-based Image Super-Resolution Approach for Capsule Endoscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02476)
*Anjali Sarvaiya,Shubh Kawa,Lalit Agrawal,Jagrit Joshi,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UnCapsTSR，一种基于Transformer的无监督GAN框架，用于提升无线胶囊内窥镜(WCE)低分辨率图像的空间分辨率。无需显式退化估计或真实LR-HR配对，通过BTV损失保证空间连续性，并在Kvasir Capsule数据集上构建新训练集，引入新无参考指标EndoQM，在KID和GIANA数据集上验证了泛化性，多项指标优于现有无监督SR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WCE相比传统内窥镜虽有其优势，但受限于胶囊尺寸和无线传输，图像分辨率较粗，影响GI道异常检查效果。现有超分辨率方法通常需要退化估计或LR-HR配对，而真实世界WCE数据难以满足这些条件，因此需要一种无需显式退化估计和配对数据的无监督SR方案。

Method: UnCapsTSR采用基于Transformer的生成对抗网络(GAN)框架实现无监督超分辨率；使用Bilateral Total Variation (BTV)损失确保SR图像空间连续性；从Kvasir Capsule数据集构建新的训练数据集；引入新的无参考图像质量指标EndoQM用于WCE领域定量评估。

Result: 在未参与训练的KID和GIANA数据集上验证了泛化性；与SOTA无监督SR方法相比，在NIQE、BRISQUE、PIQE和EndoQM指标上均有一致提升；统计评估显示在各数据集上EndoQM从LR到SR改善了40%至80%。

Conclusion: UnCapsTSR有效解决了WCE低分辨率图像的超分辨率问题，无需真实配对数据和退化估计即可工作，并提供了领域专用的评估指标和训练数据集，为WCE图像质量增强提供了可行且可泛化的方案。

Abstract: Wireless Capsule Endoscopy (WCE) captures and streams video while passing through a patient's Gastrointestinal (GI) tract and is used to examine its irregularities. Although advantageous over conventional endoscopy, WCE suffers from limitations related to capsule size and wireless transmission, resulting in images with coarser resolution. This work presents UnCapsTSR, an unsupervised transformer-based Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) framework for improving the spatial resolution of Low-Resolution (LR) WCE images. The proposed method accomplishes SR without explicit degradation estimation of real-world LR data and eliminates the need for true LR-HR pairs. UnCapsTSR employs a Bilateral Total Variation (BTV) loss to ensure spatial continuity in SR images. A newly curated dataset from the Kvasir Capsule dataset is also presented for training WCE SR models. Generalizability is validated on KID and GIANA datasets that are not used during training. A new non-reference metric, Endoscopy Quality Metric (EndoQM), is introduced for quantitative evaluation of domain-specific WCE data. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvement over state-of-the-art unsupervised SR approaches using NIQE, BRISQUE, PIQE, and EndoQM. Statistical evaluation shows 40 to 80 percent improvement in EndoQM from LR to SR across the evaluated datasets.

</details>


### [82] [Blending Concepts: Benchmarking Visual Metaphor Generation in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02502)
*Chuer Chen,Zichen Wang,Yi He,Zhengxi Yu,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VMetaphor-Bench，这是首个用于评估文生图（T2I）模型视觉隐喻生成能力的基准。该基准包含从真实创意图像中筛选的 1,500 个视觉隐喻样本，按三个层级和十个类别组织，每个样本配有两条不同明确度的提示词。评测方面，作者在 MLLM-as-judge 范式下构建了混合评估框架：一是基于多项选择题（MCQ）的协议，包含 9,594 个问题、覆盖四个隐喻保真度层级；二是基于维度打分（三个感知维度）的协议。对 11 个代表性 T2I 模型的广泛评测表明，即使最强的专有模型在组成性结构（compositional structuring）和跨域映射等隐喻表达关键能力上仍存在明显不足，凸显视觉隐喻生成是未来 T2I 研究的重要前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型在忠实渲染指定物体和属性方面已取得显著成功，但生成视觉隐喻（通过组合两个不同领域元素来传达抽象概念的图像）的能力尚未得到系统研究。作者旨在填补这一评估空白，为 T2I 模型的隐喻表达能力建立首个标准化评测基准。

Method: （1）构建 VMetaphor-Bench：从真实创意图像中精选 1,500 个视觉隐喻样本，按三个层级与十个类别组织，每个样本配两条不同明确度的提示词；（2）设计混合评估框架（MLLM-as-judge 范式）：一方面采用基于多项选择题（MCQ）的协议，共 9,594 道题、覆盖四个隐喻保真度层级；另一方面采用基于三个感知维度的打分协议，实现多角度、细粒度的自动评测。

Result: 对 11 个代表性 T2I 模型的广泛评测显示，即便是最强的专有模型也在组成性结构和跨域映射这两个隐喻表达的关键方面表现挣扎，视觉隐喻生成整体上仍是现有模型的薄弱环节。

Conclusion: 当前 T2I 模型的视觉隐喻生成能力远未成熟，尤其在组成性结构搭建与跨域语义映射上存在显著短板；视觉隐喻生成应被视为未来 T2I 研究的重要前沿方向，本基准可作为推动该方向进展的评测基础。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have achieved remarkable success at faithfully rendering specified objects and attributes, yet their ability to produce visual metaphors, images that convey abstract ideas by combining elements from two distinct domains, remains largely unexamined. To bridge this gap, we introduce VMetaphor-Bench, the first benchmark for evaluating visual metaphor generation in T2I models. It comprises 1,500 visual metaphors curated from real-world creative imagery, organized into three levels and ten categories, with each sample paired with two prompts of differing specificity. For evaluation, we develop a hybrid framework within an MLLM-as-judge paradigm, combining a multiple-choice question (MCQ) based protocol of 9,594 questions across four levels of metaphorical fidelity with a dimension-based scoring protocol along three perceptual dimensions. Extensive evaluation of 11 representative T2I models reveals that even the strongest proprietary models struggle with compositional structuring and cross-domain mapping, key aspects of metaphorical expression, highlighting visual metaphor generation as an important frontier for future T2I research.

</details>


### [83] [SR-Edit: Region-Aware Image Editing via Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02504)
*Andong Wang,Zehua Chen,Yuxuan Jiang,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SR-Edit 是一种基于迭代自精炼的图像编辑框架，通过从模型自身预测中逐步提取精确且自洽的编辑/非编辑区域分离，并以与原始采样动态一致的方式进行校正，从而在精确编辑目标区域的同时严格保持非编辑区域，解决了现有方法因区域估计不准和启发式校正带来的伪影问题，在保持性和整体图像质量上优于现有编辑技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的快速发展使图像编辑取得显著进展，但实现"只修改目标区域、严格保留其余区域"的忠实编辑仍然困难。由于外部提供的区域标注在实践中往往难以获得，现有方法尝试自动推断编辑与非编辑区域并对其施加一致性约束，但它们仍受限于区域估计不准确以及启发式校正策略对原生推理过程的扭曲——本意为提升保真度的方法反而引入了新的伪影来源。

Method: 提出 SR-Edit 框架，采用迭代自精炼机制。每一轮迭代包含两个步骤：(1) 通过轻量级后处理，从模型自身的预测中提取逐渐精确且自洽的编辑/非编辑区域分离；(2) 对非编辑区域施加保持性校正更新，且该校正与原始采样动力学保持一致，从而避免干扰原生推理过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，SR-Edit 在区域保持性和整体图像质量上均优于现有的图像编辑技术。

Conclusion: SR-Edit 通过让区域估计与校正过程都源自模型自身预测并保持与原始采样过程对齐的自精炼迭代，有效克服了区域估计不准确与校正策略失真这两大问题，证明了在免外部标注条件下实现高保真、高质量图像编辑的可行性。

Abstract: With the recent rapid progress in generative models, image editing has made remarkable advances, yet achieving faithful edits that precisely modify only the target regions while strictly preserving all other regions remains challenging. Since externally provided region annotations are often difficult to obtain in practice, a growing body of work seeks to improve preservation by automatically inferring edit and non-edit regions, and then enforcing consistency on the latter. However, these approaches still suffer from inaccurate region estimation and heuristic correction strategies that distort the native inference process, making methods designed for fidelity themselves a new source of artifacts. We propose SR-Edit, an image editing framework that overcomes these issues via iterative self-refinement. Specifically, at each iteration, SR-Edit first (i) extracts progressively precise and self-consistent region separation from the model's own predictions by lightweight post-processing, and then (ii) enforces preservation in non-edit areas through correction updates that remain aligned with the original sampling dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SR-Edit achieves superior preservation and overall image quality compared to existing editing techniques.

</details>


### [84] [Orthogonal Ensembles and Tested Explanations for Performer-Independent Body-Motion Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02510)
*Naoto Nishida,Yoshio Ishiguro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于骨架运动的仅身体、12类表演情绪分类任务，在留表演者（LPO）评估这一困难且欠定设置下（随机基线8.3%），证明可靠的性能提升并非来自新架构，而是来自组合11个具有正交错误模式的模型：等权logit均值集成在10折LPO交叉验证下达到36.80±4.00%的Macro-F1，相比同协议复现的STGCN++基线提高11.07个百分点（相对+43%）。核心贡献是一套经过检验的解释方案：通过部位掩蔽与反事实编辑证明（而非断言）模型决策依赖基于运动的身体区域证据，且该区域显著性比经典运动学更贴合基于规则的Laban动作分析（LMA）属性（区域级显著性-LMA Spearman rho=+0.500对比+0.033，约15倍），这一对齐对提交的11路集成本身同样成立（rho=+0.517）；该审计是事后性的、无需重新训练。该套件还如实报告了一个负面结果：窗口内时间显著性呈弥散而非局部化分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨架动作情绪分类研究多在有利的评估协议（如同一表演者出现在训练与测试集）下报告结果，容易高估泛化能力。在更真实、更困难的留表演者（LPO）评估下，随机基线仅8.3% Macro-F1，复现的强基线STGCN++也只有约25.7%，问题高度欠定。作者旨在找出在此困难设置下真正可靠的性能提升途径，并回答"模型到底依据什么证据做决策"这一可解释性问题——不仅要断言，还要通过测试来证明。

Method: 方法由两部分构成：(1) 性能提升：不做新架构设计，而是集成11个具有正交错误模式的模型，采用等权logit均值融合，并在10折LPO交叉验证下评估（与基线同协议、同划分）。(2) 可解释性审计：提出一套事后解释套件，对强集成成员及最终11路集成进行部位掩蔽（part-masking）和反事实编辑（counterfactual edits），生成身体区域级显著性图；随后将该显著性图与基于规则的动作分析（Laban Movement Analysis, LMA）属性及经典运动学特征（如关节速度等）分别做Spearman相关性比较；同时检查窗口内时间显著性分布。整个过程无需重新训练。

Result: 在10折LPO交叉验证下，等权logit均值集成的每折Macro-F1为36.80±4.00%，比同划分复现的STGCN++基线（25.73±4.03%）提高11.07个百分点（相对提升43%）。可解释性方面：区域级saliency与LMA属性的Spearman rho=+0.500，而与经典运动学的rho仅+0.033（约15倍差距），且该对齐在最终11路集成上依然成立（rho=+0.517）。负面结果同样被如实报告：窗口内时间saliency呈弥散分布而非局部化，说明模型并未在时间维度上形成清晰的"关键时刻"定位。

Conclusion: 在LPO这一困难、欠定的情绪分类设定下，可靠收益来自"多个正交错误模型集成"而非单一新架构；并通过可测试的解释套件证明模型决策确实依赖于与LMA一致的运动化身体区域证据，而非经典运动学信号；同时该审计方法具备通用性（事后、免训练、对集成也适用），并能诚实报告负面发现（时间saliency弥散）。整体结论支持"集成+严格可解释性审计"作为此类任务中稳健研究与验证的范式。

Abstract: We study body-only, 12-class acted-emotion classification from skeleton motion under leave-performer-out (LPO) evaluation, a hard, underdetermined setting: chance is 8.3%, and a protocol-matched reproduced STGCN++ baseline reaches only 25.73 +/- 4.03% Macro-F1. We show that reliable gains come not from a new architecture but from combining eleven models with orthogonal error modes: under 10-fold LPO cross-validation on the labeled training performers, an equal-weight logit-mean ensemble reaches 36.80 +/- 4.00% per-fold Macro-F1, a protocol-matched +11.07 pp (+43% relative) over the same-split reproduced baseline. Our central contribution is a tested explanation suite: for a strong ensemble member, part-masking and counterfactual edits show (rather than assert) that its decisions depend on motion-grounded body-region evidence, and this region saliency aligns with rule-based Laban Movement Analysis (LMA) attributes far more than with classical kinematics: region-level saliency-LMA Spearman rho = +0.500 versus +0.033, roughly 15x, and the alignment holds for the submitted 11-way ensemble itself at rho = +0.517; the audit is post hoc and needs no retraining. The same suite faithfully reports a negative: within-window temporal saliency is diffuse rather than localized.

</details>


### [85] [Beauty is in the AI of the beholder: MLLMs systematically overrate facial attractiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02512)
*Santiago Grandas,Juan Sebastian Cely-Acosta,Mohit Mendiratta,Shafee Hassan,Macken Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一项预注册探索性研究比较了2,513名人类参与者与四种商用多模态大语言模型（Claude、Gemini、GPT、Grok）对人脸吸引力的评分，发现MLLMs系统性高估吸引力且评分范围更窄，无法在绝对值上复现人类评分，但能较好地追踪人类评分的排序；AI模型之间高度一致（Grok除外），且仅人脸年龄是人与模型共同的有效预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户、企业和审美从业者日益依赖MLLMs进行外貌评估，需要检验这些AI模型能否准确反映人类对吸引力的判断，以判断其作为审美评估工具的可靠性。

Method: 采用预注册的探索性研究设计，收集2,513名人类参与者的吸引力评分，并将其与四个广泛使用的商用商业AI模型（Claude、Gemini、GPT、Grok）的评分进行对比，分析绝对评分差异、排序相关性、评分范围以及年龄、种族、性别等线索的预测作用。

Result: MLLMs系统性给出比人类更有利且范围更窄的评分，不能在绝对值上复现人类评分，但与人类吸引力判断存在强相关，能准确追踪人脸排序；模型之间高度一致，Grok例外且与人类一致性最低；仅人脸年龄对人与模型均有预测作用，种族和性别在不同模型间模式不一致。

Conclusion: 当前的商用MLLMs虽能近似人类吸引力的相对排序，但会系统性高估人脸美感，因此在需要绝对评分或精确反映人类审美判断的场景中不宜直接使用。

Abstract: Beauty assessments from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly popular amongst users, companies, and aestheticians. This raises the question of whether these AI models can accurately reflect human judgments of attractiveness. In a pre- registered exploratory study, we compared the attractiveness ratings of 2,513 human participants to four widely used commercial AI models: Claude, Gemini, GPT, and Grok. Results showed that MLLMs systematically rate faces more favourably and within a narrower range than humans and, at the time of study, do not reproduce human ratings in absolute terms. However, MLLMs exhibit strong correlations with human attractiveness judgments, accurately tracking the rank-ordering of faces. MLLMs may judge faces by different cues than humans; only face age was a predictor of facial attractiveness in both humans and MLLMs, with inconsistent patterns across models for ethnicity and gender. AI models strongly agree with one another, except for Grok, which also showed the lowest agreement with humans. Our findings suggest that while they may be able to approximate rank-orderings of human attractiveness, current off-the-shelf commercial MLLMs systematically overrate the beauty of human faces.

</details>


### [86] [Doppio: A Dataset for Contactless Weight Estimation of Falling Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02528)
*Simon Kiefhaber,Jan-Martin O. Steitz,Julia Grabinski,Christoph Reich,Paul Wagner,Max Zimmermann,Simone Schaub-Meyer,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用计算机视觉替代昂贵、复杂且特定于应用的接触式/非接触称重方案，用于测量下落颗粒（以研磨咖啡为例）的累计质量。作者构建了全新的视频数据集 Doppio（研磨咖啡下落视频 + 逐帧精确重量标注），并系统评估了从纯空间前馈网络到循环时空模型的多类深度学习架构，证明基于视觉的模型能够高精度估计下落颗粒的累计重量，为未来无接触称重系统奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中常需测量粉末（包括下落颗粒）的质量，静态称重秤有效但无法满足无接触测量需求；现有无接触方案成本高、依赖特定场景且技术复杂，缺乏通用且易部署的替代手段。

Method: 以磨咖啡为可获取的真实场景案例，构建 Doppio 视频数据集（下落咖啡颗粒视频 + 逐帧精确真值重量）；对比评估从纯空间前馈网络到循环时空模型的多种深度学习方案，并从预测精度与计算开销两方面分析其权衡。

Result: 实验表明，基于深度学习的计算机视觉模型能够准确估计下落颗粒的累计重量，验证了视觉方案在无接触质量测量上的可行性，并给出了不同模型在精度与计算成本间的折中关系。

Conclusion: 深度学习视觉模型为无接触质量测量提供了实用且可扩展的替代方案；Doppio 数据集与基线评估为该方向建立了坚实基础，可推广至更广泛的粉体与颗粒测量应用。

Abstract: Measuring the mass of powder, including falling particles, is a common task in industrial applications. While scales are effective for static measurements, many applications require contactless sensing, where existing solutions are often costly, application-specific, and technically complex. In this work, we investigate computer vision as a practical alternative for contactless mass estimation. As an accessible real-world case study, we focus on coffee grinding and introduce \emph{Doppio}, a novel video dataset capturing videos of falling ground coffee, paired with precise, per-frame ground-truth weight measurements. To demonstrate contactless measuring, we evaluate deep learning-based approaches ranging from purely spatial feed-forward networks to recurrent spatio-temporal models. These models are analyzed with respect to their predictive accuracy and computational trade-offs. We demonstrate that deep learning-based computer vision models accurately estimate the cumulative weight of falling particles, establishing a solid foundation for future vision-based contactless measurement solutions.

</details>


### [87] [ShallowStream: Index Shallow then Answer Deep for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02780)
*Jitai Hao,Ke Yang,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShallowStream 提出利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的浅层网络同时完成视频帧编码和检索索引构建，在流式视频理解中以极低的开销实现与最强现有方法相当的精度，将每帧预填充延迟和10秒端到端延迟分别降低最高52.1倍和11.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解在具身智能、自动驾驶、工业监控、安防预警和可穿戴助手等真实场景中至关重要，但用MLLM持续处理视频流计算开销巨大。现有工作多关注视觉token剪枝、token合并、量化、按需取帧和上下文卸载等维度，却忽略了模型深度这一维度：对每帧执行全深度MLLM预填充代价极高，且KV缓存随预填充深度线性增长，导致计算开销和内存压力难以承受。

Method: ShallowStream 框架包含两个核心设计：(1) 流处理阶段，仅利用MLLM的浅层网络持续处理视频帧，同时完成帧编码与检索索引构建，维护一个始终在线的轻量级索引（基于浅层KV缓存）；(2) 查询回答阶段，利用浅层网络产生的注意力分数对上下文帧进行打分，并采用多样性感知的选择策略，检索出既精确又全面的证据帧，供深层模型回答使用。

Result: ShallowStream 在精度上与现有最强的流式视频理解方法持平，同时将每帧预填充延迟最多降低52.1倍，10秒级视频的端到端延迟最多降低11.9倍，显著削减了计算开销与KV缓存增长。

Conclusion: 通过将帧编码与检索索引构建下放到浅层网络，ShallowStream 验证了"模型深度"可作为流式视频理解中一项高效且有效的优化维度，在保持性能的同时大幅降低延迟与存储成本，为流式MLLM部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding is a critical capability for real-world applications, including embodied intelligence, autonomous driving, industrial monitoring, surveillance and early warning, and wearable assistants. However, processing continuous video streams with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is computationally expensive. Existing efforts have explored reducing streaming overhead through visual token pruning, token merging, quantization, on-demand frame retrieval, and context offloading. However, most existing methods overlook the dimension of model depth. Repeatedly executing full-depth MLLM prefill over incoming frames is prohibitively expensive, incurring substantial computational overhead and causing the KV cache to grow at a rate directly proportional to the prefill depth. To address these challenges, we propose ShallowStream, a novel framework that leverages the shallow layers of an MLLM to simultaneously perform frame encoding and retrieval index building. During stream processing, ShallowStream maintains an always-on lightweight index using the KV cache of shallow layers. During query-time answering, we leverage the attention scores generated by the shallow layers to score context frames and employ a diversity-aware selection strategy to retrieve precise and comprehensive evidence. ShallowStream achieves performance on par with the strongest existing streaming methods, while reducing per-frame prefill latency and 10-second end-to-end latency by up to 52.1x and 11.9x, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/CURRENTF/ShallowStream.

</details>


### [88] [Fine-Grained Anomaly Perception in Wild UGC-Enhanced Images: A Comprehensive Dataset and Difference-Fusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02529)
*Yan Zhong,Gefei Chen,Qiufang Ma,Zhen Wang,Zhiwei Fan,Lei Shi,Tingting Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对短视频/社交平台中UGC图像增强后产生的局部视觉异常（人脸、文字、纹理）难以被传统IQA方法检测的问题，正式定义了新任务UEAP（UGC图像增强异常感知），构建了首个基准数据集UEAP-4k（来自真实业务场景，含异常类别、定位与严重程度的细粒度标注），并提出了DFAP-UGC方法（基于问题-参考差异融合、密集空间查询、区域验证与质量感知排序）及LADTP训练策略（局部感知动态任务优先化，实现端到端学习）。实验证明该方法优于经典方法改编的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像增强与修复已成为短视频和社交平台上提升UGC视觉体验的标准后端操作，但这些处理不可避免地引入视觉异常（尤其在面部、文字和纹理区域），直接损害感知保真度和观众信任。现有IQA方法虽在经典失真上表现良好，但只做整体质量评估，无法捕捉增强算法在真实UGC中造成的特定局部异常，因此需要专门的任务、数据集和方法来弥合这一空白。

Method: (1) 形式化定义新任务UEAP，并构建首个基准数据集UEAP-4k，从真实业务场景中采集，提供异常类别、定位和严重程度的细粒度标注；(2) 提出DFAP-UGC方法，采用显式问题-参考差异融合，结合密集空间查询、区域验证和质量感知排序，实现对野生UGC增强图像的稳健异常识别；(3) 提出LADTP（Locality-Aware Dynamic Task Prioritization）训练策略，处理该任务中子任务的内在耦合，实现有效的端到端学习并消除多阶段训练开销。

Result: 大量实验表明，DFAP-UGC在UEAP任务上优于从经典方法改编而来的基线方法，验证了UEAP-4k数据集的价值以及DFAP-UGC在稳健UGC增强图像异常感知方面的优越性。代码和数据将公开。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了UGC增强图像局部异常感知的研究空白，提供了首个该任务的基准数据集与专用方法，验证了差异融合与动态任务优先化策略的有效性，为UGC平台图像质量保障提供了新的评估维度与实用工具。

Abstract: Image enhancement and restoration have become standard back-end operations on short-video and social media platforms to boost UGC visual experience. Yet these processes inevitably introduce visual anomalies--especially in faces, texts, and textures--that directly undermine perceptual fidelity and viewer trust. While existing IQA methods perform well on classic distortions, they target holistic quality assessment and fail to capture the specific, localized anomalies caused by enhancement algorithms in real-world UGC. To bridge this gap, we formally define a new task-quality Anomaly Perception for UGC image Enhancement (UEAP), and contribute the first UEAP benchmark dataset, named UEAP-4k, curated from the real business scenarios. It provides fine-grained annotations for anomaly categories, localization and severity levels. Furthermore, we propose a Difference-Fusion Anomaly Perception Method (DFAP-UGC) for wild UGC-enhanced images, which leverages explicit problem-reference difference fusion with dense spatial querying, regional verification, and quality-aware ranking, enabling robust anomaly identification in challenging scenarios. To handle the inherent coupling of subtasks in this new task, we propose a Locality-Aware Dynamic Task Prioritization (LADTP) training strategy that enables effective end-to-end learning and eliminates multi-stage overhead. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms baselines adapted from classical approaches for this task, validating the value of this dataset and the superior of DFAP-UGC for robust UGC-enhanced image anomaly perception. Code and data will be public.

</details>


### [89] [Spatially Aware World Action Model via Geometric Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02531)
*Javier Alejandro Lopetegui Gonzalez,Paul Pacaud,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-WAM（空间感知世界行动模型），将预训练视频扩散模型改造为同时预测动作、RGB和深度信息的统一扩散骨干，实现3D感知的世界建模与动作预测，并在RoboCasa、LIBERO-Plus基准及UR5真实机器人上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界行动模型（WAM）通过大规模预训练视频扩散模型联合预测未来观测与动作，能从互联网级视频中继承丰富的视觉与物理先验，是机器人策略学习的重要范式；然而现有模型仅使用RGB观测，未利用3D信息，缺乏空间感知能力。作者旨在将3D几何信息引入WAM，同时不破坏预训练得到的先验。

Method: SA-WAM将预训练视频模型改造为联合动作、RGB和深度预测的统一扩散骨干。其核心设计是采用非线性编码，将无界深度信号映射到冻结VAE分词器（tokenizer）所期望的有界输入域内，从而无需针对3D进行微调即可复用分词器，在引入几何信息的同时保留预训练先验。

Result: SA-WAM在RoboCasa和LIBERO-Plus基准上取得最先进结果，同时提升了未来状态预测质量；在UR5机械臂的真实世界评估中超越强基线，尤其在随机化环境中提升显著。此外，作者分析了世界模型预测质量与任务成功率的关联，为理解WAM性能与改进方向提供了见解。

Conclusion: 将深度信息通过非线性编码融入冻结VAE分词器是高效可行的3D感知世界行动建模方案，能同时提升动作预测与未来状态预测性能；世界模型预测质量与策略成功率之间存在相关性，可为未来WAM性能的提升提供指导。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage the capabilities of large-scale pretrained video diffusion models to jointly predict future observations and actions, inheriting rich visual and physical priors from internet-scale video. This has made them a promising paradigm for robot policy learning, yet the prevailing models operate exclusively on RGB observations and do not leverage 3D information. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Spatially Aware World Action Model (SA-WAM), which repurposes a pretrained video model for joint action, RGB, and depth prediction, enabling 3D-aware world modeling and action prediction within a single diffusion backbone. We use a nonlinear encoding that maps the unbounded depth signal into the bounded input domain expected by the frozen VAE tokenizer. This allows us to reuse the tokenizer without 3D-specific fine-tuning, incorporating geometric information without sacrificing the pretrained priors. SA-WAM achieves state-of-the-art results on the RoboCasa and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks, while simultaneously improving future-state predictions. Furthermore, SA-WAM outperforms strong baselines in real-world evaluation using a UR5 robotic arm, with strong gains in randomized environments. We analyze the correlation between world model prediction quality and rollout success, providing insights into WAM performance and avenues for its improvement.

</details>


### [90] [RGB-to-IR image translation for infrared vehicle detection in unseen UAV domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02556)
*Thijs A. Eker,Ella P. Fokkinga,Jan Erik van Woerden,Elfi I. S. Hofmeijer,Sebastiaan P. Snel,Klamer Schutte,Friso G. Heslinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究利用生成式RGB-to-IR翻译来缓解热红外（IR）航空车辆检测中训练数据稀缺的问题。在配对RGB-IR源数据集上训练多种生成器（监督GAN、ControlNet扩散模型、基于LoRA的基础模型编辑），将保留目标域的RGB训练图像翻译为合成IR数据，用于训练RF-DETR车辆检测器。结果表明，Stable Diffusion 3.5配合ControlNet效果最佳，在Kust4K和VTUAV两个未见目标域上分别将mAP提升至60.1（相对50.8）和38.4（相对25.6），并且通过多种子与提示词变化进一步增益；尽管与真实目标域IR数据仍有差距，生成式翻译能有效缓解IR数据稀缺并改善跨域检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的热红外航空车辆检测数据稀缺，而无人机RGB影像丰富，因此自然想到利用RGB-to-IR翻译进行数据增强。然而，热红外中存在不可观测的热特征（如发动机热量），导致跨模态映射难以迁移。作者希望验证现代生成式翻译器能否克服这一跨模态差距，从而在未见的目标无人机域上提升红外车辆检测性能。

Method: 在配对的RGB-IR源数据集上训练三类生成式翻译器：监督GAN、基于ControlNet的扩散模型（Stable Diffusion 3.5）、以及通过LoRA进行的基础模型编辑。随后将这些翻译器应用于从保留目标数据集中抽取的RGB训练图像，生成合成IR数据，用以训练RF-DETR车辆检测器，并在五个航空数据集（以Kust4K和VTUAV为目标域）的未见IR测试集上评估。此外还探索了多随机种子与提示词变化以增加合成数据多样性。

Result: 合成IR数据一致优于RGB和灰度基线。Stable Diffusion 3.5 + ControlNet表现最佳：在Kust4K上mAP从50.8升至60.1，在VTUAV上从25.6升至38.4（对比仅用源域IR数据训练的模型）。增加输出多样性带来额外收益：VTUAV上多种子提升+1.1 mAP，提示词变化提升+3.3 mAP。但与真实目标域IR数据相比仍存在性能差距。

Conclusion: 尽管与真实IR数据仍有差距，生成式RGB-to-IR翻译能有效缓解IR数据稀缺问题，提升跨域航空车辆检测性能；扩散模型结合ControlNet是当前最优的翻译方案，且合成数据多样性（种子、提示词）对目标域性能有正向作用。

Abstract: Synthetic training data is crucial for developing vision AI when real-world data is scarce, as in thermal infrared (IR) aerial vehicle detection. While abundant UAV RGB imagery motivates RGB-to-IR translation for data augmentation, unobservable thermal traits (e.g., engine heat) make learning transferable mappings challenging. This work investigates whether modern generative translators can overcome this cross-modal gap to improve infrared vehicle detection on unseen UAV target domains. Translators are trained on paired RGB-IR source datasets and applied to RGB training images from held-out target datasets to generate synthetic IR data. Evaluated methods include supervised GANs, ControlNet-based diffusion models, and foundation-model editing via LoRA. The resulting synthetic IR imagery is used to train RF-DETR vehicle detectors, which are evaluated on unseen IR target test splits across five aerial datasets, with Kust4K and VTUAV serving as target domains. Synthetic IR consistently outperforms RGB and grayscale baselines. Stable Diffusion 3.5 with ControlNet yields the best results, improving mAP from 50.8 to 60.1 on Kust4K and from 25.6 to 38.4 on VTUAV compared to models trained only on source-domain IR data. Increasing output diversity via multiple seeds (+1.1 mAP) and prompt variations (+3.3 mAP) provides additional gains on VTUAV. Although a performance gap to real target IR data remains, generative RGB-to-IR translation effectively mitigates IR data scarcity and improves cross-domain aerial vehicle detection.

</details>


### [91] [Stereo 4D Radar for 3D Object Detection: Integrating Geometric Alignment and Absolute Velocity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02560)
*Seung-Hyun Song,Dong-Hee Paek,Woong-Chan Byun,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于立体4D雷达的3D目标检测框架，利用左右雷达之间的几何视差估计目标的绝对速度，并通过融合互补特征实现更鲁棒的感知；在自建立体4D雷达数据集上，相比最先进的单目4D雷达基线，AP 3D提升8.82点、AP BEV提升9.0点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达虽能在恶劣天气下检测3D目标并提供多普勒运动信息，但原始信号包含大量杂波（路面、护栏、周围车辆）、多径鬼影反射和接收机底噪，预处理后雷达数据往往过于稀疏；同时，多普勒测量仅反映目标速度的径向分量，难以恢复完整运动状态，这限制了基于单目4D雷达的目标检测性能。

Method: 提出立体4D雷达3D目标检测框架：利用左右两个雷达间的几何视差（disparity）估计目标的绝对速度；通过融合左右雷达的互补特征实现更鲁棒的环境感知；并在自建立体4D雷达数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在自建立体4D雷达数据集上，与最先进的单目4D雷达基线相比，AP 3D提升8.82点，AP BEV提升9.0点。

Conclusion: 绝对速度估计结合立体几何感知的特征融合，能显著提升3D目标检测性能，证明了立体4D雷达配置相比单目配置的优势。

Abstract: Four-dimensional (4D) Radar is a powerful sensing modality capable of detecting surrounding three-dimensional (3D) objects under diverse weather conditions and providing Doppler-based motion information. However, raw 4D Radar signals contain significant clutter from road surfaces, guardrails, and surrounding vehicles, along with multipath-induced ghost reflections and the receiver's inherent noise floor. Consequently, preprocessing algorithms designed to remove such invalid measurements often make the Radar data excessively sparse. Moreover, the Doppler measurements provided by 4D Radar describe only the radial component of an object's velocity, limiting their ability to recover the full motion state. In this paper, we introduce a stereo 4D Radar-based 3D object detection framework that exploits the geometric disparity between left and right Radars to estimate the absolute velocity of objects and achieve more robust perception through the fusion of their complementary features. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated on our in-house stereo 4D Radar dataset, demonstrating performance gains of 8.82 points in AP 3D and 9.0 points in AP BEV over state-of-the-art mono 4D Radar baselines. These results demonstrate that absolute velocity estimation combined with stereo geometry-aware feature fusion leads to substantial improvements in 3D object detection.

</details>


### [92] [MARS: What Retrieval Signals Are Hidden in Multimodal Large Language Models for Text-Video Retrieval?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02565)
*Uicheol Jung,Juyoung Hong,Geuntaek Lim,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARS 是一个面向文本-视频检索的多层多槽位（multi-layer, multi-slot）嵌入框架，通过融合不同解码器层的隐藏状态构造多个自适应表示槽位，并引入难负例感知的槽位专门化目标，在四个基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本-视频检索需要能区分场景、动作和时间模式相似的视频的表示。现有将多模态大语言模型用作嵌入模型的做法通常只用最终层的单个 token 表示输入，这会压缩多样的视频-文本线索、限制细粒度检索能力。

Method: MARS 设计多层多槽位嵌入框架：结合不同解码器层的隐藏状态构造多个自适应表示槽位；对文本和视频的对应槽位进行匹配（slot-wise comparison），聚合相似度进行检索；并提出难负例感知的槽位专门化目标（hard-negative-aware slot specialization objective），促使各槽位捕获具有判别性的匹配线索。

Result: 在四个文本-视频检索基准上，MARS 在直接基于相似度的检索和重排序（reranking）两种设置下均达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）结果；消融实验与分析表明，多层融合、多槽位以及难负例感知的槽位专门化三者贡献互补。

Conclusion: 通过多层表示融合与多槽位匹配，可以有效缓解单个 token 表示带来的信息压缩问题，提升文本-视频检索的细粒度判别能力；代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-video retrieval requires representations that can distinguish videos with similar scenes, actions, and temporal patterns. Recent multimodal large language models have been adapted as embedding models, but they often represent each input using a single token from the final layer. This can compress diverse video-text cues into a single vector and limit fine-grained retrieval. To address this limitation, we propose MARS, a multi-layer and multi-slot embedding framework for text-video retrieval. MARS constructs multiple adaptive representation slots by combining hidden states from different decoder layers, compares corresponding text and video slots, and aggregates their similarities for retrieval. To better handle confusing candidates, we further introduce a hard-negative-aware slot specialization objective that encourages the slots to capture discriminative matching cues. Experiments on four text-video retrieval benchmarks show that MARS achieves state-of-the-art results in both direct similarity-based retrieval and reranking settings. Ablation studies and analyses demonstrate that multi-layer fusion, multiple slots, and hard-negative-aware slot specialization provide complementary gains. Code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/MARS.

</details>


### [93] [Deeply Interleaved Text-Image Contexts for Multimodal LLMs Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02573)
*Zihao Wang,Xi Xiang,Yuwen Sun,Yingyu Li,Yabo Zhang,Yihan Zeng,Fan Li,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TIC-Bench，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在深度交织文本-图像场景下整合信息能力的全新基准，涵盖逻辑、时间与空间关联三大核心领域、八个具体类型、共 2,280 道题目。评测 10 个 SOTA MLLM 后发现其与人类专家存在显著差距，且难以整合分散在交错图文输入中的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型的评测与训练主要聚焦于多图像任务，通常将文本仅视为任务指令，缺乏与视觉内容的深层语义交互。然而，真实应用（如文本-图像协同创作、角色追踪、空间重建）需要文本与图像持续交互，要求模型深入理解交织上下文。现有基准无法有效评估这一能力，因此需要专门基准来填补空白。

Method: 设计并构建 TIC-Bench 基准，围绕逻辑关联（Logical）、时间关联（Temporal）、空间关联（Spatial）三大核心领域，细分为八个具体类型，共生成 2,280 道问答题目。题目要求模型整合分散在交织文本与图像中的线索并恢复真实事实（ground truth facts）。作者对 10 个 SOTA 多模态大语言模型进行了系统评测，并与人类专家表现进行对比。

Result: 评测结果显示，SOTA MLLM 与人类专家之间存在显著性能差距；模型普遍存在持续性的困难，尤其是在整合分布在交织视觉与文本输入中的证据方面表现不佳。该基准能够有效暴露模型在深交织图文环境中的短板。

Conclusion: TIC-Bench 为评估和推动多模态模型在深度交织文本-图像情境中整合图文信息的能力提供了有价值的分析工具，并已公开（https://huggingface.co/datasets/pino10010/TIC-Bench），可用于指导后续模型的评测与能力改进。

Abstract: Current evaluations and training of multimodal models predominantly focus on multi-image tasks, largely overlooking interleaved text-image scenarios. In such multi-image tasks, text typically serves merely as task instructions, lacking deep semantic interaction with the visual content. In contrast, realworld applications like text-image co-creation, character tracking, and spatial reconstruction require constant interaction between text and images. Consequently, models must possess a deep understanding of these interleaved contexts. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel benchmark, TIC-Bench (deeply interleaved Text-Image Contexts), designed to evaluate the capability of models to integrate text-image clues and recover the ground truth facts within deeply interleaved contexts. This benchmark encompasses three core domains: Logical, Temporal, and Spatial Association, which are further categorized into eight specific types, comprising a total of 2,280 questions. We evaluated 10 state-of-the-art MLLMs and observed a substantial performance gap compared to human experts, together with persistent difficulties in integrating evidence distributed across interleaved visual and textual inputs. Ultimately, this benchmark provides a valuable analytical tool for assessing and advancing the ability of multimodal models to effectively integrate text and image information in deeply interleaved contexts. TIC-Bench is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/pino10010/TIC-Bench

</details>


### [94] [Generalizable Brain Tumor Segmentation with Self-Training and Tumor-Aware Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02600)
*Henrique Zan Grande,Jeovane Honorio Alves,Rayson Laroca,Andre Gustavo Hochuli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对BraTS 2026挑战赛的跨肿瘤泛化（BraTS-GoAT）任务，提出基于nnU-Net框架、采用大残差编码器的分割方法，结合基于伪标签的半监督学习与肿瘤感知的可变形数据增强，在验证集上显著提升了全肿瘤、肿瘤核心和增强肿瘤三个子区域的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤子区域分割模型在不同患者群体间的泛化能力不足是临床应用的重大挑战。BraTS-GoAT任务旨在解决该问题，要求模型在异质性患者人群中实现稳健的分割。作者希望通过结合半监督学习和针对肿瘤的特定增强策略，充分利用未标注数据并增强模型对病灶形态变化的鲁棒性，从而提升跨肿瘤泛化性能。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 采用nnU-Net框架并配备大残差编码器架构用于分割；(2) 半监督学习技术：利用未标注训练数据生成伪标签，并选择置信度最高的伪标签样本参与训练；(3) 肿瘤感知的可变形增强：在保留周围正常解剖结构的同时对病灶区域进行局部变形。作者对各组件的单独贡献及组合效果进行了消融实验，并评估不同比例的置信伪标签样本的影响。

Result: 提交的泛化任务配置在BraTS-GoAT验证集上取得：全肿瘤Dice 0.881/NSD 0.473，肿瘤核心Dice 0.817/NSD 0.490，增强肿瘤Dice 0.775/NSD 0.533。在所有肿瘤区域上均优于仅用标注数据的基线，验证了自训练（伪标签）与所提增强方法具有互补性。

Conclusion: 实验证明半监督自训练与肿瘤感知可变形增强的组合能够有效提升脑肿瘤子区域分割在异质性人群中的泛化能力，二者互补而非冗余。代码已公开，为跨肿瘤泛化分割提供了可复现的解决方案与基线参考。

Abstract: This work presents an approach to the Generalizability Across Tumors (BraTS-GoAT) task of the BraTS 2026 Challenge, which focuses on robust segmentation of brain tumor sub-regions across a heterogeneous patient population. The proposed method employs the nnU-Net framework with a large residual encoder architecture, integrating a semi-supervised learning technique with pseudo-labels generated from the unlabeled training data and a tumor-aware deformable augmentation that locally deforms the lesion while preserving the surrounding anatomy. We evaluate the individual contributions of each component, as well as their combination, using varying proportions of the most confident pseudo-labeled cases. The submitted configuration for the generalization task achieves Dice and NSD scores of 0.881 and 0.473 for Whole Tumor, 0.817 and 0.490 for Tumor Core, and 0.775 and 0.533 for Enhancing Tumor on the BraTS-GoAT validation set, improving over the labeled-only baselines across all tumor regions and confirming that self-training and the proposed augmentation are complementary. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Henrique-zan/brats-goat-2026/.

</details>


### [95] [AffectDelta: Beyond Emotion Labels for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02616)
*Xingzu Zhan,Lin Gu,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AffectDelta 是一种源感知的情感驱动图像编辑方法，将编辑任务建模为八维情感分布之间的迁移（而非单一情感类别标签），利用冻结的情感分布预测器估计源状态，并通过有符号的源-目标差异向量驱动基于扩散模型的上下文相关视觉变换；配套构建了包含 248,841 对样本的 AffectPair-249K 数据集，实验证明其在情感对齐与内容保持上优于六种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
