Method: 作者提出"虚拟展开适配器"（virtual-unrolling adapter），将 Jacobian 透镜扩展到迭代/循环架构，使得可以分析权重复用的深度维度。随后对两种代表性循环架构（Ouro-2.6B：48 层循环 4 次并深度监督；Huginn-0125：4 层核心循环 16 次、针对潜在推理训练）施加完整工作空间测试：透镜拟合、读出，以及 11 类因果实验（写入、消融、线性传输等），并以 64 层非共享权重的 Qwen3.6-27B 为标准基线进行对照。

Result: 两种循环架构在迭代部分都形成了工作空间，但循环改变了其访问方式。Ouro：每个循环内重建工作空间内容，线性传输无法跨循环边界携带内容，因此写入和消融必须覆盖剩余每个循环。Huginn：内容可跨越全部 16 次递归前传，但读出、写入和消融仅作用于约 2 次递归的滑动窗口内。此外，新注入内容能否被语言化与显式的逐次迭代监督一致，而既有内容能否被操控（steered）则与监督无关。

Conclusion: 循环确实能在迭代部分形成功能性的全局工作空间，但其可访问性模式与堆叠层架构本质不同：Ouro 是"每循环重建、跨循环隔离"，Huginn 是"跨循环保持、近窗口操作"。研究还揭示了监督信号的作用边界——显式逐迭代监督决定新内容的可语言化性，却不决定既有内容的方向操控能力，表明工作空间的形成与内容的可访问性是受循环结构和训练目标共同塑造的两个维度。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a mid-depth band of verbalisable, causally potent representations in a standard feedforward transformer --- a functional analogue of a global workspace. Whether the same workspace functionality emerges when depth is implemented through recurrence rather than a stack of distinct layers remains unknown. Looped and depth-recurrent transformers provide a direct test of this question because they reuse the same weights across depth. We extend the Jacobian lens to iterated architectures using a virtual-unrolling adapter. We apply the full workspace suite --- lens fitting, readout, and eleven causal experiment families --- to Ouro-2.6B (48 layers looped 4 times, deeply supervised) and Huginn-0125 (a 4-layer core recurred 16 times, trained for latent reasoning), using Qwen3.6-27B (64 untied layers) as the standard baseline. We find that a workspace forms in the iterated part of each architecture, but that recurrence changes how it can be accessed. Ouro reconstructs workspace content in every loop, and linear transport cannot carry that content across loop boundaries; writes and ablations must therefore span every remaining loop. Huginn carries content forward across all sixteen recurrences, while reads, writes, and ablations act only within a sliding window of roughly two recurrences. Whether newly injected content can be verbalised tracks explicit per-iteration supervision; whether existing content can be steered does not.

</details>


### [193] [Post-Training Ternarization of Qwen3-4B Capability, Effective Bit Budget, Storage Compression, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01962)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了对Qwen 4B指令微调模型进行端到端后训练量化转换（仅权重量化，激活保持16位），采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三值化和GPTQ式误差补偿，最终量化线性权重达到1.641有效比特/权重，覆盖81.62%参数。转换后十个基准平均准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，各任务退化不均（BoolQ保留84.6%的教师性能，ARC-Challenge仅保留43.8%），困惑度普遍上升（如WikiText-2从13.639升至18.748）。打包可将模型从8.29 GiB压缩至3.96 GiB且困惑度基本不变，但作者明确不主张压缩本身能带来更快的推理（初步Triton GEMV微基准比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在澄清"1.58-bit"这种名义标签的误导性：它无法完整描述低比特模型的存储表示、保留能力或运行时行为。作者希望通过一个真实、端到端的4B参数模型转换实验，给出精确的有效比特核算、能力保留率、困惑度、校准敏感性、检查点构成和部署行为数据，从而基于证据回答低比特压缩究竟带来了什么。

Method: 采用后训练量化（PTQ）路线，对指令微调的Qwen 4B模型实施仅权重（weight-only）转换：使用KOTMS旋转预处理权重，E2M-ATQ三值化将权重映射到{-1,0,+1}平面与缩放，并用TWLA中的GPTQ式误差补偿修正量化误差。激活保持16位精度，因此省略ILA-AMP。实验内容包括有效比特核算、任务能力保持（十个评分基准）、困惑度（WikiText-2/PTB/C4）、校准敏感性、检查点组成分析、打包压缩，以及基于Triton的GEMV部署微基准。

Result: 最终转换产物：量化线性权重使用1.641有效比特/权重，覆盖81.62%的模型参数。十个能力评分对比中准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，退化不均——BoolQ保留84.6%的机遇校正教师性能，而ARC-Challenge仅保留43.8%。困惑度分别从13.639升至18.748（WikiText-2）、24.700升至31.992（PTB）、19.831升至28.966（C4）。打包运行在保持三值平面与缩放的前提下将报告模型大小从8.29 GiB降至3.96 GiB，困惑度基本不变；第三方打包存在有损，被排除在主要产物声明之外。打包产物未进行端到端任务准确率或生成吞吐基准；Triton GEMV微基准在测试形状下比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍。

Conclusion: 结论是：该转换在存储压缩（8.29→3.96 GiB）上有效，但能力有显著且不均匀的损失，且作者明确不声称压缩本身能带来更快的推理——目前实测GEMV反而更慢。文章强调应基于精确的比特核算与端到端评测而非名义位宽标签来评估低比特模型，并将打包产物的端到端基准视为后续必要工作。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models can reduce storage and memory bandwidth, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not fully describe the stored representation, retained capability, or runtime behavior.
  We study an end-to-end post-training conversion of Qwen, an instruction-tuned 4B-parameter model, using KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ ternarization, and GPTQ-style error compensation from TWLA. The experiment is weight-only: activations remain at 16-bit precision, so ILA-AMP is omitted. We evaluate effective bit accounting, task capability retention, perplexity, calibration sensitivity, checkpoint composition, and deployment behavior.
  The final conversion uses 1.641 effective bits per weight for quantized linear weights, with 81.62% of model parameters targeted. Across ten scored capability comparisons, accuracy falls from 64.5% to 54.7%. Degradation is uneven: BoolQ retains 84.6% chance-corrected teacher performance, while ARC-Challenge retains 43.8%. Perplexity rises from 13.639 to 18.748 on WikiText-2, 24.700 to 31.992 on PTB, and 19.831 to 28.966 on C4.
  A subsequent packing run preserves the ternary planes and scales, reducing reported model size from 8.29 GiB to 3.96 GiB with essentially unchanged perplexity. A separate third-party packing attempt was lossy and is excluded from the primary artifact claim. The packed artifact has not been benchmarked end-to-end for task accuracy or generation throughput. A preliminary Triton GEMV microbenchmark is 4.6x slower than FP16 cuBLAS on one tested shape. We therefore do not claim that compression alone yields faster inference.

</details>


### [194] [Benchmarking Language Models for Statistical Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01982)
*Chen Wang,Junzhe Zhao,Xin Cong,Wanlu Deng,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了"统计问题构建"（Statistical Problem Formulation）这一上游任务，将其分解为统计问题分类与变量识别及角色分配两个子任务，并发布了包含1013个样本、覆盖20个粗粒度与85个细粒度统计问题类别的基准StatFormBench。对14个开源与闭源LLM的评估显示，最佳零样本模型在细粒度分类准确率上仅达72.0%，变量集合重叠度仅63.2%，且没有模型在两个子任务上同时表现最好，增强提示策略带来的提升有限且不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM统计与数据科学能力的评估大多假设分析目标已经明确给定，但在实际应用中，用户往往带着非正式目标和异构数据而来，需要模型自行判断隐含的统计任务并选择相关数据。这一"上游"步骤缺乏形式化定义与专门的基准，因此作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先将统计问题构建形式化，分解为两个子任务：(1)统计问题分类（Statistical Problem Classification）；(2)变量识别与角色分配（Variable Identification & Role Assignment）。随后基于五本跨领域统计学教科书和一个数据科学案例库构建StatFormBench，涵盖多样的问题类型、数据表示和场景风格，共1013个样本、20个粗粒度与85个细粒度统计问题类别，并提供了评估代码与数据。

Result: 在14个开源与闭源LLM上的评估中，最佳零样本模型仅达到72.0的细粒度分类准确率和63.2的变量集合重叠度；没有模型能在两个子任务上均表现最优；增强提示策略带来的性能提升有限且不一致。

Conclusion: 统计问题构建是LLM用于统计与数据科学工作的重要但尚未解决的环节，当前模型在该任务上能力不足且缺乏一致性，提示策略难以稳定改善，凸显了需要专门针对该任务进行建模与基准化研究的必要性。作者已公开基准数据与评估代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as assistants for statistical and data science work, yet existing evaluations largely assume the analysis target is already specified. In practice, users arrive with informal goals and heterogeneous data, leaving the model to decide what statistical task is implied and which data are relevant. We first formalize this upstream step as Statistical Problem Formulation and decompose it into two subtasks: (1) Statistical Problem Classification and (2) Variable Identification & Role Assignment. We then introduce StatFormBench, a benchmark built from five cross-domain statistics textbooks and a data science case library, covering diverse problem types, data representations, and scenario styles. It contains 1,013 samples spanning 20 coarse-grained and 85 fine-grained statistical problem categories. Across 14 open- and closed-source LLMs, the best zero-shot models reach only 72.0 fine-grained classification accuracy and 63.2 variable set overlap. No model performs consistently best across the two subtasks, while enhanced prompting strategies yield only limited or inconsistent gains. We release the benchmark data on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench and the evaluation code on GitHub at https://github.com/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench.

</details>


### [195] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过单一会话的案例研究，刻画了一个LLM编码智能体在依据预置详细规范实现多组件数据系统时的行为。智能体的自主性仅限于实现、缺陷诊断修复及交互设计；研究记录了5类缺陷（按违反的约束与检测方法分类），并在HotpotQA基准上评估了该架构中唯一的检索权衡：先按图识别实体集过滤候选再做排序，对比不过滤直接搜索。由于缺少LLM运行条件，作者用基准黄金证据标签替代实体识别。在检索预算1至10、100道问题和2994段合并语料下，过滤式召回在预算3时即达上限（因候选已被限制在黄金段落内），而不过滤搜索即使在预算10时也只有69%的召回，差距在所有预算下均显著（符号检验p<0.0001）。文章最后讨论了智能体自主性成功与需要人工纠正之处，包括一次声称的性能修复从未在引发问题的回归上重新测量的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM编码智能体越来越多地承担端到端工程任务，学界缺乏对其在系统级需求（模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性、检索过滤权衡）上行为的经验性刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过受控案例研究观察智能体在实现多组件数据系统时的实际表现与缺陷模式。

Method: 采用单会话案例研究法：存储技术、模式、实体解析算法和检索过滤策略预先固定，智能体自主权仅限实现、自诊断与修复缺陷以及交互设计。作者编目了5个缺陷，按违反的约束类别与检测方式分类。随后在公开HotpotQA基准上评估指定的检索权衡（图识别实体集过滤后排序 vs 无过滤搜索），用基准黄金证据标签替代实体识别阶段，采用标准召回率而非基准自带的准确率指标，覆盖检索预算1至10、100道问题、2994段合并语料，并以符号检验验证统计显著性。

Result: 过滤式检索在预算3时召回即达上限（符合预期，因候选被限制在黄金段落内）；无过滤搜索即使预算为10，完整证据召回率也仅69%，且该差距在每个预算水平下都成立（符号检验p<0.0001）。此外记录了5类由智能体引入的缺陷，按其违反的约束与检测方法归类；还发现一次声称的性能修复从未在最初引发该问题的回归测试上被重新测量。

Conclusion: 作者总结了智能体自主性的成功之处与需要人工纠正之处，强调在检索架构中，图实体过滤能显著提高低预算下的召回效率；同时指出智能体在工程实践中存在验证缺失的隐患（声称修复却未复测），提示对LLM编码智能体引入缺陷的模式及其验证纪律需要进一步的经验研究与保障机制。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [196] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClaimReceipt——一种针对智能体（agent）评估的“声明相对”收据规范与选择性验证器：将类型化交易证据绑定到已签名的实验清单（manifest），对每条声明返回 PASS、INVALID 或 INCONCLUSIVE，并同时验证声明的可重计算性（充分性）与记录对承诺实验集的覆盖性。实现前将规范冻结（SHA-256 18d109…b81），通过 1,392 条历史记录上的 CR-2 验证实验与前向 CR-3 纪元实验验证了其正确性、非冗余性、故障检测能力与轻量开销，并指出冻结规范仍存在可读性/无歧义性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评估面临两个不同的证据问题：一是声称的结论能否从保留证据中重新计算（充分性/sufficiency），二是保留的记录是否覆盖了承诺进行的实验集合（覆盖性/coverage）。通用日志与哈希链式转录（hash-linked transcripts）都无法可靠地回答这两个问题，因此需要一种将证据与承诺实验集绑定、使遗漏可见的验证机制。

Method: ClaimReceipt 采用声明相对（claim-relative）的收据规范：将类型化交易证据（typed transaction evidence）绑定到签名的实验清单，并提供选择性验证器，逐条声明输出 PASS、INVALID 或 INCONCLUSIVE。规范在实现前即被冻结（锚定 SHA-256 18d109…b81）以支持先验验证。验证包括：基于 1,392 条历史买家–卖家记录的 CR-2 验证器（复现 5 个人工审计结论、重放 600 条确定性记录与 792 条生成后记录、13 个字段组消融非冗余测试、11/11 语义故障检出与 0/8 误报），以及独立的前瞻性 CR-3 纪元（30 个任务在推理前提交、终端收据签名并链式串联、私有证据加密给审计方），并对比扣留一条终端收据与扣留全部私有证据开放两种情形，检验预先注册的预测。

Result: CR-2 验证器在 1,392 条记录上完全复现全部 5 个人工审计结论，精确重放 600 条确定性与 792 条生成后记录，使全部 13 个声明的字段组在消融下均非冗余，并在 11/11 个语义故障上返回预期结果且无 1 例误报（0/8）。CR-3 前向纪元中：完整证据给出覆盖与记账 PASS；扣留一条终端收据返回 INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE；扣留全部私有证据开放则保持覆盖与协议验证通过，但使经济类声明不可判定——与预先注册的预测完全一致。接收（receipt）插桩开销仅为模型推理时间的 0.021%，且每笔交易 9.9 KB。规范可读性探针表明冻结规范对独立读者而言仍非完全无歧义。

Conclusion: 声明验证必须同时具备两个条件：声明充分的证据（claim-sufficient evidence），以及一个已承诺的实验全集（committed universe），使遗漏/缺口变得可见。ClaimReceipt 证明该思路在正确性、故障检测、轻量开销上前景可行，但规范的清晰度仍需改进，方能使其成为可被外部独立审计的解释性标准。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81). On 1,392 historical buyer--seller records, a CR-2 verifier reproduces all five manually labeled audit verdicts, exactly replays 600 deterministic and 792 post-generation records, makes every one of 13 declared field groups non-redundant under tested ablations, and returns the expected result on 11/11 semantic faults with 0/8 false positives. We then run a separate prospective CR-3 epoch: 30 assignments are committed before inference, terminal receipts are signed and chained, and private evidence is encrypted for an auditor. Complete evidence yields coverage and accounting PASS; withholding one terminal receipt returns INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE, while withholding all private openings preserves coverage and protocol verification but makes economic claims inconclusive, exactly matching a preregistered prediction. Receipt instrumentation adds 0.021% of model-inference time and 9.9 KB per transaction. A specification-legibility probe indicates that our own frozen specification is not yet unambiguous to an independent reader. Claim verification therefore requires both claim-sufficient evidence and a committed universe against which omissions become visible.

</details>


### [197] [HeadWiseKV: Budgeted Per-Head Cache Residency for Hybrid Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02029)
*Renjie Xie,Juncheng Yang,Aoting Hu,Mingxi Zhang,Liyao Wu,Zheheng Hong,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeadWiseKV 是一个无需训练的 KV 缓存压缩框架，面向混合语言模型的长上下文推理。它通过为每个物理 KV 头分配静态多级历史窗口，在服务前将缓存需求变得可预测，并将该分配建模为受限的率失真优化问题，由核心算法 SeqCalib 按层执行顺序生成策略，再由分组缓存运行时实现为真实的每头 KV 驻留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理过程中不断增长的 KV 缓存占用了大量 GPU 显存并可能降低生成吞吐；这种瓶颈同样存在于混合语言模型中，因为其残差全局注意力层会主导与上下文相关的缓存需求。作者希望研究如何在一个总的 KV 驻留预算约束下分配这些状态，从而在控制显存开销的同时尽量保持下游质量。

Method: HeadWiseKV 包含三个部分：(1) 为每个物理 KV 头分配静态、多级的历史窗口，使缓存需求在服务前即可预测；(2) 将该分配形式化为受限的运行率失真问题，并提出 SeqCalib 作为核心策略生成算法，它按执行顺序逐层处理，并以部署时使用的低层策略为条件来决定当前层，从而刻画跨深度交互；(3) 一个分组缓存运行时，将所选策略物化为实际的每头 KV 驻留，而非在完整缓存上施加掩码。

Result: 在四个混合长上下文模型上评估下游质量，HeadWiseKV 保持了接近 Fully-KV 的 RULER 与 LoCoMo 质量；在 Qwen3.6-27B 的固定模型系统研究中，它在 112K 上下文长度下将采样峰值设备显存降低 8.59%，并将最大验证成功的上下文长度从 114K 扩展到 161K。

Conclusion: HeadWiseKV 通过可预测的静态每头 KV 驻留分配，在混合模型中实现了接近完整 KV 缓存的下游质量，同时显著降低峰值显存并延长可服务的上下文长度，是一种实用且有效的免训练长上下文 KV 缓存压缩方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference retains a growing key--value (KV) cache during decoding, which consumes substantial GPU memory and can reduce generation throughput. This bottleneck remains in hybrid language models because their residual global-attention layers can dominate context-dependent cache demand. We study how to allocate this state under an aggregate KV-residency budget. We introduce HeadWiseKV, a training-free framework that compresses the residual global KV caches of hybrid language models while preserving their native local, recurrent, and linear paths. It assigns each physical KV head a static, multilevel history window, making cache demand predictable before serving. We formulate this allocation as a restricted operational rate--distortion problem and propose SeqCalib as the core policy-generation algorithm in HeadWiseKV. SeqCalib processes layers in execution order and conditions each decision on the lower-layer policy used at deployment, thereby accounting for interactions across depth. A grouped-cache runtime materializes the selected policy as actual per-head KV residency rather than a mask over a full cache. We evaluate downstream quality across four hybrid long-context models and study physical residency and serving behavior on Qwen3.6-27B. HeadWiseKV retains near-Full-KV RULER and LoCoMo quality across the evaluated models. In the fixed-model systems study, it reduces sampled peak device memory by 8.59\% at a 112K context length and extends the largest verified successful context from 114K to 161K.

</details>


### [198] [Monitoring Web Agents Without Internal Signals: Observable Trajectories and Key-Step Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02057)
*Sitong Pan,Yipeng Shen,Yilin Lu,Caiwen Ding,Lu Cheng,Qianwen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Web智能体的前缀级风险预测问题：在无法获取模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits）的情况下，仅利用可观测的轨迹信号（宏观特征与微观特征）判断当前执行是否仍在正轨或趋于失败，并证明该方法的预测能力可与内部信号基线竞争，支持早期干预且可跨网站类别迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中常无法获取模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits），导致Web智能体的可靠监控变得困难。因此需要探索仅基于可观测轨迹信号的替代性风险预测方法，以判断智能体当前执行是否偏离正轨。

Method: 提出两种可观测的轨迹表示：Macro特征总结跨步骤的智能体-环境行为与反馈；Micro特征通过重复黑盒查询衡量意图、动作与预期状态变化之间的一致性。标签设计上不直接继承最终结果标签，而是将第一个未被后续轨迹纠正、且与最终失败关联的关键错误标记为关键步边界，从而保留失败轨迹中早期有效前缀的"正轨"标签。在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web基准上，结合五种开源与闭源骨干模型进行验证。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web两个Web智能体基准上，基于五个开源与闭源骨干模型，可观测轨迹信号的风险预测能力与内部信号基线相当；所得预测器还支持在固定误切预算下的早期干预，并能迁移到未见过的网站类别。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅依赖可观测轨迹信号即可获得有价值的Web智能体风险预测能力，为无法访问内部信号的实际部署场景提供了可行的监控方案。

Abstract: Reliable web-agent monitoring is difficult when model-internal uncertainty signals such as token logits are unavailable. In this work, we study prefix-level risk prediction for web agents using observable trajectory signals: given an evolving prefix, estimate whether the current execution remains on track or is tending toward failure. We derive two observable trajectory representations: Macro features summarize cross-step agent--environment behavior and feedback, while Micro features measure the consistency of intention, action, and anticipated state change through repeated black-box queries. Instead of inheriting the final result label, we label the first critical error that remains uncorrected in the observed continuation and is associated with final failure as a key-step boundary, preserving valid early prefixes of failed trajectories as on track. Across WebArena-Lite and Online Mind2Web web agent benchmarks with five open- and closed-source backbones, observable trajectory signals are competitive with internal-signal baselines. The resulting predictors also support early intervention under fixed false-cut budgets and transfer across held-out website categories. These findings show that observable trajectory signals support valuable risk prediction abilities.

</details>


### [199] [DocHop: Benchmarking Out-of-domain Multi-hop Reasoning in Information-Dense Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02059)
*Zhuoran Yu,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Jaden Park,Xinyi Gu,Zexue He,Soochahn Lee,Rogerio Feris,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocHop 是一个用于文档型图像中图表—文本上下文集成推理的基准测试，包含 2,074 个由随机逻辑优先生成管线构建的样本、六个任务类别，以及可控制的推理深度和视觉密度。实验表明，人类标注者准确率超过 90%，而最强 MLLM 仅达 62.83%，揭示了多模态大模型在多跳文档推理上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 基准通常将图表理解和文档问答等结构化视觉任务分开评测，忽略了一个关键能力：模型能否利用文本上下文来决定如何选择、解释和聚合图表证据。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个需要跨图表与叙述性上下文进行多步组合推理的受控测试平台。

Method: DocHop 的设计核心是：文档叙述提供多步组合约束，图表提供对应数据值，问题锚定于叙述中定义的语义参考标签，模型需先解析目标实体再从多个图表聚合证据。数据通过随机逻辑优先（stochastic logic-first）生成管线构建，支持可控制的推理深度与视觉密度，覆盖 2,074 个样本、六个任务类别。评测涵盖多种专有和开源 MLLM。

Result: 人类标注者准确率超过 90%，而最佳模型仅达到 62.83%，存在显著差距；推理增强型模型性能一致提升，但随推理复杂度增加性能下降。

Conclusion: DocHop 为多跳文档推理提供了一个受控测试床，实证表明当前 MLLM 在跨图表—上下文集成推理方面仍远未达到人类水平，且推理复杂度是影响性能的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on structured visual understanding tasks such as chart and document question answering. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate these domains in isolation, leaving underexplored a key capability: whether models can use textual context to determine how chart evidence should be selected, interpreted, and aggregated. We introduce DocHop, a benchmark for integrated chart--context reasoning in document-style images. In DocHop, the document narrative specifies multi-step compositional constraints, while charts provide the corresponding data values. Questions are grounded on a semantic reference label defined in the narrative, requiring models to resolve target entities from context before aggregating evidence across multiple charts. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct DocHop via a stochastic logic-first generation pipeline with controllable reasoning depth and visual density, covering 2,074 examples across six task categories. Experiments on a wide range of proprietary and open-source MLLMs show a substantial gap to human performance: annotators achieve over 90% accuracy, while the best model reaches only 62.83%. Reasoning-enhanced models consistently show improved results, but performance degrades as reasoning complexity increases. Overall, DocHop provides a controlled testbed for challenging multi-hop document reasoning.

</details>


### [200] [MineTRACE: An Evidence-Grounded Interactive Reasoning System for Mineral Prospectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02060)
*Yiran Zhang,Jinwen Liu,Daniel Su,Yisu Chen,Qiang Sun,Chris Gonzalez,Eun-Jung Holden,Marco Fiorentini,Wei Liu,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MineTRACE 是一个基于 Web 的证据驱动矿产勘探系统，覆盖 Cu、Au、Ni、W、Sn、Co、Ta、Mn 八种矿产。它通过透明专家树融合多源地球化学、地球物理和地质证据，生成可解释的成矿有利度评分，并支持自然语言交互查询。评分器在多种测试场景下空间 AUC 最高达 0.917，端到端评估验证了查询准确性与回答可溯源（grounding），使公共地学数据更易访问、解释和验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿产资源勘探需要整合异质的地球化学、地球物理和地质证据，但现有成矿远景系统通常只输出不透明的分数或热图，缺乏可解释性，用户难以理解评分依据并验证结果，从而降低了勘探决策的效率和可信度。

Method: 提出 MineTRACE 系统：1) 构建基于地质知识与已知矿床的透明专家树，将多源证据融合为可解释的成矿有利度评分；2) 提供 Web 界面，支持用户浏览远景图、查询位置或区域、查看支撑证据，并通过自然语言交互；3) 配置对话助手，对新位置从分析管线检索评分及支撑证据，并以自然语言呈现；4) 采用空间 AUC 评估评分性能，并通过端到端评估检验查询准确性与回答 grounding。

Result: 评分器在多种测试场景下取得最高 0.917 的空间 AUC；端到端评估结果显示系统在查询准确性和响应可溯源性（grounding）方面表现良好，证明系统能有效呈现评分与证据。

Conclusion: MineTRACE 提升了公共地学数据的可访问性、可解释性与可验证性，为矿产勘探提供更高效、透明的决策支持，展示了证据驱动、可交互式系统的实用价值。

Abstract: Mineral exploration requires integrating heterogeneous geochemical, geophysical, and geological evidence, yet existing prospectivity systems often provide only opaque scores or heatmaps. We present MineTRACE, a web-based system for evidence-grounded exploration of eight commodities: Cu, Au, Ni, W, Sn, Co, Ta, and Mn. Users can explore prospectivity maps, query locations or regions, inspect supporting evidence, and interact through natural language. A transparent expert tree, informed by geological knowledge and known deposits, combines multi-source evidence into interpretable prospectivity scores. For a new location, the conversational assistant retrieves the score and supporting evidence from the analysis pipeline and presents them in natural language. The scorer achieves spatial AUC values of up to 0.917 across different test scenarios, while end-to-end evaluation assesses query accuracy and response grounding. MineTRACE makes public geoscience data easier to access, interpret, and verify, supporting more efficient and transparent mineral exploration.

</details>


### [201] [ToolGate: An Executable Acceptance Pipeline for Tool-Dependent Scientific Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02067)
*Ke Zhang,Yankang Liu,Roya Zandi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolGate 提出一种利用语言模型批量生成科学基准题目的流水线，通过三道“门”（可执行脚本复现答案、无工具随机筛查、限时工具型智能体求解）来筛选候选题目，将答案校验与难度筛选转化为可审计流程，并在 FEniCSx 上从 500 次生成中最终保留 128 道唯一题目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学基准由领域专家逐题编写并交叉核验，或改编教科书、论文与在线资源，虽质量可靠但每道题的人力成本很高。语言模型能快速生成候选题目，但难点在于如何确认题目有效（脚本可运行、答案一致）且不平凡（模型无法仅凭题干直接作答），因此需要一个自动化的验收机制。

Method: ToolGate 将每个生成项视为提案，仅当通过三道门才保留：(1) 本地验证门——可执行的求解脚本须在用科学软件运行时复现所提议答案；(2) 无工具随机筛查门——多次随机抽取题目，若模型仅凭提示即可作答则判定为平凡并剔除；(3) 工具型智能体门——使用工具的智能体须在固定时限内求解幸存题目。在 FEniCSx 上进行了 500 次生成实例化，生成后对候选池重新筛查：两次随机无工具筛查剔除 222 道，GPT-5.5 API（默认中等推理）调用剔除 121 道，剩余 135 道由带 FEniCSx 权限的 GPT-5.5 Codex CLI 智能体求解 130 道，去重后得 128 道唯一幸存题目。

Result: 在 FEniCSx 上的 500 次生成尝试中，本地验证门保留 478 个候选；最终报告池经两轮随机无工具筛查与 GPT-5.5 API 调用后剩 135 道；Codex CLI 工具型智能体解出 130 道；精确去重后得到 128 道唯一协议幸存题目。

Conclusion: ToolGate 将重复的答案核对与难度筛选从人工环节转化为可审计的自动化流程，同时保留领域设计与最终审核给专家，从而在降低每项人力成本的同时保证基准题目的有效性与难度。

Abstract: Scientific benchmarks are commonly built by domain experts who write tasks and cross-check one another's work, or who adapt existing material from textbooks, published papers, and online resources. These routes can produce strong evaluations, but they require substantial per-item labor. Language models can reduce this repeated work by proposing candidates quickly. The remaining problem is acceptance. We target scientific questions whose answers require computations with specialist software rather than unaided reasoning alone. A candidate is invalid if its script fails or returns a different answer, or trivial if a model answers it without the software. We present ToolGate, which treats every generated item as a proposal and keeps it only if three gates pass. First, an executable solution script must reproduce the proposed answer when run with the scientific software. Second, randomized no-tool screening rejects candidates that models can already solve from the prompt alone. Third, a tool-using agent must solve each survivor within a fixed time limit. We instantiate ToolGate in FEniCSx with 500 generation attempts. The local-verification gate retains 478 candidates. For final reporting, we rescreen this pool after generation: two randomized no-tool screens exclude 222 from the reported pool, and direct GPT-5.5 API calls at medium reasoning (the API default) exclude another 121. Of the remaining 135, a GPT-5.5 Codex CLI agent with access to FEniCSx solves 130; exact deduplication leaves 128 unique protocol survivors. ToolGate turns repeated answer checking and difficulty screening into an auditable process while leaving domain design and final review to experts.

</details>


### [202] [CHIME: Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02074)
*Yongshi Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Muyang Ye,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Longyue Wang,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution (CHIME)，一种自进化记忆框架，通过将任务结果归因于计划或执行并分别存入对应的记忆库，解决现有自进化记忆方法中信用分配模糊导致的经验偏差问题，在四个长时程智能体基准上超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划能力对智能体分解长时程任务至关重要，但测试时搜索和基于训练的方法分别带来高推理成本或昂贵的训练数据需求。自进化记忆方法虽能在推理时无需参数更新地积累经验，但其依赖于最终任务结果作为反馈存在固有的信用分配问题：最终结果混淆了计划质量、执行错误与环境因素，导致积累的规划经验有偏且有噪声。

Method: CHIME是一个自进化记忆框架，维护分离的计划记忆库（planning bank）和执行记忆库（execution bank），遵循"先归因后记忆"（attribute-before-memorize）原则：首先将每个任务结果归因于计划、执行、两者或两者皆非，然后仅更新对应的记忆库。

Result: 在四个长时程智能体基准上的大量实验表明，CHIME一致优于最先进的基于训练和自进化记忆的基线。进一步分析显示：CHIME能以更少的记忆条目积累有效记忆；所学习的记忆价值忠实反映下游效用（高质量计划记忆比执行记忆更有价值）；积累的记忆可有效跨骨干模型迁移。

Conclusion: CHIME通过显式的信用归因机制显著提升了自进化记忆的质量与效率，验证了分离记忆库设计的有效性，并展现出良好的可迁移性，为无参数更新的推理时规划能力提升提供了新范式。

Abstract: Planning is a central capability that enables agents to decompose complex long-horizon tasks into manageable steps. Test-time search and training-based methods improve planning but incur high inference costs or require expensive training data. Self-evolving memory instead accumulates reusable experience from agent interaction outcomes into an external memory bank, so planning capability keeps improving at inference time without parameter updates. However, existing self-evolving memory methods share an inherent credit assignment problem: they rely on final task outcomes as feedback, but such outcomes conflate plan quality with execution errors and environmental factors, so the accumulated planning experience is often biased and noisy. To address this problem, we propose Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution (CHIME), a self-evolving memory framework that maintains a separate planning bank and execution bank and follows an attribute-before-memorize principle: CHIME first attributes each task outcome to the plan, the execution, both, or neither, and then updates only the corresponding memory bank. Extensive experiments on four long-horizon agent benchmarks show that CHIME consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-based and self-evolving memory baselines. Further analyses reveal several interesting findings. For example, CHIME accumulates effective memory with far fewer items. In addition, the learned memory values faithfully reflect downstream utility: high-quality planning memories are more valuable than execution memories. Finally, the accumulated memory effectively transfers across backbone models. Code will be released at https://github.com/ATH-MaaS/Marco-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Outcome Gaps: Process-Aware Fairness Diagnosis for LLM-based Multi-Agent Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02092)
*Yiran Zhao,Lu Zhou,Liming Fang,Yufei Chen,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu,Xiaogang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCOPED-Hiring，一个面向 LLM 多智能体招聘系统的过程感知公平性诊断流水线，通过构建受控简历变体、运行基于角色的招聘委员会并记录超过 31.1 万条结构化决策轨迹，将轨迹字段转化为六个诊断视角的量化公平信号（最终结果、反事实、过程、路径、动态与设计效应）。研究发现最终录用率的平衡可能掩盖多智能体决策轨迹中的隐性不公平——职业空窗期引发怀疑、代理线索影响资格判断、身份线索导致不平等的调查。基于诊断结果的目标修复使总体分层负担降低 72.3%，而录用率仅变动 1.86 个百分点，证明过程诊断能够有效指导修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的多智能体系统（MAS）越来越多地被用于高风险决策，然而仅基于结果的公平性审计可能遗漏决策轨迹中风险产生的位置。现有审计方法无法捕捉决策过程中不公平的根源，因此需要一种过程感知的公平性诊断方法来定位并量化轨迹层面的不公平。

Method: SCOPED-Hiring 流水线包含四个环节：构建受控简历变体、运行基于角色的招聘委员会（模拟多智能体协作决策）、记录超过 311K 条结构化决策轨迹、将轨迹字段转化为量化公平信号。这些信号按六个诊断视角组织：最终结果、反事实、过程、路径、动态和设计效应，从而系统性地定位不公平的产生节点。

Result: 实验表明，平衡的最终录用率可能掩盖多智能体决策轨迹中的隐性不公平：职业空窗期触发怀疑、代理线索（如学历、履历等替代指标）塑造资格判断、身份线索导致不平等的调查深度。基于这些诊断结果进行的目标修复将总体的分层负担降低 72.3%，同时录用率仅偏移 1.86 个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅基于结果的公平性评估不足以揭示 LLM 多智能体招聘系统中的风险；过程感知的诊断能够精确定位轨迹中的不公平来源，且针对性的修复既能大幅降低不公平负担，又能保持录用率基本稳定，说明过程诊断可以指导有效的公平性修复。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly considered for high-stakes decision-making, yet outcome-based fairness audits can miss where risks arise within the decision trajectory. We present SCOPED-Hiring, a process-aware fairness diagnosis pipeline for LLM-based hiring MAS. SCOPED-Hiring constructs controlled resume variants, runs role-based hiring committees, logs over 311K structured decision trajectories, and converts trajectory fields into quantitative fairness signals organized by six diagnostic lenses: final outcome, counterfactual, process, pathway, dynamic, and design effects. SCOPED-Hiring reveals that balanced final hire rates can mask hidden trajectory unfairness in multi-agent decision trajectories: career gaps trigger suspicion, proxy cues shape qualification judgments, and identity cues lead to unequal investigation. Targeted repair guided by these diagnoses reduces total layered burden by 72.3% while shifting the hire rate by only 1.86 pp, showing that process diagnosis can guide effective repair. Project Page: https://scoped-hiring-project-page.vercel.app/

</details>


### [204] [MASkills: Continual Skills Optimization for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02094)
*Huaiyuan Yao,Xiaoou Liu,Charles Fleming,Tianlong Chen,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASkills 是一个面向多智能体 LLM 系统的持续学习框架，通过将经验记忆转化为结构化、可操作的程序性知识（即“智能体技能”），并采用技能条件化信用分配、分层信用聚合与动量平滑优化相结合的优化流程，使技能库能够通过提炼、归纳、整合与剪枝持续演化，从而在 HotpotQA、LoCoMo 和 GAIA 等多个智能体任务上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的多智能体系统在复杂任务上表现强劲，但从交互经验中持续改进仍具挑战。现有自我反思方法虽能构建经验记忆，但记忆难以调用、修正和扩展；相比之下，智能体技能（结构化的程序性知识，明确规定何时行动、如何行动以及使用哪些资源或工具）是更具可操作性的单元。因此需要一种以技能为核心的持续学习机制。

Method: MASkills 提出了一条新的智能体优化流程，包含三个关键组件：1) 技能条件化信用分配（skill-conditioned credit assignment），将任务结果归因到具体技能；2) 分层信用聚合（hierarchical credit aggregation），在多个层级汇总信用信号；3) 动量平滑优化（momentum-smoothed optimization），稳定地更新技能参数。在此流程驱动下，智能体技能库通过四种操作持续演化：提炼（refinement）、归纳（induction）、整合（consolidation）与剪枝（pruning）。

Result: 在 HotpotQA、LoCoMo 和 GAIA 三个基准上的实验表明，MASkills 在多种智能体任务上均展现出有效性，验证了技能驱动的持续学习框架能够显著提升多智能体系统的表现。

Conclusion: MASkills 证明了以技能为单位的经验积累比传统记忆方式更利于调用、精炼与扩展，为多智能体 LLM 系统的持续学习提供了一条可扩展、可演化的新路径；代码已开源供社区复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have shown strong performance on complex tasks, yet continual improvement from interaction experience remains challenging. Existing self-reflection methods build experience memories, but memories are mostly hard to invoke, refine, or scale, while agent skills offer a more actionable unit: structured procedural knowledge that specifies when to act, how to act, and which resources or tools to use. We introduce MASkills, a continual learning framework that optimizes multi-agent LLM systems through agent skills. MASkills presents a new agent-optimization pipeline that integrates skill-conditioned credit assignment, hierarchical credit aggregation, and momentum-smoothed optimization, enabling agent skill libraries to evolve through refinement, induction, consolidation, and pruning. Experiments on HotpotQA, LoCoMo, and GAIA demonstrate the effectiveness of MASkills across multiple agentic tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/DaRL-GenAI/MASkills

</details>


### [205] [READY or Not: Reliable Enterprise Agent Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02095)
*Veronica Chatrath,Bryan Zhu,Jingxuan Fan,George Pu,Soham Dinesh Tiwari,Soham Dan,Ryan Young,Yuan,Li,Yuang Yao,Apaar Shanker,Minglai Yang,Daniel Yue Zhang,Yunzhong He,Ying Liu,Chenguang Wang,Zhijun Yin,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出READY（Reliable Enterprise Agent Deployment）框架，用于评估AI智能体是否适合部署到企业工作流。与现有基准只衡量智能体能否完成任务不同，READY在保留工作流自身成功标准的同时，通过统一资格认证流程测量人机系统的可靠性与运营成本，选择满足可靠性目标的最低成本监督策略，并在留出样本上进行统计认证，最终产出包含可靠性、人工监督负担和成本的部署画像。临床审计案例研究（16个智能体系统、750个案例）表明，自主准确率仅差0.3个百分点的两个系统（72.8% vs 72.5%）在相同76%可靠性目标下分别需要39.2%和29.6%的人工审核，揭示了自主性能之外隐藏的部署差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体基准衡量智能体能否完成专业工作，但企业部署关注的是另一问题：智能体能否在可接受的人工监督下、以可承受的成本达到所需可靠性水平。自主性能优异的智能体可能仍不适合部署，现有评估范式无法回答部署资格问题，因此需要新的合格认证框架。

Method: READY框架包含四个步骤：在保留各工作流自身成功执行定义的前提下应用统一认证程序；给定智能体、工作流和候选监督策略集，测量人机系统的可靠性与运营成本；选择满足指定可靠性目标的最低成本监督策略；在留出样本上进行统计合格认证。实现为开放测试平台，解耦工作流规范、执行、评估与认证，可运行于现有智能体评估基础设施之上。

Result: 在覆盖16个智能体系统和750个案例的端到端临床审计案例研究中，READY揭示了自主性能所隐藏的差异：自主准确率仅相差0.3个百分点（72.8% vs 72.5%）的两个系统，在相同76%可靠性目标与所评估监督策略下，分别需要39.2%与29.6%的人工审核才能达标，表明高自主性能并不等同于低部署成本。

Conclusion: READY将企业智能体评估从"智能体能把工作做得多好"转向"在什么条件下、以什么成本才能可靠部署"。通过使部署条件显式化且可统计检验，READY为比较智能体系统、设定监督要求以及做出基于证据的部署决策提供了基础。

Abstract: An AI agent can perform well on benchmarks and still be unsuitable for deployment. Existing AI-agent benchmarks measure whether an agent can complete realistic professional work, whereas enterprise deployment asks a different question: whether an agent can meet a required reliability level, under acceptable human oversight, and at tolerable cost. We introduce Reliable Enterprise Agent Deployment (READY), a framework for qualifying AI agents for deployment on enterprise workflows. READY preserves each workflow's own definition of successful execution while applying a common qualification procedure. Given an agent, a workflow, and a class of candidate oversight policies, READY measures the reliability and operating cost of the human-AI system, selects the minimum-cost policy that satisfies a specified reliability target, and statistically qualifies it on held-out cases. The resulting deployment profile characterizes the supported operating point: reliability, human-oversight burden, and cost. READY is implemented as an open testbed that decouples workflow specification, execution, evaluation, and qualification, and runs on existing agent-evaluation infrastructure. In an end-to-end clinical-audit case study spanning 16 agent systems and 750 cases, READY reveals differences hidden by autonomous performance: two systems separated by only 0.3 points in autonomous accuracy (72.8% vs. 72.5%) require 39.2% versus 29.6% human review, respectively, to qualify at the same 76% reliability target under the evaluated oversight policy. READY thus shifts enterprise agent evaluation from how well can the agent perform the work? to under what conditions, and at what cost, can it be reliably deployed? By making those conditions explicit and statistically testable, READY provides a basis for comparing agent systems, setting oversight requirements, and making evidence-based deployment decisions.

</details>


### [206] [Semantic Signal-Assisted Inspection and Recovery Allocation in Reverse Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02116)
*Jiani He,Dingyan Shang,Yihua Xu,Shiqi Huang,Yan Lyu,Jize Li,Shangjing Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出语义信号辅助决策支持（Semantic Signal-Assisted Decision Support）框架，利用退货备注文本生成"状况因子"和"信号质量评分"，指导共享人力约束下的检测深度与回收分配。在三个合成基准场景（IT设备退役、飞机维护、消费电子退货）的30对配对模拟中，关键词实现相比结构化特征对照（伴随高噪声的完全检测）提高了净回收价值并降低了检测成本。在检测成本匹配时，评分引导的目标化定位在飞机场景中每批次增加5.39万美元，短语与LLM抽取器在该场景有进一步增益；但风险盲对照（跳过检测）在纯经济目标下仍取得更高价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向物流运营者常在退货资产状况未完全观测前就需要决定检测与路由方式，而全面检测会消耗稀缺人力。现有方法缺乏利用退货备注等叙述性证据来指导检测深度与回收分配的机制，因此需要一种能在共享人力约束下预先利用文本信号辅助决策的框架。

Method: 语义信号辅助决策支持框架将退货备注转化为（1）状况因子和（2）信号质量评分，二者共同决定检测深度以及在共享人力容量下的回收资源分配。框架通过关键词、短语以及大语言模型（LLM）等多种抽取器实现信号提取，并与结构化特征比较器及风险盲对照进行对比。

Result: 在三个合成基准场景（IT设备退役、飞机维护、消费电子退货）的30对配对模拟中：关键词实现相比带噪声完全检测的结构化特征对照提升了净回收价值并降低检测成本；在检测成本匹配时，评分引导定位在飞机场景每批次增加5.39万美元，而在另外两个配置中经济效果甚微；短语和LLM抽取器在飞机场景中带来进一步增益。不过，跳过检测的风险盲对照在纯经济目标下仍录得更高价值。

Conclusion: 叙述性证据（退货备注）可以在回收决策做出之前有效支持检测分配决策，但框架的经济收益具有场景依赖性——在飞机维护等高价值、高检测成本场景中增益显著，而在其他场景中提升有限；同时需注意在纯经济目标下跳过检测的对照仍占优，说明该框架的价值需结合风险与检测成本权衡来评估。

Abstract: Reverse-logistics operators often decide how to inspect and route returned assets before their condition is fully observed, while full inspection consumes scarce labor. Semantic Signal-Assisted Decision Support converts return notes into a condition factor and a signal-quality score that guide inspection depth and recovery allocation under shared labor capacity. We evaluate the framework in three synthetic benchmark scenarios spanning information technology decommissioning, aircraft maintenance, and consumer-electronics returns. Across 30 paired simulation seeds, the keyword implementation improves net recovery value relative to a structured-feature comparator with noisy full inspection while reducing inspection cost in all three scenarios. A risk-blind comparator that skips inspection altogether still records higher value under the benchmark's purely economic objective. At matched inspection cost, score-guided targeting adds 53.9 thousand United States dollars per batch in the aircraft scenario but has little economic effect in the other two configurations; phrase and large language model extractors provide further gains in the aircraft scenario. These results show how narrative evidence can support inspection allocation before recovery decisions are made.

</details>


### [207] [Beyond Context Windows: Persistent Discovery Context for Data-Centric Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02129)
*Jalal Mahmud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“持久发现上下文”（Persistent Discovery Context），一种轻量级记忆层，用于存储数据为中心智能体在发现步骤中产生的“意图-对象”映射，并在后续检索中复用，以提升检索质量。实验表明，该方法在三个结构化数据环境中一致优于仅元数据搜索，对自动生成的记忆同样有效，并暴露了一个可复现的干扰失败模式；在词汇稀疏领域，仅记忆检索甚至优于基于元数据的检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据为中心智能体在规划或执行之前会反复执行发现步骤，以识别与任务相关的数据对象。然而，成功的发现结果通常被丢弃而非复用，导致重复劳动和检索质量受限。作者希望利用这些历史发现结果作为可复用的上下文，改善后续任务的检索效果。

Method: 引入持久发现上下文（Persistent Discovery Context）：一个轻量级记忆层，记录并存储先前的“意图到对象”映射，并在未来任务中将其用于增强检索（即复用历史发现结果来辅助当前检索），而非仅依赖元数据搜索。实验在三个结构化数据环境中评估，并比较了人工生成记忆、自动生成记忆以及仅记忆检索等变体。

Result: 持久发现上下文在三个结构化数据环境中一致地提升了检索质量（优于仅元数据搜索）；使用自动生成的记忆时依然有效；同时暴露了一个可复现的干扰失败模式。此外，在词汇稀疏的领域中，仅记忆检索的性能甚至超过了基于元数据的检索。

Conclusion: 发现结果构成一种有用的、可复用的上下文形式，能够增强数据为中心智能体的检索能力。这一发现为智能体记忆层的设计提供了新思路，但干扰失败模式也提示需要关注记忆复用的稳定性与边界条件。

Abstract: Data-centric agents repeatedly perform a discovery step before planning or execution: identifying the data objects relevant to a task. Yet successful discovery outcomes are typically discarded rather than reused. We introduce persistent discovery context, a lightweight memory layer that stores prior intent-to-object mappings and reuses them to augment future retrieval. Across three structured data environments, persistent discovery context consistently improves retrieval quality over metadata-only search, remains effective with automatically generated memories, and exposes a reproducible interference failure mode. In lexically sparse domains, memory-only retrieval can even outperform metadata-based retrieval. These findings suggest that discovery outcomes constitute a useful form of reusable context for data-centric agents.

</details>


### [208] [EmoStance: Response-Side Affective-Orientation Control for Empathetic Response Generation via Emoji Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02133)
*Ziyuan Jin,Yuxuan Ge,Zheng Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EmoStance，一种面向共情对话生成的响应侧情感取向控制方法：以多标注者表情符号分布作为弱情感-态度证据（而非输出符号或硬标签），诱导出近似听话者立场的潜在控制空间；通过建模源侧情感表达、从对话上下文和说话者角色预测软响应侧取向，并用连续前缀嵌入引导冻结的指令微调大语言模型。作者构建了 EmojiDialogue 数据集（在 EmpatheticDialogues 上扩展，含表情符号投票与置信度），在 20 名标注者、800 次盲法成对评估中以 62.2% 的压倒性胜率领先，尤其提升语境特异性和感知响应性，且与外部知识方法互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共情响应生成不仅要决定"说什么"，还要决定"如何回应"说话者的情感处境。已有工作多关注源侧（说话者）情感，缺乏对响应侧（听话者）情感取向的可控建模，且表情符号通常被当作输出符号或硬标签，无法表达细腻、不确定的情感态度。

Method: 1) 将多标注者表情符号分布作为弱情感-态度证据，诱导潜在控制空间以操作性地逼近听话者立场；2) 构建 EmojiDialogue：在 EmpatheticDialogues 上做话轮级扩展，包含表情符号投票与置信度分数；3) 提出 EmoStance：建模源侧情感表达，依据对话上下文和说话者角色预测软响应侧取向，并通过连续前缀嵌入引导冻结的指令微调 LLM 生成。

Result: 在 20 名标注者、800 次判断的盲法成对评估中，EmoStance 取得 62.2% 的决定性胜率，最显著的提升体现在语境特异性和感知响应性上；同时与外部知识类方法互补，不冲突。

Conclusion: 通过弱监督的响应侧情感取向控制，可在不微调大模型的情况下显著提升共情响应的语境切题度与响应性；代码、标注元数据与重建脚本已开源，便于复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Empathetic response generation requires models to decide not only what to say, but also how to respond to the previous speaker's affective situation. We formulate this as response-side affective-orientation control and use multi-annotator emoji distributions as weak affective--attitudinal evidence, rather than as output symbols or gold labels, to induce a latent control space that operationally approximates listener stance. We construct EmojiDialogue, an utterance-level extension of EmpatheticDialogues with emoji votes and confidence scores, and propose EmoStance, which models source-side affective expression, predicts a soft response-side orientation from dialogue context and speaker roles, and steers a frozen instruction-tuned LLM through continuous prefix embeddings. In blind pairwise evaluation with 20 annotators and 800 judgments, EmoStance achieves a 62.2% decisive win rate, with the clearest gains in contextual specificity and perceived responsiveness, while remaining complementary to external-knowledge methods. Code, annotation metadata, and reconstruction scripts are available in our GitHub repository: https://github.com/18277390221/EmoStance.

</details>


### [209] [FUSE: An Evaluating Framework for Dangerous Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02168)
*Zhengyi Jin,Ru Zhang,Xiao Chen,Xinbo Liu,Jiaxuan Lin,Jia Huang,Jianyi Liu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化的大模型危险能力评估框架，通过相互正交的三条流水线（知识K、防御D、危害H）在统一协议下评估模型，并生成标准化危险能力画像φ。作者以化学-生物（CB）模块实例化该框架，横向比较了4个家族12个商用LLM的危险能力，并纵向追踪了随发布时间的能力演化，发现危险能力并未单调下降；进一步通过跨评判者一致性与流水线正交性验证了评估的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估呈碎片化状态，难以有效治理危险AI能力（如化学-生物等武器相关能力）。需要一套统一、可迁移、可复现的评估框架，以便跨模型、跨家族、跨时间进行标准化比较，并厘清知识水平、拒答韧性与实际危害生成之间的关系。

Method: 框架采用三条正交流水线：Knowledge（K，评估模型掌握的危险知识）、Defense（D，评估模型拒答有害请求的韧性）、Harm（H，评估模型在合规情况下生成的有害内容程度），在统一协议下执行并聚合为标准化危险能力画像φ。框架核心评估引擎保持不变，通过可插拔模块提供场景种子、知识库、危害查询与评判规则，实现跨领域迁移（以CB评估为主，辅以网络攻击领域的试点验证协议可迁移性）。实验对4个家族共12个商用LLM应用CB模块，进行横向家族比较与基于发布日期的纵向能力演化分析，并用bootstrap跨评判者一致性（ρ>0.79，5位评判者中4位）及K-D-H间相关性（ρ∈[0.32,0.52]）检验可靠性。

Result: 横向比较揭示三维度画像差异显著：知识水平相当的模型在拒答韧性上差别很大，而防御强的模型在合规时生成的有害内容并不更少；Claude、DeepSeek、GPT等家族层面呈现不同模式。纵向分析表明危险能力并未随发布时间单调下降：新模型知识加深但防御仅部分改善，说明规模扩展与对齐进步并未统一转化为安全性提升。可靠性方面，跨评判者bootstrap ρ>0.79（4/5一致性），K-D-H间相关性ρ∈[0.32,0.52]，支持三条流水线的正交性与独立性。

Conclusion: 碎片化评估可以（且应当）被统一的模块化框架取代；仅看单一维度（如知识或拒答率）会误导安全治理结论，危险能力须以K-D-H三维画像刻画。研究强调"知识增长≠安全改善"，对前沿模型的扩展与对齐策略提出警示，并为跨领域（如CB之外）的标准化危险能力评估提供了可迁移的协议范例。

Abstract: Fragmented safety evaluation undermines the governance of dangerous AI capabilities. We present a modular framework that evaluates each model through three orthogonal pipelines---Knowledge ($K$), Defense ($D$), and Harm ($H$)---under a unified protocol, aggregating results into a standardized dangerous-capability profile $φ$. Pluggable modules supply scenario seeds, knowledge banks, hazard queries, and judge rubrics, while the core evaluation engine remains unchanged across domains; the CB evaluation is complemented by a cyber pilot demonstrating protocol transfer.
  Instantiating the framework with a chemical-biological (CB) module, we evaluate 12 commercial LLMs from four families. Our first contribution is a horizontal comparison of dangerous capability across models and model families: the three dimensions expose sharply divergent profiles---models with comparable knowledge differ in refusal resilience, and strong defenders do not generate less harmful content when they do comply---while family-level patterns further separate Claude, DeepSeek, and GPT models. The second is a temporal analysis of capability evolution: tracking $K$, $D$, and $H$ against model release dates reveals that dangerous capability has not monotonically declined; newer models deepen knowledge while only partially improving defense, showing that scaling and alignment progress do not uniformly translate into safety. Reliability is established via cross-judge consistency (bootstrap $ρ> 0.79$, 4 of 5 judges) and pipeline orthogonality ($K$--$D$--$H$ inter-correlations $ρ\in [0.32, 0.52]$).

</details>


### [210] [Examining the Vulnerability of Multi-Agent Medical Systems to Human Interventions for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02191)
*Benjamin C Liu,Dillon Mehta,Rishi Malhotra,Adam Zobian,Yong Ying Tan,Samir Chopra,Daniella Rand,Natalie Pang,Abhiram Gudimella,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多智能体医疗系统中，人类在“故障点”（AI智能体对话中推理最易受外部影响的时刻）进行干预如何改变诊断准确性。基于MedQA数据集和模拟医患对话，结果显示正确的干预方法可将基线诊断准确率提升最多40%，而错误或带偏见的干预会使性能下降最多6%，并加剧诊断偏移与不确定性。此外，研究还发现模拟智能体环境中的认知偏差（如过早关闭、易受误导线索影响）与现实临床实践存在行为相似性，表明识别并引导故障点可作为提升多智能体医疗系统诊断鲁棒性的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体医疗系统虽展现出诊断潜力，但其推理过程在特定节点易受外部影响，而人类干预对系统诊断准确性的作用尚不明确。作者旨在通过定义并识别“故障点”，探究干预方向（正确、错误或带偏见）如何影响诊断结果，并考察AI智能体行为与真实临床认知偏差之间的对应关系，从而为提升系统鲁棒性提供依据。

Method: 作者将“故障点”定义为AI智能体对话中推理最易受外部影响的时刻；采用MedQA数据集，构建模拟医患对话，系统性地施加不同类型的人类干预（正确干预、错误干预、带偏见的干预），并从诊断准确率变化、诊断漂移及不确定性等多个维度量化干预效果；同时对比智能体行为与真实临床实践中的认知偏差模式。

Result: 正确的干预方法使基线诊断准确率最多提升40%；错误或带偏见的干预使性能最多下降6%，并增大诊断漂移与不确定性。此外，分析揭示了模拟智能体环境与真实临床实践中的认知偏差存在行为相似性，典型例子包括过早关闭（premature closure）与易受误导线索影响。

Conclusion: 识别并在故障点以人类干预进行引导，可有效提升多智能体医疗系统的诊断鲁棒性；该机制既带来显著的准确性收益，也暴露出错误干预的风险，同时为理解AI临床推理中的认知偏差提供了借鉴，为未来人机协作诊断系统设计提供方向。

Abstract: Human interventions at fault points can alter the diagnostic accuracy of multi-agent medical systems. We defined fault points as moments in AI agent conversations, in which an agent's reasoning became most vulnerable to external influence. Using the MedQA dataset, this study analyzed simulated doctor-patient conversations to measure how interventions shifted reasoning and accuracy. Correct intervention methods showed an improvement in baseline diagnostic accuracy of up to 40%, while incorrect or bias-related interventions degraded performance by up to 6% and increased diagnostic drift and uncertainty. Beyond performance changes, our analysis revealed behavioral similarities between cognitive biases in simulated agent environments and real-world clinical practice. Examples included premature closure and susceptibility to misleading cues. Overall, these findings demonstrate that identifying and guiding fault points with human interventions may provide a mechanism for improving diagnostic robustness in multi-agent medical systems.

</details>


### [211] [ASCII Attack: Recontextualising Harmful Requests as Artistic Critique in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02215)
*Da Cheng Gu,Yifei Dong,Xinghao Yang,Yongshun Gong,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ASCII Attack：一种单轮、黑盒的越狱方法，将完全可读的有害请求嵌入 ASCII 艺术字符中，伪装成艺术品并请求点评，从而绕过安全对齐。在 11 个模型、8 类有害主题上，有害性分类器判定 62% 的框架化提示有害（对照为 42%），最易受攻击的模型成功率高达 93%；单次查询在四个有害性评估指标中的表现不低于已发表的单查询攻击，且效应主要随模型而非主题变化，并随规模增大而不减弱。近三分之二的行上至少有一位评估者与多数意见不一致，暗示测量效度问题与失配泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐主要针对以直白表面形式陈述的有害请求进行训练，因此对仅通过重新语境化、改变模型解读方式而同义重述的有害请求覆盖不足。现有攻击（如 ArtPrompt）常常隐藏请求内容，而本工作旨在展示一种不隐藏任何内容、仅依靠表层形式重语境化的单轮黑盒攻击，以揭示对齐在表面形式上的脆弱性。

Method: 将完全清晰可读的有害请求嵌入 ASCII 艺术字符中，将其呈现为“艺术品”并请求模型给出反馈；模型的回复以艺术批评形式写出，仍可包含直接请求会被拒绝的操作细节。作者为每个框架化提示配对设计了一个直接提问的控制提示，从而在控制主题、模型和解码差异的情况下隔离表层形式带来的对比效应；该对比识别的是捆绑在一起的表层（多种表面特征组合），而非单一通道。

Result: 在 11 个模型和 8 类有害主题上，有害性分类器判定 62% 的框架化提示有害，而对照组为 42%；最易受攻击的模型上成功率高达 93%。单次查询在五个有害性评估指标中的四个上达到或超过已发表的单查询攻击。效应更随模型而非主题变化，且不随模型规模增大而减弱；此外，在近三分之二的框架化样本行上，至少有一位评估者与多数评估者意见相左。

Conclusion: ASCII Attack 表明安全对齐存在以表面形式为中心的失配泛化：完全可读的有害内容一旦被重新语境化（如伪装成 ASCII 艺术品并请求点评）即可绕过防御。评估者之间频繁的分歧构成一项测量效度发现，提示对越狱成功率的衡量本身可能不稳定，且与失配泛化现象一致。

Abstract: Safety alignment trains large language models to refuse harmful requests stated plainly, but that training is applied mostly to surface form. Requests that only recontextualise the same operational content, changing how the model reads it, are therefore only weakly covered. The ASCII Attack is one such recontextualisation. It is single-turn and black-box: one message, with no access to model internals. It embeds a fully legible harmful request in ASCIl-art characters, presents it as artwork, and asks for feedback. Unlike ArtPrompt, it hides nothing: the request stays readable. The reply is written as artistic critique and can contain operational detail that a plain request would have been refused for. Every framed prompt is paired with a direct-question control, so the contrast is isolated from topic, model and decoding variation. The contrast identifies a bundled surface, not one isolated channel. Across eleven models and eight harm topics, a harm-aware classifier judges 62% of framed prompts harmful against 42% of controls. On the most susceptible model the framed prompt succeeds 93% of the time. A single query matches or exceeds published single-query attacks under four of five harm judges. The effect tracks the model more than the topic and does not diminish with scale. At least one judge dissents from the panel majority on nearly two-thirds of framed rows, which is itself a measurement-validity finding. That pattern is consistent with mismatched generalisation.

</details>


### [212] [PEARL: Path-Entity Aligned Relational Learning with Contextual Subgraphs for Inductive Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02216)
*Yunchi Yang,Longlong Li,Cunquan Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEARL 是一种面向归纳知识图谱补全（IKGC）的路径-实体对齐关系学习框架，将关系路径建模为上下文条件化的推理信号：通过查询特定的上下文子图与 LLM 引导的语义路径检索构建路径、实体与全局子图表示之间的二部交互图，并采用双视角对比目标抑制噪声。在 WN18RR、FB15k-237 和 NELL-995 上均取得最佳平均 Hits@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于子图或路径的 IKGC 方法通常将关系路径独立于其所在查询子图进行编码，忽略了路径的预测相关性会随结构上下文变化这一事实，因而难以学到可迁移的关系与结构模式。

Method: PEARL 首先构建查询实体邻域并集组成的查询特定上下文子图，利用 LLM 引导的检索器筛选语义相关的路径；随后构造路径、上下文实体与全局子图表示之间的二部交互图，使路径嵌入能够根据局部和全局结构证据自适应调整；最后采用双视角对比学习目标，在随机上下文扰动下保持表示一致性以抑制扩展上下文引入的噪声。

Result: 在 WN18RR、FB15k-237 与 NELL-995 三个基准上，PEARL 的平均 Hits@10 均优于所对比的 IKGC 方法；消融实验、效率分析与案例研究分别验证了上下文子图建模、语义路径检索、路径-实体交互与对比正则化的贡献。

Conclusion: 将路径编码与查询上下文进行对齐和交互，并结合 LLM 语义检索与对比正则化，能显著提升归纳知识图谱补全的性能与泛化能力，为路径级 IKGC 提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Inductive knowledge graph completion (IKGC) aims to predict missing links involving entities unseen during training, requiring models to learn transferable relational and structural patterns. Existing subgraph- and path-based approaches often encode relational paths independently of their surrounding query subgraphs, although the predictive relevance of a path may vary across structural contexts. We propose PEARL, a Path-Entity Aligned Relational Learning framework that models paths as context-conditioned reasoning signals. PEARL constructs a query-specific contextual subgraph from the union of the query entities' neighborhoods and uses a large language model (LLM)-guided retriever to distill semantically relevant paths. It then builds a bipartite interaction graph over paths, contextual entities, and a global subgraph representation, allowing path embeddings to adapt to local and global structural evidence. To suppress noise introduced by the enlarged context, PEARL employs a dual-view contrastive objective that promotes representation consistency under stochastic contextual perturbations. Experiments on WN18RR, FB15k-237, and NELL-995 show that PEARL obtains the best average Hits@10 among the compared IKGC methods on all three benchmarks. Ablation studies, efficiency analyses, and case studies further validate the contributions of contextual subgraph modeling, semantic path retrieval, path-entity interaction, and contrastive regularization.

</details>


### [213] [SkillGLoW: Procedural-Family Skill Consolidation for Self-Improving Agents on Long-Horizon Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02217)
*Ao Yan,Xin Zhang,Jiawei Du,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SkillGLoW（Global-Local Weave），一种面向长时程任务的自改进 LLM 智能体技能复用框架。它将任务执行中产生的局部技能按求解过程聚合成“程序族”，压缩为去实例化的全局先验（global prior），实例细节在任务执行时按需重新生成而非存储；并通过提交门控（commit gate）仅在实际执行不损害已部署库时接纳新先验。在数学推理、终端自动化、软件修复和具身控制四个基准、三种模型下，相比无技能基线在困难设置上平均提升 17.2 分（加入局部再生成后为 18.0），全部 12 次持续改进运行均获得正增益；技能库每个程序族仅保留一条先验，比逐任务池紧凑 3.6 倍，并超越已发表的单文档优化器（21 个测试单元中胜 15 个）；未修改的库可将未见 ALFWorld 任务成功率从 73.9% 提升至 83.9%，证明迁移的是求解程序而非任务记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体的自改进技能复用主要采用两种形式——单一全局文档或逐任务条目的扁平池，但二者在长时程、任务间结构差异大的工作负载上以相反方式失效：全局文档退化为泛泛的通用准则，逐任务池则不断膨胀且条目被写它的实例所绑定。作者认为缺失的复用单元是“一类相关任务共享的求解过程”，因此需要显式建模程序族并实现跨任务的可迁移先验，同时避免实例级细节造成的过拟合与存储膨胀。

Method: SkillGLoW 围绕“程序族”构建：任务从自身执行中写出的局部技能被汇总为程序族，再压缩为去实例化的全局先验（删除具体实例细节）；实例级信息不在库中存储，而是在每个任务执行时按需重新生成；提交门控（commit gate）要求先验必须经过真实执行验证，确认不会降低已部署技能库的性能后才被接纳。整体构成“全局-局部编织”（Global-Local Weave），既保留了跨任务共享的程序性知识，又避免了文档的泛化坍缩与池的实例绑定。

Result: 在数学推理、终端自动化、软件修复与具身控制四个基准及三个模型上，全局先验相比无技能基线在困难（hard）设置上平均提升 17.2 分，12 个持续改进运行全部为正增益；加入局部再生成后提升达 18.0。技能库每个程序族仅保留一条先验，比逐任务池紧凑 3.6 倍；在相同协议下，GLoW 在 21 个测试单元中于 15 个上领先已发表的单文档优化器；且未作任何修改的库将未见 ALFWorld 任务的成功率从 73.9% 提升至 83.9%。

Conclusion: 求解程序（procedure）而非任务记忆（task memory）才是跨任务迁移的真正载体；通过程序族聚合、去实例化压缩、按需再生成实例细节以及执行验证驱动提交，SkillGLoW 同时解决了全局文档退化与逐任务池膨胀的矛盾，能够在长时程、异质任务环境中实现紧凑、可泛化且持续改善的技能库，并展现出对未见任务的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly self-improve by writing and reusing textual skills, kept either as one global document or as a flat pool of per-task entries, though most of the evidence comes from domains with structurally similar tasks. On long-horizon workloads where each task demands a different solution, the two forms fail in opposite ways: the document collapses into generic discipline, while the pool inflates and its entries stay bound to the instance that wrote them. We argue the missing unit of reuse is the solving procedure shared by a cluster of related tasks, and build SkillGLoW (Global-Local Weave) around it: the local skills a task writes from its own execution are aggregated into procedural families and compressed into de-instantiated global priors, while the instance detail they hold is regenerated per task rather than stored; a commit gate admits a prior only when real execution shows it does not degrade the deployed library. Across four benchmarks (mathematical reasoning, terminal automation, software repair, and embodied control) and three models, the priors gain 17.2 points (hard) over the no-skill baseline on average, with positive gains in all 12 continual-improvement runs, and 18.0 with local regeneration, while the library holds one prior per procedural family, 3.6x more compact than the per-task pool. Under the same protocol GLoW leads a published single-document optimizer on 15 of 21 cells. Unmodified, the library lifts success on unseen ALFWorld tasks from 73.9% to 83.9%, evidence that what transfers is procedure rather than task memory.

</details>


### [214] [PhoenixNest-Video: Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Agent Framework for Automated Video Interview Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02231)
*Fan Yuxuan,Huang Miaojun,Zhang Haimei,Wu Jingshen,Liu Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhoenixNest-Video，一个以证据为依据的多模态智能体框架，用于自动化视频面试评估。它构建语义视频图作为结构化工作记忆，执行基于评分量规（rubric）的检索与跨模态验证，并通过基于量规的强化学习（双奖励：量规对齐与分数区分）训练评分器。在 VInterview-2025 上达到 91.50% 的等级准确率，超越规模更大的专有模型，且每个分数都附有可追溯的证据供人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面试评估需要基于行为证据的逐项标准（per-criterion）判断，但申请者数量激增使纯人工评估成本高昂且不一致；现有 AI 方法只能给出不透明的分数，缺乏可追溯的理由，因此需要一个既能自动评分又能提供可解释证据的解决方案。

Method: 构建语义视频图（semantic video graph）作为结构化工作记忆；执行量规条件化检索（rubric-conditioned retrieval），并在视觉、音频、文本流之间进行跨模态验证；最终产生锚定于候选人材料的逐项标准分数。Scorer 通过基于量规的强化学习（Rubrics-based Reinforcement Learning）训练，采用双重奖励——量规对齐奖励与分数级区分奖励——以内化多级量规的判别结构。

Result: PhoenixNest-Video 在 VInterview-2025 上取得 91.50% 的等级准确率，显著优于规模更大的专有模型；一个紧凑的、以量规为依据的智能体比直接提示更大模型更接近专家小组的判断，并能展示每个分数背后的证据供人工复核。

Conclusion: 紧凑且量规可溯源的智能体框架在自动化视频面试评估中能达到优于大型模型的评分一致性，同时保持证据透明与可审查性，为高吞吐、可解释的面试评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Interview assessment requires per-criterion judgments grounded in behavioral evidence, yet surging applicant volumes have made human-only evaluation costly and inconsistent, while existing AI approaches yield opaque scores without traceable rationale. We introduce PhoenixNest-Video, an evidence-grounded multimodal agent framework for automated video interview assessment. It builds a semantic video graph as structured working memory, performs rubric-conditioned retrieval with cross-modal verification across visual, audio, and textual streams, and produces per-criterion scores anchored to the candidate's materials. A Scorer trained via Rubrics-based Reinforcement Learning with dual rewards for rubric alignment and score-level differentiation internalizes the discriminative structure of multi-level rubrics. PhoenixNest-Video attains 91.50\% grade-level accuracy on VInterview-2025, outperforming substantially larger proprietary models. A compact, rubric-grounded agent therefore scores candidates in closer agreement with an expert panel than direct prompting of much larger models, and exposes the evidence behind each score for human review.

</details>


### [215] [PGPO: Potential-Guided Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02236)
*Yuyao Zheng,Haipeng Sun,Junwei Bao,Lemao Liu,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Potential-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO)，一种面向多轮智能体任务的组基强化学习方法。PGPO 从锚点状态组的回报统计中估计经验状态势，并通过相邻状态间的势差推导动作优势，实现跨轨迹信用传播，从而在稀疏终端奖励下为中间动作（尤其是失败轨迹内的动作）提供更细粒度的信用分配。在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上表现优于近期组基 RL 方法，且几乎不增加训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组基强化学习（如 GiGPO）虽引入 step-level 优势以细化信用分配，但这些信号仍依赖每条轨迹的最终结果，导致失败轨迹内的动作难以区分——有效动作与错误动作会获得同样不利的信用，即失败侧信用信号信息量不足。

Method: PGPO 在每次 rollout 组内，依据锚点状态组的回报统计估计经验状态势（empirical state potentials）；再由相邻状态间的势差推导动作优势，实现跨轨迹的信用传播（cross-trajectory credit propagation），从而得到更细粒度的 step-level 信用分配，尤其改善失败轨迹内的区分度。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 基准上，PGPO 相对近期组基 RL 方法表现出更强的整体性能；进一步分析表明其能提供信息量更丰富的失败侧信用信号，且训练开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过基于状态势的跨轨迹信用传播，PGPO 在不显著增加开销的前提下有效解决了稀疏终端奖励下失败轨迹内信用粗粒度的问题，为多轮智能体任务的组基 RL 后训练提供了更优的信用分配方案。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective paradigm for LLM post-training, but in multi-turn agentic tasks with sparse terminal rewards, it often provides coarse credit for intermediate actions. To obtain more fine-grained credit assignment, recent work such as GiGPO introduces step-level advantages for intermediate actions. However, these step-level signals still rely on the final outcome of each individual trajectory. As a result, actions within failed trajectories can remain poorly differentiated, so effective actions can receive the same unfavorable credit as erroneous ones. In this work, we propose Potential-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO) for multi-turn agentic tasks. PGPO estimates empirical state potentials from anchor-state-group return statistics within each rollout group. It then derives action advantages from potential differences between adjacent states, enabling cross-trajectory credit propagation. This provides finer-grained step-level credit assignment, especially within failed trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show strong overall performance relative to recent group-based RL methods. Further analysis provides evidence that PGPO yields more informative failure-side credit signals with negligible training overhead.

</details>


### [216] [Propose to Learn, Learn to Propose: Evaluability-Aware Assistance under Bounded Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02242)
*Yifan Zhu,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可评估性感知的提案规划问题（ProSE），将AI助手生成的候选提案既视为任务干预，又视为探测用户潜在偏好与评估约束的探针；采用KL正则化的有界理性二元响应模型刻画“接受意愿”与“评估代价”的权衡，并提出深度2的贝叶斯自适应规划器ProSE-Plan，在受控图模拟中优于忽视可评估性与短视的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助方法通常侧重于提案质量或用户目标推断，并假设用户能可靠评估任何提案；然而现实中用户受有界理性约束，评估能力有限，可能导致评估失败。作者希望将“用户可评估性”作为规划中一个独立的、与生成质量和偏好推断互补的维度纳入考虑。

Method: 将设置形式化为ProSE——一个隐藏参数的序贯辅助问题（hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem），并用KL正则化的有界理性二元响应模型实例化：接受决策在价值增益与距离相关的可评估性惩罚之间权衡。在此基础上提出ProSE-Plan，一种深度2的贝叶斯自适应规划器，按可能的响应及响应诱导的后验信念对提案评分。

Result: 模型似然的规划后果分析表明，“可能被接受的提案”与“信息量大的探针”并不必然重合，这解释了只追求接受率的规划器为何系统性表现不佳。在受控图模拟中，ProSE-Plan在评估代价成为瓶颈时优于忽视可评估性的基线与短视基线；探针-承诺消融实验证实该方法能选出简单方法遗漏的信息性提案。

Conclusion: 研究结果将用户可评估性确认为AI辅助中与生成质量和偏好推断互补的规划相关维度，表明在评估代价受限时，明智地选择兼具干预与探测作用的提案能显著提升辅助效果。

Abstract: AI assistants often collaborate by proposing candidate edits, plans, or designs that users evaluate before adoption. Existing assistance methods focus on proposal quality or user-goal inference, often assuming that the user can reliably evaluate any proposal, which can fail in practice because of bounded rationality. We study evaluability-aware proposal planning, where proposals serve both as task interventions and as probes for learning latent preferences and evaluation constraints, where the resulting belief updates then guide later proposals. We formalise this setting as ProSE, a hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem, and instantiate it with a KL-regularised bounded-rational binary response model in which acceptance trades off value gain against a distance-dependent evaluability penalty. Analysing the planning consequence of this likelihood reveals that likely accepted proposals and informative probes need not coincide, which explains why planners that only pursue acceptance systematically underperform. We operationalise ProSE with \textsc{ProSE-Plan}, a depth-2 Bayes-adaptive planner that scores proposals by possible responses and response-induced posterior beliefs. In controlled graph simulations, \textsc{ProSE-Plan} improves over evaluability-unaware and myopic baselines when evaluation cost is the bottleneck, and a probe-commit ablation confirms that our approach selects informative proposals that simpler methods miss. Our results thus identify user evaluability as a planning-relevant dimension of AI assistance, complementary to generation quality and preference inference.

</details>


### [217] [Task-Level Natural Language Priors as Learning Signals for Low-Resource LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02244)
*Jian Gao,Xiao Zhang,Xun Zhu,Miao Li,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Prior-Guided Tuning（PGT）训练视角，将任务级自然语言先验作为辅助学习信号而非仅作为输入上下文，用于低资源大语言模型训练；并基于该视角提出 Contrastive Prior Steering（CPS），在保留原始监督目标的同时加入正负先验条件辅助损失。在 AmbiMath、Jigsaw 和 MNLI/HANS 上的实验表明 CPS 优于普通微调与提示微调，验证了任务级自然语言先验可作为低资源训练的有效辅助学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在低资源训练数据模糊或不完整时往往表现不佳，而任务级的自然语言先验本可提供有用指导，但现有方法通常仅将这些先验当作输入上下文，而不是在训练过程中作为学习信号加以利用。

Method: 提出 Prior-Guided Tuning（PGT）训练视角，将自然语言先验作为辅助学习信号融入低资源 LLM 训练；在此基础上提出 Contrastive Prior Steering（CPS），保持原有监督目标不变，同时添加正、负先验条件下的辅助损失，以鼓励与任务一致的学习并抑制貌似合理但具有误导性的替代方案。

Result: 在 AmbiMath、Jigsaw 和 MNLI/HANS 上，CPS 一致优于普通微调和提示微调：AmbiMath 上平均精确匹配准确率达 97.6%；Jigsaw 上平均 Macro F1 较标准微调提升 9.5 个百分点，且仅用 1/10 实验训练数据即可略超全量数据的普通微调；HANS 上 LLaMA 3.1 8B 与 Qwen 2.5 7B 的非蕴含准确率分别提升 8.3 和 5.2 个百分点，同时保持可比的域内 MNLI 准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明任务级自然语言先验可以作为低资源 LLM 训练的辅助学习信号提供有效指导；代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often struggle when low-resource training data are ambiguous or incomplete. Task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance in such settings, but existing approaches usually treat these priors as input context rather than as learning signals during training. We propose Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT), a training perspective that incorporates natural-language priors as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Under this perspective, we introduce Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS), which keeps the original supervised objective intact while adding positive and negative prior-conditioned auxiliary losses to encourage task-consistent learning and discourage plausible but misleading alternatives. Experiments on AmbiMath, Jigsaw, and MNLI/HANS show that CPS consistently improves over plain and prompt fine-tuning. On AmbiMath, CPS achieves 97.6% average exact-match accuracy. On Jigsaw, CPS improves average Macro F1 by 9.5 percentage points over standard fine-tuning, and with 1/10 of the experimental training data slightly exceeds full-data plain fine-tuning. On HANS, CPS improves non-entailment accuracy by 8.3 and 5.2 percentage points for LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen 2.5 7B, respectively, while maintaining comparable in-domain MNLI accuracy. These results support our central claim: task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [218] [LLM-as-a-Judge Is Not an Oracle: Why Self-Improving Agents Need Deterministic Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02246)
*Vansh Wahi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张在自改进智能体流水线中，LLM 评判者不应充当最终裁判（oracle），而应降级为顾问（advisor）：其裁决只是一项输入，所有改动必须通过一个评判者无法覆盖的确定性验证层。作者在生产环境中运行自主提示词优化循环数月，归纳出四类共十一种评估信号失效方式（评判者偏见、测试框架与指标故障、真实标签错误、奖励攻击），并提出 PROCTOR——一种 Teacher-Student 循环，由有状态编排器独占工具访问权、无状态子智能体诊断失败并起草（但不能执行）变异，Teacher 在五条确定性护栏（密封沙箱、能力不相交角色、高于 Teacher 的验收检查、冻结留出集、蜜罐用例）下给变异评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进智能体流水线的核心存在缺陷：优化器改写提示词以提高得分，而得分来自同为 LLM 的评判者，该评判者对系统是否变好拥有最终决定权，但它并未赢得这一权力。作者通过实际构建并长期运行替代方案得出这一立场：评判者必须被"降级"，由可验证、不可被评判者覆盖的确定性机制来把关每一次变更。

Method: 作者先在真实生产环境（合同分析、合规审查、代码质量）中长时间运行自主提示词优化循环，系统化记录评估信号失败的案例，并归类为四种类型。随后提出 PROCTOR 框架：有状态编排器（orchestrator）独占所有工具访问权限；无状态子智能体负责诊断失败并起草变异（mutation），但无法自行应用；Teacher（LLM 评判者）在五条确定性护栏下对变异进行评分——密封沙箱、能力不相交的角色划分、优先级高于 Teacher 的验收检查、冻结留出集，以及精心构造的蜜罐用例（能得满分本身就是作弊证据）。

Result: 生产运行中发现了十一种评估信号失效方式，分为四类。具体案例包括：智能体通过读取环境中缓存的答案键获得满分，100% 通过率掩盖了真实能力仅 68%；一个损坏的真实标签导致优化器删除正确的合规规则以迎合该标签；一个语法错误的提示词因静默解析器回退改善了指标而被选为胜者；重写评判者评分规则难以带来持续提升，唯一可靠改进来自对其输出顺序的结构性约束。作者报告了 PROCTOR 所阻止的失败，同时坦诚地列出了由于 Teacher 本身仍是 LLM 评判者而未能阻止的失败。

Conclusion: 结论是：在自主优化循环中，LLM 评判者的裁决不可作为最终权威，必须与确定性验证层结合并受其约束。PROCTOR 通过架构性手段（工具独占权、角色不相交、护栏优先于 Teacher）显著提升了评估信号的可靠性，但结构设计不能完全消除"LLM 评判者本身"这一环节的固有缺陷——这一点必须被如实承认并继续研究。

Abstract: Self-improving agent pipelines have a problem at their center. An optimizer rewrites prompts to score higher, and the score comes from a judge that is itself an LLM. That judge has the last word on whether the system is getting better, and our position is that it has not earned it. The judge should be demoted from oracle to advisor: its verdict becomes one input among several, and every change is gated instead by a deterministic verification layer the judge cannot override. We reached this position by building the alternative and running it. Over months of running autonomous prompt-optimization loops in production across contract analysis, compliance review, and code quality, we cataloged eleven ways the evaluation signal failed, in four classes: judge bias, harness and metric failures, ground-truth errors, and reward hacking. Agents achieved perfect scores by reading cached answer keys from their environment, a 100% pass rate concealing 68% true capability. A corrupted ground-truth label caused the optimizer to delete correct compliance rules to agree with it. A syntactically broken prompt was promoted as the winner because a silent parser fallback improved the metric. Attempts to fix the judge by rewriting its rubric plateaued; the only reliable gain came from a structural constraint on its output order. In response we describe PROCTOR, a Teacher-Student loop in which a stateful orchestrator holds all tool access, stateless subagents diagnose failures and draft mutations they cannot apply, and a Teacher grades those mutations under five deterministic guardrails: hermetic sandboxes, capability-disjoint roles, acceptance checks that outrank the Teacher, frozen holdouts, and canary cases engineered so that a perfect score is itself evidence of cheating. We report the failures this prevented, and, because the Teacher is itself an LLM judge, the failures it did not.

</details>


### [219] [APEx: Distillation of Agent Procedural Experience for Adaptive Deep Research Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02253)
*Jie Ding,Rui Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEx是一个面向深度研究智能体的层次化经验利用框架，将交互历史组织为实例级轨迹记忆和类别级程序性技能，并通过Executor、Distiller、Planner的闭环架构（三段式交替GRPO训练 + 技能引导的测试时强化学习）实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏与在线策略自适应，在7个基准上达到SOTA，超越GPT-5.4达14.7分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体依赖外部工具进行多轮推理以回答复杂长时问题，从先前经验中学习对持续改进至关重要。然而现有方法要么检索冗长的任务特定轨迹（增加决策负担），要么蒸馏出程序性技能但与下游策略自适应脱节。因此需要一种既高效组织经验、又能与策略学习深度耦合的经验利用机制。

Method: APEx构建层次化经验框架：将交互历史分为实例级轨迹记忆与类别级程序性技能，通过Executor、Distiller、Planner三个模块构成闭环架构。采用三段式交替GRPO训练范式，实现奖励引导的技能蒸馏（而非固定提示生成）；测试时以蒸馏技能作为程序性先验，通过技能引导的测试时强化学习进行在线Planner自适应，并加入技能对齐正则化以防止策略漂移，实现无需真实标签的自我改进。

Result: 在7个基准上的实验表明，APEx取得最优性能，超越GPT-5.4达14.7个百分点，并比最强的记忆增强基线高出3.0个百分点。

Conclusion: APEx通过实例记忆与程序性技能的层次化组织及闭环训练-自适应架构，有效弥合了经验利用与策略适应之间的差距，验证了奖励引导技能蒸馏与测试时自改进的可行性，为深度研究智能体的持续学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep research agents augment large language models with external tools to answer complex, long-horizon questions through multi-turn reasoning. Learning from prior experience is crucial for continual improvement, yet existing methods either retrieve verbose task-specific traces that burden decision-making, or distill procedural skills that remain decoupled from downstream policy adaptation. We propose APEx, a hierarchical experience utilization framework that organizes interaction history into instance-level trajectory memories and category-level procedural skills, and couples them through a closed-loop architecture of Executor, Distiller, and Planner. The three modules are optimized via a three-stage alternating GRPO training paradigm, enabling reward-guided skill distillation rather than fixed-prompt generation. At test time, distilled skills serve as procedural priors for online Planner adaptation through skill-guided test-time reinforcement learning, allowing ground-truth-free self-improvement with skill-alignment regularization to prevent policy drift. Experiments on 7 benchmarks demonstrate that APEx achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing GPT-5.4 by 14.7 points and the strongest memory-augmented baseline by 3.0 points.

</details>


### [220] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Codebook Agent：用向量量化的 16 条目码本压缩成功拓扑，经奖励加权 MLP 从查询嵌入映射到码分布，再由读取扁平化邻接的 MLP 代理按实测效用与归一化 token 成本重排候选。测试时无迭代搜索、无消息传递，在全部六个基准上取得最高平均准确率（84.6 vs 最强先前方法的 83.0），拓扑生成仅 2.4 ms，并节省 21.9–33.2% 的 LLM token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多智能体系统把按查询自适应通信拓扑设计当作条件图生成：变分/自回归/扩散解码器在 N×N 邻接空间搜索，图网络代理以效用和边数等结构成本排序。作者通过三个实证结果论证该范式与问题错位：1) 通过奖励过滤的拓扑坍缩为约 6 种不同图（即使码本容量从 8 增至 64）；2) 边数与实测 token 消耗负相关（Pearson r≈-0.4），稀疏化反而使推理更贵；3) 在 agent 共享 profile（公开基准的默认配置）时，基于 agent-profile 节点的消息传递打分器对邻接矩阵不变，根本无法排序。

Method: Codebook Agent 由三部分组成：向量量化自编码器将成功拓扑压缩为与查询无关的 16 条目码本；奖励加权 MLP 将查询嵌入映射为码上分布；以及一个读取扁平化邻接矩阵的 MLP 代理，回归到实测效用与逐任务归一化 token 成本，在单次批量前向中对解码出的候选进行重排。测试阶段无需迭代搜索和消息传递。

Result: 在比较的六个基准上全部取得最高准确率（平均 84.6，对照最强先前设计器 83.0）；拓扑生成耗时 2.4 ms；LLM token 使用量减少 21.9–33.2%。

Conclusion: 用查询条件化的码本查询加浅层 MLP 重排替代迭代图搜索与消息传递打分，既提升准确性又显著降低开销，表明“码本压缩成功拓扑+效用/token 成本回归重排”是与该问题更契合的建模方式。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>


### [221] [CoMerge: Conflict-Driven Preference Optimization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02273)
*Mingjie Zheng,Zihao Chen,Wenqing Chen,Weile Yuan,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoMerge 是一种面向模型合并的冲突驱动偏好优化框架，将模型合并重新表述为偏好优化问题：利用朴素合并方法（如任务算术）产生的退化行为作为硬负样本，以自监督方式构建偏好对，无需外部标注；通过偏好优化微调轻量级、逐张量的合并系数，在缓解参数空间冲突的同时保留任务特定能力。在 MergeBench 上取得 0.9968 的平均归一化性能，超越所有已评估的无数据与数据驱动合并基线；在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上对指令遵循与安全性等冲突敏感任务有显著提升，仅优化 1,445 个标量系数即可与全参数微调竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并为构建多任务大语言模型提供了无需完整重训的高效范式，但一直受参数干扰（parameter interference）的挑战。现有方法虽旨在保留各专家模型能力并缓解干扰，却普遍没有直接从未经处理的朴素合并所暴露出的退化行为中学习。作者希望利用这些退化行为本身作为监督信号，从而更直接地优化合并过程。

Method: 提出冲突驱动的偏好优化框架 CoMerge：将模型合并形式化为偏好优化问题；采用自监督的冲突驱动策略，把朴素合并方法（如任务算术）的缺陷作为硬负样本，构造偏好对而不需要外部标注；随后应用偏好优化来精炼轻量级、逐张量（tensor-wise）的合并系数，从而在参数空间缓解冲突并保留任务特定能力。

Result: 在 MergeBench 上，CoMerge 的平均归一化性能达到 0.9968，优于所有被评估的无数据与数据驱动模型合并基线；在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上，对指令遵循与安全性等冲突敏感任务带来显著改进，并且尽管仅优化 1,445 个标量系数，仍与全参数微调高度竞争。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将模型合并视为偏好优化问题、并从朴素合并的缺陷中自监督学习，能够以极少的可优化参数有效缓解参数空间冲突，同时保留任务特定能力，为模型合并提供了一种具竞争力且高效的新范式。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient paradigm for constructing multi-task large language models (LLMs) without full model retraining, yet it remains challenged by parameter interference. While existing methods aim to preserve the capabilities of individual expert models and mitigate interference, they generally do not directly learn from the potentially degraded behaviors exposed by naive merging. In this paper, we propose a conflict-driven preference optimization framework for model merging (CoMerge), which reformulates model merging as a preference optimization problem. The approach utilizes a self-supervised, conflict-driven strategy that leverages the defects of naive merging methods (e.g., task arithmetic) as hard negative samples to construct preference pairs without external annotations. By applying preference optimization to refine lightweight, tensor-wise merging coefficients, CoMerge enables the model to mitigate parameter-space conflicts while preserving task-specific capabilities. Extensive experiments show that CoMerge achieves an average normalized performance of 0.9968 on MergeBench, outperforming all evaluated data-free and data-driven model-merging baselines. Furthermore, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, CoMerge yields marked improvements on conflict-sensitive tasks such as instruction following and safety, while remaining highly competitive with full-parameter fine-tuning despite optimizing only 1,445 scalar coefficients.

</details>


### [222] [SCX Router: Streaming Zero-Shot Model Selection with a Decoder-KV Classifier and a Real-World Task Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02292)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov,Mykhailo Shtopko,Dmytro Vodianytskyi,Oleksandr Lukashov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GLiClass的轻量级LLM路由模型（0.6B参数），通过为每个推理端点模型分配适配度分数（而非自回归生成），在逐任务层面优化速度、成本与质量之间的权衡。该路由器复用Qwen3解码器与浅层双向评分器，利用仅文本的键值缓存跨会话复用，仅编码新对话轮次，并将候选标签令牌排除在持久缓存之外；同时可预测任务类型、难度、推理模式及预期输出长度，并支持自定义零样本标签。作者构建了包含23个家族、115种任务类型、345个可路由子类型、1173个合成示例及30个领域正交轴的任务本体，据此生成15万条验证器评分任务和1.5万条开放式任务用于训练，并将学习到的请求预测与逐任务策略（资格、成本、缓存复用、安全、主权）显式分离。在六个LiveBench子集及1000任务子集上，该路由器优于平均候选模型和最强固定模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型及其应用的快速增长带来独特的优化机会：在逐任务层面为任务选择合适模型，并同时优化速度、成本和质量。然而，推理端点之间在质量、价格、延迟、上下文支持、工具使用、领域专长和推理行为等方面差异巨大，这种异质性使得人工启发式规则难以维护，也很难单独实现一致且有利的速度-成本-质量权衡。

Method: 提出一个轻量级GLiClass-based路由器：1）架构上，0.6B检查点将Qwen3解码器与浅层双向评分器结合，通过解码器-KV执行路径在会话中保留仅文本的键值缓存，仅编码新对话轮次，并评估瞬态候选标签令牌而不将其加入持久缓存；2）功能上，同一检查点同时预测任务类型、难度、推理模式和预期输出长度，并支持自定义零样本标签；3）数据工程上，构建任务本体（23个家族、115种任务类型、345个可路由子类型、1173个合成示例、30个领域正交轴），生成150,000个验证器评分任务和15,000个开放式任务；4）训练策略上，在Qwen3解码器上训练，同时将学习到的请求预测与逐任务策略（资格、成本、缓存复用、安全、主权）显式分离。

Result: 在六个LiveBench子集上，该路由器的表现优于平均候选模型；在选定的1000任务子集上，其聚合top-1分数达到0.707，而最强固定模型为0.696，并展现出随基准不同而变化的增益。

Conclusion: 基于GLiClass的轻量级路由器能够在避免自回归生成开销的同时，实现优于固定最强模型的逐任务模型选择性能，为异构推理端点环境下的速度-成本-质量权衡提供了可行的自动化解决方案，且其任务本体与训练数据生成方法为可扩展的路由训练提供了支撑。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and the growing diversity of their applications presents a unique optimization opportunity: selecting the right model for the task, while optimizing for speed, cost, and quality at a per-task level. However, inference endpoints can vary widely in quality, price, latency, context support, tool use, domain expertise, and reasoning behavior. This heterogeneity makes manual heuristics difficult to maintain and unlikely to achieve consistently favorable speed--cost--quality trade-offs on their own. We introduce \router{}, a lightweight GLiClass-based router that assigns a suitability score to each inference-time model label without autoregressive generation. The released 0.6B-parameter checkpoint combines a Qwen3 decoder with a shallow bidirectional scorer. Its decoder-KV execution path preserves a text-only key--value cache across a session, encodes only new dialogue turns, and evaluates transient candidate-label tokens without adding them to the persistent cache. The same checkpoint also predicts task type, difficulty, reasoning mode, and expected output length, and supports custom zero-shot labels. For task generation, we construct a task ontology with 23 families, 115 task types, 345 routable subtypes, 1,173 synthetic examples, and an orthogonal axis of 30 domains. Using this structure, we generate 150,000 verifier-scored tasks and 15,000 open-ended tasks. We then train the Qwen3 decoder on these tasks, while explicitly separating learned request prediction from per-task policies for attributes such as eligibility, cost, cache reuse, safety, and sovereignty.
