Across six LiveBench subsets, the router outperforms the mean candidate; on the selected 1,000-task subset, it achieves an aggregate top-1 score of 0.707 versus 0.696 for the strongest fixed model, with benchmark-dependent gains.

</details>


### [223] [Improving Evaluation Realism with Inference-Time Compute and Deployment Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02302)
*Axel Ahlqvist,Richard Guan,Juan-Pablo Rivera,Adeline Kassler,Dmitrii Troitskii,Alexandra Souly,Kai Fronsdal,Robert Kirk,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种提升对齐评估真实性的技术——critique refinement（利用额外推理时计算生成并细化候选动作）与 DISH（将目标模型封装进智能体框架以缩小模拟与真实部署环境的差距），实验表明二者可组合使用，且比单纯延长审计更能有效利用额外算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐评估面临的核心障碍是"评估感知"（evaluation awareness）：能力强的模型能够识别自己正在被测试而非被部署，从而削弱安全评估结论的可信度。需要让模拟评估更接近真实部署场景，以使评估结论更具说服力。

Method: 提出两种技术：1）Critique refinement——在模拟器的每个动作上投入额外推理时计算：生成多个候选动作，利用目标模型实例的反馈对其细化以提升部署真实性，然后以最接近部署的候选继续评估；2）DISH（Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness）——将目标模型封装进智能体框架，缩小编码场景中模拟环境与真实部署环境的差距。

Result: 在多个目标模型上测试表明两种技术具有组合性：同时应用二者比单独使用任一技术获得更大的真实性提升。结果还显示自动化方法能提升对齐评估的真实性，且这些改进对额外算力的利用效率高于简单地延长审计时间。

Conclusion: 自动化方法可以有效地提高对齐评估的真实性；在算力预算固定的情况下，采用这两种技术（尤其是组合使用）比简单地延长审计长度更能提升评估质量。

Abstract: A core obstacle to alignment evaluation is evaluation awareness: capable models can tell when they are being tested rather than deployed, weakening the conclusions a safety evaluation can support. We present two techniques that make simulated alignment evaluations harder to distinguish from real deployments. Our first technique, critique refinement, spends additional inference-time compute on each simulator action: the simulator generates multiple candidate actions, refines them using feedback from an instance of the target model on how to make them more realistic, and continues the evaluation with the most deployment-like candidate. Our second technique, DISH (Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness), wraps the target in an agent harness, reducing the gap between simulated and real deployment environments in coding settings. We test the techniques on multiple target models and find that they compose: applying both yields larger realism gains than either alone. Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [224] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SALA（语义感知逻辑对齐）框架，用于面向复杂推理任务的大模型上下文学习（ICL）示例选择。SALA不依赖固定的推理步骤库，而是自动学习任务特定的推理操作，并将其嵌入连续语义空间，再利用动态时间规整（DTW）对推理序列进行软对齐与灵活匹配，兼具可解释性。在四个推理基准和三个大语言模型上的实验表明，SALA优于现有示例选择方法，并验证了操作归纳与逻辑语义对齐两个模块的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂推理任务中的ICL效果高度依赖示例选择。传统基于表面相似度的检索无法捕捉底层解题逻辑；现有基于逻辑的方法虽能匹配预定义的推理步骤，但规则僵硬、采用精确匹配标准，难以应对灵活多样的推理过程，因此需要一种既能捕捉推理逻辑又足够柔性的示例选择方法。

Method: SALA框架包含两个关键设计：1) 操作归纳（Operation Induction）：不依赖固定操作库，自动从数据中学习任务特有的推理操作；2) 逻辑语义对齐（Logical Semantic Alignment）：将推理操作嵌入连续语义空间，并用动态时间规整（DTW）对齐推理序列，实现推理逻辑的软性、灵活匹配，同时保持高可解释性。

Result: 在四个推理基准和三种大语言模型上的实验表明，SALA在示例选择性能上优于现有方法；进一步的消融与分析实验证实了操作归纳和逻辑语义对齐两个模块各自的作用。

Conclusion: SALA通过自动学习任务特异推理操作并结合DTW语义对齐，克服了传统表面相似度检索与刚性逻辑匹配的局限，为复杂推理的ICL示例选择提供了更有效且可解释的方案。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [225] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGENTSCOPE，一种神经符号（neuro-symbolic）方法，用于诊断LLM智能体（agent）的失败模式：将智能体轨迹抽象为结构化表示，引入"神经不变量"（neural invariants）刻画行为属性，并借助LLM引导的推理在结构化表示上对照神经不变量，精确定位失败步骤及其类型；在Who&When与新建的AgentErrata数据集上显著优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体的普及，理解并诊断其失败对提升有效性与可信度至关重要。智能体失败常表现为冗长复杂的轨迹，人工排查如同大海捞针；传统软件缺陷诊断技术难以应对LLM智能体失败，而完全依赖LLM作为评判者又会产生不可靠的诊断结果，因此亟需一种兼具可靠性与可解释性的自动诊断方法。

Method: AGENTSCOPE采用神经符号混合思路：首先基于智能体轨迹将其行为抽象为结构化表示；其次引入"神经不变量"概念来形式化刻画智能体的行为属性（期望性质）；最后利用LLM引导的推理，在结构化表示上对照神经不变量进行推理，从而同时定位轨迹中的失败步骤（fault localization）并判定其失败类型（attribution）。结构化抽象提供可验证、可解释的底座，LLM提供语义推理能力，二者互补。

Result: 在公开的智能体失败数据集Who&When以及作者自建、更全面的AgentErrata数据集上，AGENTSCOPE在故障定位与失败归因（attribution）准确率方面均显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将结构化抽象与LLM引导推理相结合，能够为LLM智能体失败提供有效、可靠且可解释的诊断，为智能体可观测性与可信赖性奠定基础。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [226] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对定量双极论证框架（QBAFs）提出对比性解释（contrastive explanations），不同于现有解释只关注单一主题论证的推理结果，对比性解释用于解释两个主题论证之间的结果差异。作者定义了通用的对比归因函数（CAFs）及其应满足的性质，并提出了基于移除、梯度和Shapley值的三类CAFs，最后在医疗和偏见识别场景中展示了其应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证框架是表示和推理信息的有效工具，可用于补充AI模型的分类任务并提供额外可解释性。然而，现有QBAF解释方法只解释单个主题论证的推理结果，无法回答"为什么两个论证的结果不同"这类对比性问题，这一局限性催生了对比性解释的需求。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了对比性解释并引入对比归因函数（CAFs）的一般形式，确立了CAFs应满足的一组通用性质；随后提出三种具体的CAFs实例——基于移除（removal）、梯度（gradients）和Shapley值的方法，并系统研究它们的性质；最后通过医疗和偏见识别案例进行应用演示。

Result: 成功建立了QBAFs中对比性解释的形式化框架，给出了CAFs的通用性质集合，并实现了基于移除、梯度和Shapley值的三种CAFs变体；实验性演示表明这些对比性解释在医疗场景和偏见识别中具有实用价值。

Conclusion: 对比性解释扩展了QBAFs的解释能力，从解释单个论证结果拓展到解释两个论证结果之间的差异，为论证框架的可解释性研究提供了新方向，并在实际应用中展现出帮助识别偏见、支持医疗决策的潜力。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [227] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UTP-Bench，一个面向不确定性感知旅行规划的大规模基准，包含印度504个城市的真实旅行数据（景点、餐厅、住宿、多模态交通网络），并引入经验延误分布与人群密度模式来模拟随机扰动。同时提出三项评价指标（BAS、CATS、TDAS），量化行程在交通延误与人群波动下的稳健性。对GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral和Phi-4等SOTA LLM的实验表明，模型生成的行程与人工撰写行程之间仍存在显著差距，尤其在时间缓冲、延误感知的交通调度和人群敏感规划方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的旅行规划本质上是高度不确定的：交通延误、人群波动和意外随机扰动会频繁使原本可行的行程失效。然而，现有基准（如TravelPlanner和TripCraft）均假设确定性环境，仅评估静态约束满足情况，忽视了计划在面对不确定性时是否依然稳健。这一局限性促使作者构建一个能模拟真实随机扰动的基准，用以更真实地评估LLM的行程生成能力。

Method: (1) 数据集构建：整合覆盖印度504个城市的真实旅行数据，包括景点、餐厅、住宿及多模态交通网络；(2) 不确定性建模：引入从主要城市收集的经验延误分布和人群密度模式，在随机条件下评估行程；(3) 评价指标设计：提出三个量化指标——Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS，缓冲充分性)、Crowd-Aware Timing Score (CATS，人群感知时序)和Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS，交通延误吸收能力)；(4) 实验评估：对GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral、Phi-4等先进LLM进行系统测试，并与人工撰写的行程对比。

Result: 实验结果显示，SOTA LLM生成的行程与人工撰写的行程之间存在显著差距，具体体现在三个维度：时间缓冲不足（BAS较低）、缺乏延误感知的交通调度（TDAS表现不佳）、以及人群敏感型规划能力弱（CATS评分低）。这表明当前LLM在不确定性感知的稳健旅行规划方面仍有明显短板。

Conclusion: UTP-Bench填补了确定性旅行规划基准的空白，为评估LLM在随机扰动下的行程稳健性提供了大规模测试平台与量化指标体系。实验结果揭示了现有LLM在不确定性场景下的系统性不足，为未来构建更稳健、更符合现实需求的自动化旅行规划模型指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [228] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CivBench——一个基于《文明VI》与模型上下文协议（MCP）的开源评测基准，用于评估语言模型智能体在长时程、工具中介环境中的表现。单集包含300余轮对话、数千次工具调用与76个MCP工具，并配有将视觉游戏状态转为结构化文本的叙述层。作者在23次有效运行中（4个模型家族）引入两个界面级指标：主动监控率（PMR）与RAG@10。结果表明：智能体对策略相关状态监控不足（未按指令每20回合查询胜利进度，7/20次可检测失败中未在20回合警告窗口内查询），且常未执行自身规划反思中的近期承诺（RAG@10介于48.2%–65.8%）。作者将其解读为“指令下的偏离”而非能力缺失，并开源全部环境、场景、日志、指标与分析管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测主要关注短时程或单一工具调用，难以刻画智能体在长时程、部分可观测、大动作空间中的规划、状态监控与持续执行能力。作者希望借助MCP这一标准化工具协议，构建可复现、开放的基准，以剖析智能体在真实复杂环境中的行为模式，而非简单排名。

Method: 基于《文明VI》构建MCP工具中介环境（76个工具），并加入叙述层将视觉状态转为结构化文本以实现部分可观测的文本接口。设计两个界面级指标：PMR衡量智能体是否主动查询潜在战略状态；RAG@10衡量结构化规划反思中陈述的承诺是否在随后10回合内被执行。在4个模型家族的23次有效运行中，按统一“剧本协议”（playbook protocol）开展受控实验并分析日志。

Result: 两个一致行为模式：其一，智能体系统性低估监控——尽管剧本明确要求每20回合查询胜利进度，实际间隔为30–75回合，且在20次可检测失败中有7次未在游戏结束前20回合警告窗口内查询；其二，智能体频繁未执行自身反思中提出的近期承诺，RAG@10在各模型间为48.2%–65.8%。以上现象在工具可用且有明确指引时依然出现。注意样本仅为试点规模，聚合结果不足以可靠区分模型。

Conclusion: 智能体的欠监控与承诺执行失败并非源于能力缺失（工具与指引均已提供），而应解释为“在指令下的行为偏离”，即遵守指令与自主执行之间的差距。CivBench提供可测量的界面级指标与完整开源资源，为后续研究长时程智能体行为、指令遵循与自我承诺落实提供了基准与分析方法。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [229] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张，生成式AI对创造力的影响应从"混合集体"（人与算法共同组成的群体）的视角理解，而非个体视角。争议源于混淆了新颖性（单个产物的属性）与多样性（群体的属性）：AI辅助构思虽能稳定提升个体输出的新颖性，却会在总体上收窄多样性；但这并非必然结果，因为人类与模型的搜索方式互补，混合群体可以超越任一单一类型群体。决定性因素在于群体构成（哪些智能体、比例如何、连接方式如何），核心问题从"AI是否有益于创造力"转变为"何种混合方式能让个体收益累积而不侵蚀集体多样性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者对生成式AI是丰富还是贫化文化存在分歧，作者认为分歧根源在于混淆了创造力的两个不同组成部分——作为单个产物属性的新颖性与作为群体属性的多样性；因此需要重新概念化创造力，将其视为混合集体的属性而非个体的属性。

Method: 这是一篇概念性的论证论文：通过区分"新颖性"与"多样性"两个维度，提出创造力应定位在人与算法交互的混合集体层面；基于人类与模型在搜索空间上的互补性，论证混合群体的构成（智能体种类、比例、连接拓扑）决定创造力收益能否在群体层面累积。

Result: 作者指出AI辅助构思会可靠提升个体输出新颖性，但收窄总体多样性；同时论证这种多样性萎缩并非人机协同的必然代价——混合群体在表现与多样性上可超越纯人类或纯AI群体，且机器发现的解可进入人类文化并持续存在。结果由群体组成（何种智能体、比例、连接方式）决定。

Conclusion: 结论是：核心议题不再是有无AI之辩，而是何种"混合配方"能让个体层面的创造力收益积累，同时避免集体层面多样性的侵蚀；创造力应被理解为混合集体的涌现属性。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [230] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loom是一个面向工业级根因分析（RCA）的生成式共识框架：它将模块化启发式规则产生的开放形式文本假设投影到连续嵌入空间，通过迭代质心重加权算法聚合冲突信号，最终仅用一次轻量级LLM调用生成综合结论。在OpenRCA基准上，Loom以单次LLM调用（约26倍速度提升，8B合成器下约33倍）匹配了最先进自主Agent在Bank和Market-2上的精度，仅在Market-1和Telecom上略有落后，占据精度-效率Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实工业场景中，将嘈杂且相互冲突的文本假设聚合为可靠共识是部署NLP系统的根本挑战。单体LLM Agent表达力强但受限于上下文长度、幻觉累积和推理延迟；传统弱监督虽有统计严谨性，却在数学上局限于离散类别。Loom旨在弥合这两类范式的鸿沟，在保证准确性的同时大幅降低推理开销。

Method: Loom将模块化启发式（由事件特有实体、时间与指标动态填充的诊断模板）产生的开放形式假设投影到连续嵌入空间，借助迭代质心重加权算法消解冲突信号，所得共识权重为单一轻量级LLM综合步骤提供依据（每个事件仅需一次LLM调用）。

Result: 在OpenRCA基准上，Loom占据精度-效率Pareto前沿：在Bank和Market-2上匹配最先进的自主Agent，在Market-1和Telecom上略有差距；四个数据集上均仅使用一次LLM调用，速度约提升26倍，采用8B参数合成器时约提升33倍。

Conclusion: 作者分享了部署经验及教训：Agent式推理深度与推理延迟之间存在权衡；冗余检测方面存在负面结果（即该方法未能有效识别冗余假设）；确定性共识机制有助于赢得领域专家（SME）的信任。这印证了"确定性聚合+单次轻量综合"路线在工业RCA中的实用价值。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [231] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统检验"门面技术"（先提大要求被拒后再提小要求以提高顺从率）在九个来自三家供应商的生产级语言模型上是否有效。结果因模型而异：Anthropic 前沿模型（Opus 5）上有效（顺从率从直接询问的29.3%升至65.8%），而在 OpenAI、Google 前沿模型及 Haiku 4.5 上反而适得其反（顺从率下降15.5至23.0个百分点）。对照实验表明让步本身在所有九个模型上都起作用，但"刚被拒绝后的反应"因模型家族而异；该方法也无法迁移到公共基准中的拒绝样本。决定让步是否奏效的关键是请求内容：将265个被拒的可用指令请求改写为同一主题的解释请求，在263例中消除了拒绝。结论：人类影响技术对语言模型的迁移是逐模型家族发生的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于考察社会心理学中的经典说服技巧"门面技术"（door-in-the-face）能否迁移到现代语言模型上：在人类中，先被拒绝的大请求会使随后的小请求更可能被答应。由于影响技巧可能被滥用（例如越狱、操纵模型），有必要实证检验该技巧对当前生产级模型的适用性，并探究其作用机制与边界条件。

Method: 选取来自三家供应商（Anthropic、OpenAI、Google）的九个生产模型作为被试。实验范式为：对每个模型先提出一个被拒绝的大请求，随后提出同一内容的较小版本请求，并将其顺从率与直接提出小请求的基线进行比较。同时设置无关主题的被拒大请求作为对照，以区分"让步行为本身"与"刚被拒绝后的反应"两种机制；另用公共基准中的拒绝样本检验技巧的可迁移性，并通过将"可用指令"类请求改写为"解释"类请求来分析拒绝的决定因素。

Result: 结果呈鲜明的模型家族分化：Anthropic 前沿模型（Opus 5）上技巧有效，小请求顺从率从直接询问的29.3%升至65.8%；而在 OpenAI、Google 的前沿模型及 Haiku 4.5 上，技巧反而适得其反，顺从率下降15.5至23.0个百分点。对照实验显示，无关主题的被拒大请求在所有九个模型上的效果均弱于相关主题，说明让步本身在所有模型上都起作用，而"刚拒绝过某事的反应"则因模型家族而异。该技巧不能迁移到公共基准的拒绝样本；对265个被拒的可用指令请求改写为解释请求后，263例不再被拒，表明请求的目标类型（指令 vs. 解释）是决定性地影响让步能否奏效的因素。

Conclusion: 人类影响技术对语言模型的迁移并非普适，而是逐模型家族发生的：门面技术是否奏效取决于具体模型的反应倾向（是否对让步作出积极回应），同时请求内容本身（索要指令还是解释）是拒绝与否的更强决定因素。这提示影响技巧在 LLM 上的效果需逐个模型评估，不能简单外推。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [232] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DisCo，一个以技能驱动的自主科研智能体，将领域内广泛存在的"操作性知识"（operational knowledge）蒸馏为紧凑、可验证的技能，并在研究任务中复用。任务无关蒸馏覆盖开源生态，产出包含 5,000+ 已验证技能、覆盖 20 个领域和 178 个能力族系的 AREX-Skill Library；任务导向蒸馏则针对具体任务生成所需技能。在固定模型骨干（GPT-5.5）、研究框架和执行预算的条件下，配备技能库的智能体在 MLE-bench、PaperBench、FrontierCS、PassNet 上分别提升 134.3%、34.4%、9.2% 和 14.0%，收益全部来自新增的蒸馏操作上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体已能端到端开展机器学习研究，其"模型骨干+规划/执行/记忆/验证框架"的架构仍把领域专属的实操知识排除在智能体之外。这类操作性知识（知道方法到真正跑通方法之间的诀窍）散落在论文和代码库中，但以面向人类读者、体量过大无法在任务中加载的形式存在，导致每个任务都要重新摸索。作者的目标是将这些知识蒸馏成紧凑、可验证、可跨任务复用的技能。

Method: 提出 DisCo（skill-powered research agent），采用两种互补的蒸馏形式：1）任务无关蒸馏：对开源生态中广泛使用的 ML 代码库进行冷凝，产出 AREX-Skill Library（5,000+ 已验证技能，源自 1,000 个广泛使用的仓库，组织为 20 个领域、178 个能力族系）；2）任务导向蒸馏：针对具体研究任务生成所需技能。技能在后续研究任务中被加载并复用。

Result: 在模型骨干、研究框架与下游执行预算全部固定的对照设置下，装备技能的智能体相比无技能基线在 MLE-bench 上提升 134.3%、PaperBench 提升 34.4%、FrontierCS 提升 9.2%、PassNet 提升 14.0%，证明收益全部来自所添加的蒸馏操作上下文。

Conclusion: 操作性知识可以脱离人类可读形态，被蒸馏为紧凑、可验证、可跨任务复用的技能，显著提升自主科研智能体的端到端研究能力。DisCo 验证了"把领域 know-how 显式沉淀进智能体"这一方向的可行性，并以大规模开放式技能库（AREX-Skill Library）作为可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [233] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体LLM系统中的编排者-工人交互建模为双层协调博弈，并将文本反思刻画为语义记忆空间上的随机游走；在证明“仅观察生成文本的门控无法一致改进、而环境接地门控可行”的信息论不可能性结果后，提出随机反思记忆上升（SRMA）算法，给出精确的收敛率（几何或多项式）与紧性构造，并在500个SWE-bench实例上验证（72.2% vs 70.8%基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统（编排者分解任务、工人执行、文本反思改进）虽有强经验结果，但缺乏关于协调机制、记忆改进和外部验证角色的统一理论解释；作者希望为这些现象建立严谨的博弈论与学习理论框架，并澄清“反思+门控”何时有效、何时必然失效。

Method: (1) 将编排者-工人交互建模为双层协调博弈：在有界耦合下，工人局部更新博弈是近似势博弈，均衡松弛由分解质量控制；(2) 将反思建模为语义记忆状态上的随机运动，自由形式反思给出有限时间上界、最坏情形紧性与持续性伤害条件下的正下界；(3) 证明信息论不可能性：仅观测生成文本的门控在文本不可区分环境上无法一致改进，环境接地门控则可行；(4) 提出SRMA：仅当接地评估风险严格下降时才接受候选记忆，并在标定与非退化修正质量下证明几何或多项式精确收敛、两种速率均为阶紧；(5) 提供随机评估的置信门控与分段平稳环境的再锚定保障。

Result: 理论部分给出近似势博弈的均衡松弛界、自由形式反思的有限时间界与紧性、门控的信息论分离结果，以及SRMA的精确收敛率与阶紧匹配构造；实验使用环境接地指标验证所预测的协调与漂移定律，Kimi完整系统在500个SWE-bench实例上解决72.2%，高于70.8%的mini-SWE-agent公开参考。

Conclusion: 该工作为多智能体LLM的协调、反思式记忆改进与外部验证提供了统一的理论基础，并通过环境接地门控解决了仅文本观测无法一致改进的固有限制；理论上保证收敛、经验上超越基线，表明接地评估是可靠反思改进的关键要素。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [234] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向工厂车间的本地（on-premise）智能助手：通过对大语言模型进行结构化压缩与检索增强（RAG）自适应后，模型参数量不再是判断答案质量的可靠指标。作者提出将部署视为“自适应后选择”问题，在可配置的通用能力下限与内存预算约束下，依据评测的答案质量和实测端上吞吐量为每个设备选择子网络；采用权重共享超网络配合三明治式原位蒸馏降低选择成本。在制造手册案例中，抽取使评测质量损失13.7%，检索接地蒸馏将其恢复至4.6%以内（挽回约三分之二损失），同一助手可在三个异构边缘层级以1.3–5瓦待机功耗运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂车间需要本地助手以便工人以对话方式访问机器文档，但能够胜任该任务的模型通常体积过大、难以适配车间硬件（如异构边缘设备、低功耗、内存受限）。现有压缩与自适应方法缺乏对“部署后真实答案质量”的系统性考量，且仅按参数量、速度或质量单一指标优化会牺牲能力或吞吐量。因此需要一个面向多设备异构环境的低成本子网络选择与部署方案。

Method: 提出两个关键观点与方法：(1) 实证表明经结构化压缩和检索接地自适应后，模型大小与适应后答案质量不再强相关：通用能力随参数量近乎线性下降，而经评测的检索增强答案质量则不随之下降；(2) 将部署形式化为“后自适应选择问题”：在每个设备的度量答案质量和实测吞吐量上选择一个子网络，并施加可配置的通用能力下限与内存预算约束。为实现低成本选择，使用权重共享超网络并配合三明治式（sandwich-style）原位蒸馏来训练所有子网络。

Result: 在制造手册案例研究中：抽取（压缩）使模型相对未剪枝模型的评测质量损失13.7%；检索接地蒸馏将其恢复到4.6%以内（即在质量上挽回原先损失的三分之二）。同一助手可部署在三个异构边缘计算层级上，待机功耗仅1.3至5瓦，说明该方法能在异构硬件上以极低功耗运行。

Conclusion: 结论是：当结合结构化压缩与检索接地自适应时，模型参数量不再能预测部署后的答案质量，因此部署应作为后自适应选择问题处理，依据实测答案质量与吞吐量、在能力下限和内存预算约束下为每个设备挑选子网络。仅优化大小、速度或质量的单一规则都会牺牲能力或吞吐量，而权重共享超网络加三明治式原位蒸馏使得这种按设备定制选择保持低成本，并可在工厂车间边缘硬件上以数瓦级功耗运行。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [235] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeEvolve 是一种基于经验的自我演化框架，用于 LLM 智能体的安全对齐。它从已完成的同策略轨迹中提取安全经验，驱动运行时 harness（提示与层级技能）与策略的双向协同演化：harness 侧进行有界、组件级、可审计可逆的更新，策略侧采用两阶段 SFT-RL 范式（harness 使用 SFT 引导策略利用演化后的 harness，harness 增强的 RL 通过验证器分解奖励塑造多步探索中的自主安全行为）。实验表明其在智能体安全基准上取得更强的安全-效用权衡，例如 Qwen3.5-4B 在 AgentDojo 上 ASR 降低 3 倍且良性效用从 59.79% 提升至 61.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体的安全风险同时来自最终有害响应和多步执行轨迹，而现有安全对齐机制要么依赖外部 harness 更新、要么依赖策略优化，单独应用任一范式都无法将运行时控制与内在安全性打通，因此需要一种能同时演化 harness 与策略的统一框架。

Method: SafeEvolve 采用经验驱动的自我演化闭环：1) harness 侧将轨迹级安全证据转化为有界、组件级（安全提示与层级技能）的更新，生成可审计、可逆的 harness 产物；2) 策略侧采用两阶段 SFT-RL：harness 使用 SFT 引导策略主动利用演化后的 harness 产物，harness 增强 RL 通过验证器分解的奖励在多步探索中塑造自主安全行为；3) 通过 harness-策略协同演化将安全经验转化为演化后的运行时 harness 与改进的策略行为。

Result: 在智能体安全基准上的实验表明，SafeEvolve 相比现有基线取得了更强的安全-效用权衡；在 Qwen3.5-4B 上，AgentDojo 的 ASR（攻击成功率）降低 3 倍，同时良性效用从 59.79% 提升至 61.86%。

Conclusion: 通过 harness-策略协同演化，SafeEvolve 成功将安全经验转化为演化的运行时环境与改进的策略行为，弥合了运行时控制与内在安全性之间的鸿沟，实现了更强的安全-效用折中。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [236] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了电信网络根因分析（RCA）从规则/机器学习方法到LLM赋能技术的演进，并提出一个与电信领域证据和知识对齐的结构化推理框架，用于提升5G/6G网络故障诊断的准确性和决策一致性。在两个5G基准数据集（TeleLogs、TelecomTS）上的实验表明，该框架优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G/6G网络存在复杂的跨层依赖，性能劣化诊断困难；直接使用vanilla LLM进行电信RCA会出现幻觉、推理不稳定、与结构化网络证据对齐差等问题，因此需要专门设计的结构化推理机制。

Method: 首先综述电信RCA的演进历程（规则式、机器学习、LLM赋能）及近期范式（结构化推理、检索增强知识接地、智能体编排、可验证推理）；在此基础上提出结构化推理框架：先将异构网络遥测数据组织为规范上下文（canonical contexts），再在诊断过程中强制执行决策路径推理（decision-path reasoning），最终生成基于证据的解释（evidence-grounded explanations）以支持可靠的故障识别。

Result: 在两个5G RCA数据集TeleLogs和TelecomTS上的实验表明，所提框架相比基线技术在诊断准确性和决策一致性上均有持续提升，且跨数据集结果验证了结构化推理设计对实际LLM-based RCA系统的重要性。

Conclusion: 结构化推理设计是构建实用型LLM电信RCA系统的关键，它能将LLM的推理能力与电信领域证据、领域知识对齐，从而减少幻觉并提升诊断可靠性，为下一代电信网络的智能运维提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [237] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AICOME（AI Contextual Measurement）框架，用于评估由AI生成的受访者层面测量能否在情境（contextual）模型中恢复个体层面与群体层面的效应；并以2022年中国家庭追踪调查（CFPS）中基于职业分组的多项工作相关变量进行验证，结果表明在数据信息丰富时AI测量可较好恢复情境模型信息，且存在明确的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者日益依赖人工智能来构建传统调查中缺失的社会、组织和职业特征测量，但现有做法往往将AI测量视为响应预测问题，缺乏系统性框架来评估AI生成的受访者层面测量能否在情境模型中同时恢复个体层（组内）与群体层（组间）效应，从而难以判断其对下游因果/关联推断的有用性。

Method: 提出AICOME框架：核心思想是将受访者层面的AI测量分解为群体层面的聚合值（组间）与个体偏离值（组内），从而同时估计组间与组内关联，而非仅当作响应预测。验证设计采用2022年CFPS数据，以职业作为经验性分组结构，以计算机使用、外语使用、每周工时和管理职责等多项工作相关调查变量作为基准，开展响应层面、模型层面、情境模型层面及边界条件四类验证。

Result: 当可获得丰富的受访者与职业特征时，AI情境测量能够恢复观测调查变量所蕴含的大部分情境模型信息。其中每周工时是最强验证案例，AI派生测量成功复现了CFPS中工时与满意度之间显著的职业间（组间）与职业内（组内）负向关联。边界条件亦清晰：当信息仅限职业与基本人口学特征时表现明显下降；当多个相关概念被同时视为未观测时，恢复能力减弱。

Conclusion: AICOME框架最适用于从信息丰富的既有数据集中恢复数量有限的理论重要构念，并提示研究者应在何种数据条件下信任AI派生测量用于情境分析，同时应警惕信息贫乏或概念高度重叠场景下的测量局限性。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [238] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“预测状态匹配”（predicted-state matching）的训练目标，用于训练网络智能体的世界模型。该方法要求世界模型预测的状态表征能够区分真实结果状态与替代动作所达状态，从而与下游排序器（PRM）的目标对齐，显著提升了动作排序和端到端任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络智能体的世界模型通过监督式下一状态预测（supervised next-state prediction）训练，生成固定表示（如HTML或AXTree快照）。然而该目标与下游排序器不一致：排序器依赖预测状态在候选项之间具有判别力（discriminative）才能准确打分，而仅追求预测保真度的目标无法保证这种判别性。

Method: 提出预测状态匹配训练目标：预测的表征必须能够区分真实结果状态与替代动作（alternative actions）导致的状态。为此，基于WebArena Go-Browse轨迹构建了分支式（branching）网络智能体数据集，每个决策点包含多个替代动作及其结果状态。在保留的预测状态匹配基准上训练并评估世界模型。

Result: 实验显示：1）在保留的预测状态匹配基准上，该方法优于监督式下一状态预测训练的世界模型；2）在WebPRMBench上，该方法相比仅用动作的PRM和用监督式下一状态世界模型增强的PRM，提升了PRM式动作排序性能；3）在WebArena-Lite上，用该方法的世界模型进行测试时动作选择能提升端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 预测状态匹配这一训练目标有效弥合了世界模型训练与下游排序/动作选择目标之间的错配，使世界模型生成的预测状态更具判别力，从而在动作排序和端到端任务成功率上均带来一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [WMLLM: Self-Evolving Optimization Agents via Predict-Then-Act World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01608)
*Zhongzheng Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Nian Ran,Wenhao Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WMLLM，一种基于"先预测后行动"（predict-then-act）世界建模的自进化优化智能体框架，利用大语言模型的隐式知识预测有前景的优化方向，再生成候选解，并结合多轮智能体精炼、种群搜索和强化学习，在搜索过程中同时改进隐式世界模型与优化策略，在黑盒优化任务（尤其是多目标分子优化）上显著提升样本效率并取得最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化问题因搜索空间大、结构弱、维度高而具有挑战性；现有方法依赖直接候选生成或试错式精炼，样本效率低下。自然语言模型因具备隐式知识能够以一定准确率预测候选结果，因此利用世界建模来识别有前景的优化方向，可在大规模评估之前提高搜索效率。

Method: 提出WMLLM框架：智能体先预测有前景的优化方向（predict），再据此生成候选（act）；结合智能体多轮精炼、基于种群的搜索和强化学习，在搜索过程中同时精炼隐式世界模型与优化策略，实现自进化。

Result: 在黑盒优化任务尤其是多目标分子优化基准上，WMLLM在有限评估预算下提升了样本效率与最终优化性能，并取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型的知识以世界建模方式融入黑盒优化搜索，通过预测引导行动并配合自进化机制，能够有效提升样本效率与优化效果；WMLLM为有限评估预算下的多目标优化提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems remain challenging because of large, weakly structured, and high-dimensional search spaces. Existing methods often suffer from poor sample efficiency because they rely on direct candidate generation or trial-and-error refinement. A natural way to improve search efficiency is to use world modeling, which can help identify promising optimization directions before costly evaluation. Large language models can predict the outcomes of these candidates with nontrivial accuracy because of their implicit knowledge. Motivated by this observation, we propose WMLLM, a self-evolving optimization-agent framework based on predict-then-act world modeling. The agent first predicts promising directions and then acts to generate candidates. Combined with agentic multi-turn refinement, population-based search, and reinforcement learning, WMLLM refines both its implicit world model and its optimization strategy during search. Experiments on black-box optimization tasks, especially multi-objective molecular optimization, show that WMLLM improves sample efficiency and final optimization performance. On the multi-objective molecular optimization benchmark, WMLLM achieves state-of-the-art results under a limited evaluation budget.

</details>


### [240] [DiDrive: A Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion Framework for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01609)
*Qisong Guo,Jingtang Chen,Zhilin Chen,Pei Xu,Mingjian Fu,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiDrive，一种面向离线强化学习自动驾驶决策的分布引导式离线扩散框架，通过风险感知分层扩散（RHDif）架构与 3DICE 策略优化范式协同，在状态空间过滤冗余信息并聚焦安全关键威胁，在动作空间抑制分布外动作的过估计与梯度振荡。在 CARLA 基准上，DiDrive 在 60 辆高密度车辆场景下取得 85% 成功率与 4295.68 平均奖励，优于 IQL、CQL、Diffusion-QL 等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能高效捕获自动驾驶的多模态行为先验，但离线强化学习策略仍面临四类挑战：分布偏移（distribution shift）、重尾风险信号（heavy-tailed risk signals）、分布外（OOD）动作生成以及高维状态冗余，这些问题严重制约安全可靠的自动驾驶决策。

Method: DiDrive 包含两个协同组件：1) RHDif（Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion）架构，在状态空间利用低层风险门控编码器（low-level risk-gated encoder）过滤环境冗余，并由高层上下文调制器（high-level contextual modulator）聚焦安全关键威胁；2) 3DICE（3D In-sample Calibrated ...）策略优化范式，在动作空间通过样本内校准引导、时空优化与集成式候选排序，缓解 OOD 过估计和梯度振荡问题。

Result: 在 CARLA 基准测试中，DiDrive 全面优于 IQL、CQL、Diffusion-QL 等基线；在包含 60 辆车辆的复杂高密度交通场景下表现尤为突出，达到 85% 成功率与 4295.68 的平均奖励。

Conclusion: DiDrive 通过状态空间的风险感知分层过滤与动作空间的样本内校准优化，为安全自动驾驶决策提供了稳健的可行路径，验证了分布引导式离线扩散框架在复杂高密度交通环境中的有效性。

Abstract: While diffusion models effectively capture multimodal behavioral priors for autonomous driving, offline reinforcement learning (RL) policies remain susceptible to distribution shift, heavy-tailed risk signals, out-of-distribution (OOD) action generation, and high-dimensional state redundancy. To address these challenges, we propose DiDrive, a distribution-guided offline diffusion framework featuring two synergistic components: the Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion (RHDif) architecture and the 3DICE policy optimization paradigm. In the state space, RHDif utilizes a low-level risk-gated encoder and a high-level contextual modulator to filter environmental redundancy and focus on safety-critical threats. In the action space, 3DICE mitigates OOD overestimation and gradient oscillation through in-sample calibrated guidance, spatiotemporal optimization, and ensemble-based candidate ranking. Evaluations on the CARLA benchmark demonstrate DiDrive's superiority over baselines like IQL, CQL, and Diffusion-QL, particularly in complex, high-density traffic scenarios with 60 vehicles, where it achieves an 85% success rate and a 4295.68 average reward, providing a robust pathway for safe autonomous driving decision-making.

</details>


### [241] [Prompt-Space Meta-Learning Does Not Transfer Across Users: A Frozen-LLM Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01615)
*Liam Byrne,David Dylan,Orla Fitzgerald,Eoin Doyle,Ciara Nolan,Padraig Lynch,Sinead Gallagher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究“在提示空间中通过元学习个性化冻结大语言模型”这一范式是否真的学到了可迁移的跨用户适应能力。作者提出Muse（通过反射式提示进化在元训练用户群体上进化单一共享适应提示并冻结、零样本应用于新用户），在LaMP-2与LaMP-3上各200个留出用户的实验表明：Muse相比未进化的种子提示、结构破坏控制（错配用户-支持对）均无显著提升，且在评分任务上明显劣于普通少样本检索（Delta MAE +0.175, p<0.001）。作者将原因归结为“元目标坍缩”：元验证目标在统计上对用户-支持对应关系是否真实不敏感（p=0.555与p=0.622），因此无法优化出可迁移的适应，只能奖励指令润色与验证集过拟合。种子提示、错配支持与不变性预言机三组控制构成了可复用的协议，用于把学到的适应与这些混淆因素分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化冻结LLM常被建模为提示空间中的元学习问题：每个用户是一个任务，目标是找到一个共享的自然语言适应策略，用用户少量带标签交互配置冻结模型。该框架因骨干无关且复用提示优化机制而具有吸引力，但领域内很少检验优化后的元目标编码的究竟是可迁移的跨用户适应，还是仅仅是一般的指令质量。作者旨在严格检验这一核心假设。

Method: 提出Muse方法：在元训练用户群体上通过反射式提示进化（reflective prompt evolution）进化出一个共享的适应提示，随后冻结并在留出用户上零样本应用。同时设计匹配的控制组以隔离学习效应与措辞、选择等混淆因素，包括：(1) 未进化的种子提示；(2) 结构破坏控制——在错配的用户-支持对上进行元训练；(3) 不变性预言机控制（invariance-oracle）——检验元验证目标对用户-支持对应关系是否统计不敏感。实验采用LaMP-2（分类）与LaMP-3（评分）两个标准个性化基准，各含200个留出用户，并对比普通少样本检索。

Result: Muse在两个基准上均未显著优于自己的未进化种子提示，也未优于在错配用户-支持对上元训练的结构破坏控制；在LaMP-3评分任务上，Muse被普通少样本检索显著超越（Delta MAE +0.175, p<0.001）。元验证目标对真实与错配的用户-支持对应关系统计不敏感（LaMP-2 p=0.555，LaMP-3 p=0.622），证实元目标坍缩：目标无法区分真实适应与虚假适应，因而被优化为指令润色和验证过拟合而非可迁移的个性化能力。

Conclusion: 元学习提示个性化范式的核心目标存在系统性缺陷：元验证目标在统计上对用户-支持对应关系的真实性不变，导致模型无法通过该目标学习可迁移的跨用户适应，只能获得一般的指令质量提升。作者提供的对照协议（种子提示、错配支持、不变性预言机）可作为可复用的评估工具，用于分离学到的适应与措辞、选择等混淆因素，提醒未来工作在提示空间个性化中须验证元目标确实编码了用户信息而非仅奖励提示润色。

Abstract: Personalizing a frozen large language model (LLM) to individual users is often framed as a meta-learning problem in prompt space: each user is a task, and one seeks a shared natural-language adaptation policy that, given a handful of the user's labeled interactions, configures the frozen model for that user. The framing is attractive because it is backbone-agnostic and reuses the machinery of prompt optimization, yet the field rarely tests whether the optimized meta-objective encodes transferable cross-user adaptation rather than generic instruction quality. We study this question with Muse (Meta-learned User-adaptation via Shared Evolution), which evolves a single shared adaptation prompt over a meta-train user population by reflective prompt evolution, freezes it, and applies it zero-shot to held-out users; matched controls isolate learning from confounds of phrasing and selection. On two standard personalization benchmarks (LaMP-2 categorization and LaMP-3 rating) over 200 held-out users each, Muse does not significantly improve on its own un-evolved seed prompt or on a structure-broken control that meta-trains on mismatched user-support pairs, and is dominated by plain few-shot retrieval on the rating task (Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001). We attribute these outcomes to a single mechanism, meta-objective collapse: the meta-validation objective is statistically invariant to whether the user-support correspondence is genuine (p=0.555 on LaMP-2, p=0.622 on LaMP-3), so it cannot be optimized into transferable adaptation and instead rewards instruction polish and validation overfitting. The seed-prompt, wrong-support, and invariance-oracle controls form a reusable protocol that separates learned adaptation from these confounds.

</details>


### [242] [Efficient Context-Limited Telescope Bibliography Classification for the WASP-2025 Shared Task Using SciBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01647)
*Madhusudhana Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 SciBERT 的高效方法，用于自动将科学论文分为“科学（science）、仪器（instrumentation）、提及（mention）、非望远镜（not telescope）”四类，以构建望远镜文献目录。在严格限制上下文长度（最多512个token）和有限计算资源的条件下，该方法取得0.89的宏F1分数，在 WASP-2025 排行榜上排名第一。作者还分析了截断的影响，并讨论了截断、分块与长上下文模型之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 望远镜文献目录的构建是评估天文台科学影响力和确保天文学可复现性的关键环节，需要识别、分类并关联引用或使用特定望远镜的科学出版物。然而该过程目前主要依赖人工，既耗时又耗费资源，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用基于 SciBERT 的分类方法，将论文自动归入四个类别（science、instrumentation、mention、not telescope）。在最大512 token的严格截断约束与有限计算资源下进行训练与推理，并系统分析截断对分类性能的影响，对比截断、分块与长上下文模型三种策略的权衡。

Result: 该方法取得了0.89的宏F1分数，位列 WASP-2025 排行榜第一。实验表明即使超过一半样本超出token限制，SciBERT 的领域对齐能力仍能保证稳健的分类性能。

Conclusion: SciBERT 的领域预训练使其在截断条件下仍能保持高分类性能，为科学文本策展提供了高效前沿的洞见；截断带来的效率收益可以抵消信息损失，是资源受限场景下的优选策略。

Abstract: The creation of telescope bibliographies is a crucial part of assessing the scientific impact of observatories and ensuring reproducibility in astronomy. This task involves identifying, categorizing, and linking scientific publications that reference or use specific telescopes. However, this process remains largely manual and resource intensive. In this work, we present an efficient SciBERT-based approach for automatic classification of scientific papers into four categories - science, instrumentation, mention, and not telescope. Despite strict context-length constraints (maximum 512 tokens) and limited compute resources, our approach achieved a macro F1 score of 0.89, ranking at the top of the WASP-2025 leaderboard. We analyze the effect of truncation and show that even with half the samples exceeding the token limit, SciBERT's domain alignment enables robust classification. We discuss trade-offs between truncation, chunking, and long-context models, providing insights into the efficiency frontier for scientific text curation.

</details>


### [243] [CliffRank: A Dual-Branch Framework for Activity-Cliff Ranking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01673)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Peiyu Yang,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CliffRank，一种结合绝对活性回归与排序一致性学习的活性悬崖排序方法。它使用两个并行预测器，联合均方误差、阈值化列表损失和 Pairwise Preference Consistency（PPC）损失进行训练。在三个抗菌肽数据集上，CliffRank（ESM2-t12）取得最高平均 Spearman 相关系数 0.5393 和平均 Recall@50 21.4；在三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank（PNA，PPC 在 120 轮后激活）取得最高平均 Spearman 0.6890，平均 Recall@50 30.4 与 ACANet-PNA 持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性悬崖排序仍然困难：局部结构变化可能导致大的活性差异，而能解析其潜在机制的高质量数据有限。因此需要更有效地利用已有活性标签，以提升活性悬崖排名性能。

Method: 将绝对活性回归与排序一致性学习相结合：训练两个并行预测器，损失函数包含均方误差、阈值化列表损失以及 Pairwise Preference Consistency（PPC）——在偏好概率空间中对齐相对排序。同时探索了非对称初始化以及延迟激活 PPC 的训练调度。

Result: 在三个抗菌肽数据集上，CliffRank 使用 ESM2-t12 取得最高平均 Spearman 相关系数 0.5393 和平均 Recall@50 21.4，但领先方法因数据集而异。在三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank 使用 PNA（PPC 在 120 轮后激活）取得最高平均 Spearman 0.6890，平均 Recall@50 30.4 与 ACANet-PNA 持平。非对称初始化提升 MolCLR-GIN 的平均指标但未提升所有目标；对无预训练权重的 PNA，延迟 PPC 改善部分指标，但没有单一调度同时在两个指标上最优。

Conclusion: CliffRank 有效利用有限标签提升活性悬崖排序，PPC 结果也界定了其实际局限。未来工作应评估更多目标和抗菌肽体系、开发自适应 PPC 调度，并在可用时融入蛋白质或膜上下文信息。

Abstract: Activity-cliff ranking remains difficult because local structural changes can cause large activity differences, while high-quality data that resolve the underlying mechanisms remain limited. To use available activity labels more effectively, we combine absolute-activity regression with ranking-consistency learning. CliffRank trains two parallel predictors with mean squared error, a thresholded listwise loss, and Pairwise Preference Consistency (PPC), which aligns relative ordering in the preference-probability space. On three antimicrobial peptide datasets, CliffRank with ESM2-t12 achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.5393 and mean Recall@50 of 21.4, although the leading method varied across individual datasets. On three small-molecule datasets, CliffRank with PNA, where PPC was activated after 120 epochs, achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.6890, while its mean Recall@50 of 30.4 matched that of ACANet-PNA. The PPC results also define its practical limits. Asymmetric initialization improved the MolCLR-GIN averages but did not improve every target. For PNA without pretrained weights, delayed PPC improved selected metrics, but no schedule was best for both mean Spearman correlation and mean Recall@50. Future work should evaluate more targets and antimicrobial peptide systems, develop adaptive PPC schedules, and incorporate protein or membrane context when available.

</details>


### [244] [Sim2Signal: Sim-to-Real Benchmarks for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01676)
*Ferdous Al Rafi,Susrik Mukherjee,Latika Liladhar Dekate,Jennifer Yawa Lavoe,Huaiyuan Yao,Shlok Mohanty,Longchao Da,Xuesong Zhou,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sim2Signal，一个将交通信号控制中强化学习的 Sim-to-Real 差距系统分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四种差距的基准测试框架。通过在 33 种差距设置、10 个基于 5 个真实地点构建的标定网络、2 个基础控制器上评估 18 种缓解方法，发现直接迁移在四种差距源上均导致性能下降，但下降严重程度无法预测缓解方法的有效性；缓解效果高度依赖网络和差距设置，最有效的方法通常是估计差距带来的变化，而非通过域随机化或不变量表示使策略不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在仿真中能达到良好的交通信号控制性能，但仿真中训练的策略在真实世界部署时常常失败（Sim-to-Real 差距）。该差距来源于感知、动作执行、交通动态和控制目标等多个方面，但各来源的相对影响以及现有缓解方法的可靠性尚缺乏充分理解，且该领域缺少一个标准基准来系统测量差距并评估缓解方法。

Method: 提出 Sim2Signal 基准：将 Sim-to-Real 差距分解为观测差距（observation gap）、动作差距（action gap）、转移差距（transition gap）和奖励差距（reward gap），分别对应底层 MDP 四个组成部分的不匹配，并在统一协议下独立诱导每种差距。在此基础上，对 2 个基础控制器、33 种差距设置、10 个由 5 个真实地点构建的标定网络，系统评估 18 种缓解方法。

Result: 直接迁移在四种差距源上均持续导致性能下降，但性能下降的严重程度并不能预测缓解方法的有效性。缓解方法的有效性强烈依赖于网络和差距设置：除动作差距外，一种方法在某情况下有效，在另一种情况下可能失效。最有效的方法通常是估计差距带来的变化（gap-dependent estimation），而非通过域随机化或不变量表示使策略对差距不敏感。

Conclusion: Sim2Signal 提供了首个系统分解与测量交通信号控制 Sim-to-Real 差距的标准基准，揭示了缓解方法的有效性与网络、差距设置强相关这一关键发现，并指出估计差距的方法优于域随机化等不敏感化方法，为该领域的后续研究与方法选择提供了实证依据和统一评测平台。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning achieves strong traffic signal control performance in simulation, yet policies trained in simulators often fail once deployed in the real world, a failure known as the Sim-to-Real gap. When RL is applied to traffic signal control, this gap arises from several sources: sensing, action execution, traffic dynamics, and the control objective. Their relative impact and the reliability of existing Sim-to-Real mitigation methods remain insufficiently understood, and the field lacks a standard benchmark for systematically measuring the gap and evaluating mitigation methods. We present Sim2Signal, a benchmark that decomposes the Sim-to-Real gap into observation, action, transition, and reward gaps, corresponding to mismatches in the four components of the underlying MDP, and induces each gap in isolation under a shared protocol. We evaluate 18 mitigation methods on 2 base controllers, across 33 gap settings and 10 calibrated networks built from 5 real-world locations. We find that direct transfer consistently degrades performance across all four gap sources, but the severity of the degradation does not predict the effectiveness of mitigation. Instead, mitigation effectiveness depends strongly on the network and gap setting: outside the action gap, a method that helps in one case may fail in another. The most effective methods generally estimate what the gap changes, rather than make the policy insensitive through domain randomization or invariant representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Red-Pheonix/Sim2RealTSCBenchMark

</details>


### [245] [A Survey on Self-Improving Test-Time Intelligence: Feedback-Driven Adapting, Learning, and Scaling at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01679)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Yaofo Chen,Zhiquan Wen,Jinwu Hu,Zeshuai Deng,Deyu Chen,Shuhai Zhang,Renjie Chen,Zihao Lian,Shoukai Xu,Gang Dai,Yunbei Zhang,Wei Luo,Yifan Zhang,Mingkui Tan,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"反馈驱动的测试时智能（TTI）"作为统一视角，将测试时自适应、测试时学习与测试时扩展整合为部署期间自我改进的AI系统研究框架，并综述其方法范式、典型应用与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在部署期间利用测试时信息和额外计算来改进自身行为的能力日益重要，相关研究沿着两条路径发展（修改模型状态的方法、通过额外推理资源改进预测的方法），但这两条方向常被不同社区以不同术语分别研究，导致概念割裂、连接难以辨识，因此需要一个统一框架来整合这些碎片化思想。

Method: 采用综述方法，提出"反馈驱动的测试时智能（TTI）"这一统一视角，用以关联测试时自适应、测试时学习和测试时扩展，阐明三者的区别与混合系统中的重叠；随后回顾主要方法论范式、代表性应用（视觉、语言、多模态学习、生成模型、机器人、医疗）并梳理开放挑战，形成研究路线图。

Result: 该统一框架成功连接了先前分散的研究方向，为推断时自我改进提供了更清晰的概念基础，并系统梳理了跨多个领域的代表性应用与开放挑战，给出了自改进AI系统测试时研究的研究路线图。

Conclusion: 反馈驱动的测试时智能（TTI）为研究部署期自我改进的AI系统提供了连贯的概念基础，有助于弥合测试时自适应、学习与扩展之间的鸿沟，并为该领域的未来发展指明方向。

Abstract: The ability of AI systems to improve their behavior during deployment is becoming increasingly important. As inference moves beyond the static execution of a fixed trained model, a growing body of work studies how models can refine their behavior on the fly by exploiting test-time information and additional computation. These developments have largely evolved along two directions: methods that modify the model's state using test-time signals, and methods that improve predictions through extra inference-time resources such as more sampling and tool use. However, these directions are often studied in separate communities with different terminology, making their connections harder to see. In this survey, we present feedback-driven Test-Time Intelligence (TTI) as a unified perspective for understanding such deployment-time improvement. We use this view to relate test-time adaptation, test-time learning, and test-time scaling, highlighting both their distinctions and their growing overlap in hybrid systems. This unified framework helps connect previously fragmented ideas and provides a clearer conceptual foundation for studying inference-time self-improvement. We review major methodological paradigms, representative applications, and open challenges across vision, language, multimodal learning, generative models, robotics, and healthcare. Our goal is to provide a coherent foundation and research roadmap for the study of self-improving AI systems at test time.

</details>


### [246] [Reinforcement Learning and Rule-Based Peer-to-Peer Pricing in Residential PV-BES Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01680)
*Pablo Benalcazar,Maciej Kalka,Wilian Guamán,Jacek Kamiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了住宅光伏社区点对点（P2P）电力交易中的基于规则与基于学习的定价机制。在纯光伏配置下，基于规则的基准方法（账单分摊、中间市场价、供需比定价）优于最优强化学习（RL）策略；引入电池储能后，最优RL策略的社区节省从EUR 734.23提升至EUR 978.52。在所有学习模式中，SDR形定价均优于乘数型参数化方案，且收益在家庭间分布不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着住宅光伏社区的普及，P2P电力交易定价机制的设计至关重要。该研究旨在通过系统比较基于规则的传统定价机制与基于深度强化学习的自适应定价机制，回答"哪种定价方式更能提升社区整体节省"的问题，并为社区储能部署下的定价策略选择提供实证依据。

Method: 采用对比实验框架：规则型基准包括事后分配机制（账单分摊）、中间市场率和供需比（SDR）定价；学习型方案以Deep Q-Network实现强化学习，在乘数型与可学习SDR形定价下评估，并设固定参数SDR变体作为非学习对照。评估指标为社区节省及补充的财务与运营指标，分别在纯光伏配置和含电池储能配置（仅评估RL策略）下进行实验。

Result: 在纯光伏基准配置中，规则型基准方法优于最优RL策略；加入电池储能后，最优RL策略的社区节省由EUR 734.23增至EUR 978.52。在全部学习型模式及两种配置下，SDR形定价均优于所考察的乘数型参数化；家庭间的收益分配具有异质性。

Conclusion: 在可直接对比的场景中，规则型定价仍具有很强的竞争力，并非必然被学习型方法取代；储能能够显著改善学习型定价的社区节省表现，但收益分配不均提示需关注公平性问题。后续研究可扩展至更丰富的储能配置下的直接对比，并改进收益公平分配。

Abstract: This paper compares rule-based and learning-based pricing mechanisms for peer-to-peer (P2P) electricity trading in residential photovoltaic communities. The rule-based benchmarks comprise bill-sharing as an ex post allocation mechanism, the mid-market rate, and supply-demand-ratio pricing. The reinforcement-learning (RL) formulation is implemented through a Deep Q-Network and evaluated under multiplier-based and learnable SDR-shaped pricing, with a fixed-parameter SDR variant as a non-learning control. Performance is assessed through community savings together with complementary financial and operational indicators. In the base PV-only configuration, the rule-based benchmarks outperform the best RL policy. With battery energy storage, evaluated for the RL policies only, community savings under the best RL policy increase from EUR 734.23 to EUR 978.52. Across the learning-based modes and in both configurations, SDR-shaped pricing outperforms the multiplier-based parameterization considered. The results indicate that rule-based pricing remains highly competitive wherever the two families are compared directly, and that storage substantially improves the learning-based outcomes under this accounting, while the distribution of benefits remains heterogeneous across households.

</details>


### [247] [Median-of-Means as an Extremal Convex Estimator and a Nonconvex Route to the Trimmed Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01689)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从确定性优化视角重新审视中位数均值（median-of-means）估计，提出一族非凸 block-Lp 估计量，用于重尾与对抗污染数据下的鲁棒学习。作者证明所有凸块 M-估计量的最坏情形鲁棒常数至少为 1/(1−2ε)，无法达到修剪块预言常数 1/(1−ε)；而 p∈(0,1) 的非凸 block-Lp 估计量的全局极小值边界可随 p→0 连续逼近预言常数，且具有良性景观（无坏吸引域）。结合块级浓度，可得到在有限 2+δ 阶矩下的次高斯偏差界，并推广至高维鲁棒均值估计与稀疏回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典中位数均值方法在重尾与对抗污染数据下具有鲁棒性，但其凸优化形式存在固有的鲁棒性极限：凸块 M-估计量的最坏情形鲁棒常数被限制为 1/(1−2ε)，无法达到更优的修剪块预言常数 1/(1−ε)。论文旨在突破凸类限制，构造能在有限样本下逼近最优预言鲁棒常数的非凸估计框架，并保证优化景观与统计界可兼得。

Method: 论文首先在块污染模型（至少 1−ε 比例的块为“好块”）中证明：任何凸块 M-估计量的最坏情形鲁棒常数下界为 1/(1−2ε)，与经典中位数均值界匹配，从而排除凸类达到预言常数的可能。随后引入非凸 block-Lp 估计族（p∈(0,1)），推导所有全局极小值点的确定性有限样本鲁棒界；当 p 从 1 降至 0 时，这些界连续逼近修剪块预言常数，且在温和分离条件下小 p 的全局极小值与预言极小值重合。进一步分析显示该目标的景观良性，所有局部极小值接近真实参数且无坏吸引域。最后将块级浓度与上述结果结合，导出有限 2+δ 阶矩下的次高斯偏差界，并给出高维鲁棒均值估计与稀疏回归的推广。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 凸类下界定理——所有凸块 M-估计量在最坏情形下的鲁棒常数至少为 1/(1−2ε)，与中位数均值界一致，证明凸类无法实现修剪块预言常数 1/(1−ε)；(2) 非凸 block-Lp 估计族的确定性有限样本鲁棒界，并随 p 减小连续逼近预言常数；(3) 小 p 下全局极小值与预言估计一致（在分离条件下）；(4) 良性景观证明——所有局部极小值接近真值、无坏吸引域；(5) 结合块级浓度得到 2+δ 阶矩条件下的次高斯偏差界，以及高维鲁棒均值估计与稀疏回归的扩展结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：在鲁棒估计中凸性本身构成统计最优性的瓶颈，通过非凸 block-Lp 目标可同时获得接近修剪块预言的鲁棒常数、良性优化景观以及次高斯统计保证，为重尾与对抗污染数据下的鲁棒学习（含高维均值估计与稀疏回归）提供了理论完备的新框架。

Abstract: We revisit median-of-means estimation from a deterministic optimization viewpoint and develop a family of block-Lp estimators for robust learning with heavy-tailed and adversarially corrupted data. In a block contamination model with at least a fraction 1 minus epsilon of good blocks, we first show that every convex block M-estimator has worst-case robustness constant at least 1 divided by 1 minus 2 epsilon. This matches the classical median-of-means bound and proves that the trimmed-block oracle constant 1 divided by 1 minus epsilon cannot be attained within the convex class. We then introduce a nonconvex block-Lp family for p between 0 and 1 and derive finite-sample deterministic robustness bounds for all global minimizers. As p decreases from 1 toward 0, these bounds continuously approach the trimmed-block oracle constant. For sufficiently small p, the global minimizers coincide with those of the oracle under a mild separation condition. We also show that the block-Lp objectives have a benign landscape, with all local minima remaining close to the truth and no bad basins. Combining these results with block-level concentration yields sub-Gaussian deviation bounds under finite 2 plus delta moments and high-dimensional extensions to robust mean estimation and sparse regression.

</details>


### [248] [Tri-Band Channel Measurement-Enabled Multi-Layer Digital Twin for Terahertz Wireless Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01699)
*Mingjie Zhu,Ziming Yu,Guangjian Wang,Chong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向太赫兹（THz）无线数据中心、由测量驱动的多层数字孪生（DT）框架，包含物理层、信道层、评估层与操控层。基于140/220/300 GHz三频段实测数据建立测量校准的物理孪生，并利用视线（LoS）感知隐式神经场构建AI信道孪生，实现接收功率与LoS概率的实时预测；系统级评估层分析AP-to-rack与rack-to-rack的覆盖和干扰。实验表明AI孪生重建误差低于现有神经场基线且支持实时推理，天花板部署AP在10 dB SINR阈值下覆盖率超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算的快速增长对数据中心互联提出了灵活、大容量的需求；THz通信凭借超宽带宽和高空间复用能力成为未来无线数据中心的候选方案，而数字孪生可支持高效的无线规划与实时优化。现有方法缺乏基于实测的多层孪生框架，难以同时实现准确的传播建模、高效信道重建与系统级评估。

Method: 提出自底向上构建物理层、信道层、评估层和操控层的多层数字孪生框架。首先在140、220、300 GHz开展信道测量以刻画频率相关传播特性；随后联合优化几何、材料、天线和混合传播模型建立测量校准的物理孪生；在此基础上，以LoS感知的隐式神经场构建AI信道孪生，从校准孪生中学习位置相关的信道统计量，实现接收功率和LoS概率的实时预测；最后构建系统级评估层，分别分析AP-to-rack与rack-to-rack通信的覆盖与干扰。

Result: 实验表明，所提AI信道孪生在接收功率重建误差上优于现有神经场基线，同时保持实时推理能力；天花板安装AP在10 dB SINR阈值下覆盖率达90%以上，验证了框架对THz无线数据中心规划与优化的有效性。

Conclusion: 该测量驱动的多层数字孪生框架能够将实测信道特征、校准物理孪生、AI信道重建与系统级评估有机结合，为THz无线数据中心的规划与优化提供了高效、准确且可实时运行的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI computing has driven increasing demands for flexible and high-capacity data-center interconnections. Owing to its ultra-wide bandwidth and high spatial reuse capability, terahertz (THz) communication has emerged as a promising solution for future wireless data centers, while digital twins (DTs) enable efficient wireless planning and real-time optimization. In this work, a measurement-driven multi-layer DT framework is proposed for THz wireless data centers, where the physical, channel, evaluation, and manipulation layers are progressively constructed from bottom to top. First, extensive channel measurements are conducted at 140, 220, and 300 GHz to characterize frequency-dependent propagation behaviors. Based on the tri-band measurements, a measurement-calibrated physical twin is established by jointly optimizing the geometry, material, antenna, and hybrid propagation models. On top of the physical twin, a line-of-sight (LoS)-aware implicit neural field is developed to construct an AI channel twin for efficient channel reconstruction. The proposed AI twin learns location-dependent channel statistics from the calibrated twin, enabling real-time prediction of received power and LoS probability. Building upon the reconstructed channel field, a system-level evaluation layer is derived to analyze coverage and interference for both AP-to-rack and rack-to-rack communications. Experimental results show that the proposed AI twin achieves lower power reconstruction error than existing neural-field baselines while maintaining real-time inference capability. Moreover, the ceiling-mounted AP deployment achieves over 90% coverage under a 10 dB signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) threshold, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed DT framework for THz wireless data-center planning and optimization.

</details>


### [249] [Generative Diffusion Surrogates with Analytical Variance Schedule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01705)
*Patrick Reichherzer,Gianluca Gregori,David N. Hosking,Subir Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式扩散模型的前向噪声率设定为物理系统均方位移（方差）的时间导数，从而把生成时间变成校准的输运时钟，使扩散模型在无需中间时刻物理输运数据的情况下，成为随机输运系统的概率化、时间分辨且能表征非高斯分布结构的替代模型。在湍流等离子体的弹道—扩散输运中，该替代模型无需调度调参即可匹配测试粒子分布、复现实验室测量的方差标度并跟踪模拟的峰度演化，支持标定仿真与基于似然的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机输运系统的理想替代模型应具备概率性、时间分辨性和非高斯分布表征能力；生成式扩散模型天然具备这些性质，但其噪声调度通常依赖启发式选择（如图像/音频生成中没有物理时钟）。而在输运问题中，方差（均方位移）常常可由宏观理论或经验标度获知，即使完整分布未知，这为校准扩散模型的噪声调度提供了物理依据。

Method: 将扩散模型的前向加噪率设定为系统方差的时间导数，把生成时间转化为经过校准的输运时钟。方差路径通过构造强制执行，而学习得到的分数（score）场表示从入口数据继承的非高斯结构沿该路径被平滑的过程；该方法不需要中间时刻的物理输运数据。

Result: 在湍流等离子体的弹道—扩散输运中，该替代模型无需噪声调度调参即可匹配测试粒子分布，复现实验室测量的方差标度，并跟踪模拟的峰度演化，从而实现了标定仿真与基于似然的推断。

Conclusion: 通过将已知的宏观方差标度注入扩散模型的噪声调度，可以把生成模型转化为物理上可信、时间可校准的随机输运替代模型，在缺乏完整分布或中间时刻数据时仍能提供可靠的概率预测与推断工具。

Abstract: Stochastic transport describes physical systems in which an initially structured distribution spreads under unresolved forcing, scattering, or heterogeneous media. Useful surrogates for such systems should be probabilistic, time-resolved, and able to represent non-Gaussian distributional structure. Generative diffusion models, which corrupt data with Gaussian noise and learn a reverse flow back to structured states, have these properties. Their noise schedules, however, are usually chosen heuristically: image and audio generation---the canonical use cases---provide no physical clock. In transport, by contrast, the variance, or mean-square displacement, is often known from macroscopic theory or empirical scaling even when the full distribution is not. Here we prescribe the forward noising rate as the time derivative of this variance, turning generative time into a calibrated transport clock. The variance path is enforced by construction, while the learned score field represents how non-Gaussian structure inherited from entrance data is smoothed along that path, requiring no intermediate-time physical transport data. For ballistic-to-diffusive transport in turbulent plasmas, the surrogate matches test-particle distributions, reproduces the laboratory-measured variance scale, and tracks the simulated kurtosis evolution without schedule tuning, enabling calibrated emulation and likelihood-based inference.

</details>


### [250] [RecKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with a Learnable Recursive Polynomial Basis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01729)
*Amirhosein Azarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RecKAN，用二阶多项式递推 R_{n+1}(x) = (ax²+bx+c)R_n(x) + (dx+e)R_{n-1}(x) 定义基函数本身，五个系数与网络联合学习，从而超越固定的经典基（B样条、Chebyshev、小波、Jacobi），并在多个分类/回归基准上超越参数匹配的 KAN 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN 用可学习的单变量函数替代固定标量权重，但现有变体仍固定基函数（B样条、Chebyshev、小波、Jacobi 多项式），只学习其上的组合权重。作者希望打破这一限制，让基本身可学习，从而超越任何固定经典选择，获得更强的表达能力和可解释性。

Method: 定义由二阶多项式递推生成的基函数族，其五个系数 (a,b,c,d,e) 与网络权重联合优化。理论上证明该递推能恢复多种经典多项式族（两类 Chebyshev、Fibonacci、Pell、Jacobsthal），并证明仅在包含所有这些族的子族上次数随 n 线性增长，从而刻画"可学习基"如何超越固定经典基。实验中在分类、时间序列、函数拟合、CNN 分类头等场景下与参数匹配的 Chebyshev/ Jacobi/样条 KAN 及 MLP 对比，并分析学习到的递推系数的可解释性。

Result: 在图像、文本、生物医学时间序列分类和时间序列预测等多个基准上，RecKAN 在所有分类任务上优于三个参数匹配的 KAN 基线（Chebyshev、Jacobi、样条），并在 ETTh1 预测上取得最低 MSE；作为卷积骨干的分类头时，在 Fashion MNIST、CIFAR-10、SVHN 上精度高于标准 MLP 头；在合成函数拟合上能捕捉剧烈振荡目标而参数相当的 MLP 欠拟合。可解释性方面，在最需要局部结构的任务上，训练将基移出包含所有经典族的线性次数增长区域，与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: RecKAN 表明让基函数本身可学习（而非仅学习基上的权重）能显著提升 KAN 的表达能力与泛化性能，同时生成的可学习递推系数具有可解释性，其偏离经典族的行为符合理论预测，为可学习基的 KAN 设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed scalar weights of a standard network with learnable univariate functions on each edge, but existing variants still fix the \emph{basis} that those functions are built from: B-splines, Chebyshev polynomials, wavelets, or Jacobi polynomials, and learn only the combination weights over it. We introduce RecKAN, which instead defines the basis itself by a second order polynomial recurrence, $R_{n+1}(x) = (ax^2+bx+c)R_n(x) + (dx+e)R_{n-1}(x)$, whose five coefficients are learned jointly with the network. We show this recurrence recovers several classical polynomial families including both kinds of Chebyshev polynomials, Fibonacci, Pell, and Jacobsthal polynomials as special cases, and prove that its degree grows linearly in $n$ exactly on the sub-family containing all of them, giving a concrete sense in which the learned basis can move beyond any fixed classical choice. Across multiple benchmark datasets spanning image, text, biomedical time series classification, and time series forecasting, RecKAN outperforms three parameter-matched KAN baselines (Chebyshev, Jacobi, and spline based) on all classification tasks and achieves the lowest MSE on the ETTh1 forecasting benchmark. Additionally, when used as a classifier head with a convolutional backbone, RecKAN achieves higher accuracy than standard MLP heads on Fashion MNIST, CIFAR-10, and SVHN. On a synthetic function fitting benchmark it tracks a sharply oscillatory target that a parameter comparable MLP under fits. We further show that the learned recurrence coefficients are interpretable: on the task requiring the most local structure, training moves the basis away from the linear degree growth regime that contains every classical family we identify, consistent with our theoretical analysis of what that structural shift enables.

</details>


### [251] [CAT-Flow: Curvature-Adaptive sTeps for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01746)
*Qinchan Li,Pedro Cisneros-Velarde,Keru Fu,Samuel Antunes Miranda,Sharan Vaswani,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种轻量级、无需训练的推理时自适应步长算法 CAT-OV 与 CAT-OT，用于加速 Flow Matching（流匹配）的 ODE 采样。二者基于流匹配采样与梯度流之间的新联系，通过有限差分或向量场梯度近似曲率来动态调整步长，无需额外神经网络前向计算，并具有常数阶截断误差界。在四个文生图流匹配模型上，相比现有步长启发式方法，它们以最多减少 40% 的采样步数达到同等图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching 虽已成为生成建模的主流框架（如 FLUX、Stable Diffusion 3.5），但其基于 ODE 的迭代采样存在根本性效率瓶颈：生成质量对步长选择高度敏感，现有模型通常需要 20–30 步才能获得良好质量，导致采样成本高昂。

Method: 方法基于流匹配采样与梯度流的新联系，提出两种推理时自适应步长算法：CAT-OT 通过向量场时间导数的有限差分近似来估计曲率；CAT-OV 通过向量场的状态空间梯度近似曲率。两者均为轻量级、无需训练，且不增加额外的神经网络函数评估；在适当条件下具有常数阶截断误差界。

Result: 在四个文生图 Flow Matching 模型上，CAT-OV 与 CAT-OT 在图像质量指标上均优于现有步长启发式方法，可将达到可比质量所需的生成步数减少高达 40%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过曲率感知的推理时自适应步长控制，可在不训练、不增加额外网络评估开销的前提下显著提升流匹配采样的效率，为加速基于 ODE 的生成模型提供了简单有效的通用方案。

Abstract: Flow Matching has emerged as a leading framework for generative modeling, powering state-of-the-art systems such as FLUX and Stable Diffusion 3.5. However, the iterative nature of its ODE-based sampling process creates a fundamental efficiency bottleneck: the quality of generated samples is highly sensitive to the choice of step-sizes, and current models typically require 20 to 30 steps for good quality. In this work, we propose two lightweight, training-free algorithms, CAT-OV and CAT-OT that adapt step-sizes at inference time based on a novel connection between Flow Matching sampling and gradient flow. Our algorithms are computed efficiently by not requiring additional neural function evaluations. Specifically, CAT-OT estimates curvature over time via a finite-difference approximation of the time-derivative of the vector field, while CAT-OV approximates curvature over the state space via a gradient of the vector field. Under suitable conditions, both methods have truncation error bounds of constant order. Empirically, CAT-OV and CAT-OT outperform existing step-size heuristics in image quality metrics across four text- to-image Flow Matching models, reducing the number of generation steps required to reach comparable quality by up to 40%.

</details>


### [252] [A Study of Conditional Diffusion Models for Open-Loop Control under Dry Friction and Stiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01756)
*Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将动作序列扩散模型（Action Diffusion）用作开放回路（open-loop）规划中的提议分布，针对具有干摩擦和静摩擦（stiction）的点质量系统。基于条件1D U-Net生成有界的控制序列，并与均匀随机采样、基于结构化数据集先验的随机采样及交叉熵方法（CEM）对比，结果表明Action Diffusion在低采样数量场景下能显著降低终端误差和卡滞步数，通过条件化与重组训练先验中的结构化控制原语生成时序连贯的控制序列以克服静摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型作为表达力强的生成先验已用于规划与控制，但现有工作较少关注摩擦系统这类控制输入需超过静摩擦阈值才能启动运动的场景。在此类基准中，有效控制只占据动作序列空间中一个小而具有时序结构特征的子集，传统随机采样方法难以高效覆盖该区域，因此需要一种能生成时序连贯控制序列的机制。

Method: 采用动作扩散（Action Diffusion）公式，即对动作序列进行扩散建模，并将其用作开放回路规划的提议分布；使用一个紧凑的条件化1D U-Net，以初始状态和目标状态为条件，生成有界控制序列；在包含干摩擦与静摩擦的点质量系统基准上，与均匀随机射击、从同一结构化数据集先验的随机射击以及交叉熵方法（CEM）进行对比实验。

Result: 实验结果显示，Action Diffusion在终端误差和卡滞步数上均优于对比方法，尤其在低采样数量（low-sample）场景下优势更明显，说明其能够更有效地生成有效控制序列。

Conclusion: 条件扩散为生成时序连贯的控制序列提供了有效机制：通过对训练先验中的结构化控制原语进行条件化与重组，能够在状态到状态的开放回路控制中克服静摩擦，展现出作为控制提议分布的价值。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as expressive generative priors for planning and control. This paper studies Action Diffusion, an action-sequence diffusion formulation used as an open-loop proposal distribution for a point-mass system with dry friction and stiction. In this benchmark, motion starts only when the applied input exceeds a static-friction threshold, so effective controls occupy a small and temporally structured subset of the action-sequence space. A compact conditional 1D U-Net generates bounded control sequences conditioned on initial and target states. We compare it with uniform random shooting, random shooting from the same structured dataset prior, and the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). Results show that Action Diffusion reduces terminal error and stuck steps, especially in low-sample regimes. These results indicate that conditional diffusion provides an effective mechanism for generating temporally coherent control sequences that overcome stiction by conditioning and recombining structured control primitives from the training prior for state-to-state open-loop control.

</details>


### [253] [Toward Explainable and Policy-Aware AI for Carbon Credit Price Prediction: A Research Framework for Emerging Carbon Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01765)
*Summaiya Unnisa Begum,Mohammed Nadeem Ullah,Mohammed Abdul Ghani Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对碳市场价格预测领域，论文系统梳理了十类常见研究缺口，提出融合市场序列与政策文本的六层架构 EPA-CarbonNet（含交叉注意力、校准区间与政策归因解释），并在11年S&P碳指数日数据上测试。结果以负面发现为主：随机游走模型在五日RMSE上优于该模型（0.0365 vs 0.0475），SHAP排序跨背景重采样仅rho=0.54，政策注意力未与监管事件重合；但方向准确率58.6%领先所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有碳价预测研究存在系统性缺陷：研究对象集中于欧盟与中国机制、将监管文本简单压缩为情感分数、仅报告准确率而缺乏校准与解释稳定性评估。这些缺口导致模型难以在真实碳市场中落地与可信使用。

Method: 作者将十类反复出现的缺口整理为“影响-可行性矩阵”，并提出EPA-CarbonNet：六层架构通过交叉注意力融合市场行情序列与政策文本，同时输出校准后的预测区间及可归因于政策的解释。在11年S&P碳指数日频数据上构建并测试。

Result: 结果是“大体负面”并如实报告：五日RMSE上随机游走（0.0365）优于所提模型（0.0475）；SHAP重要性排序在不同重采样背景下仅rho=0.54，解释稳定性不足；政策注意力与已记录的监管事件从未重合。唯一正向结果为方向准确率58.6%，领先所有基线。

Conclusion: 作者以可复现方式（开源代码、数据文档与结果工件）呈现负面结果，说明碳价点值预测的复杂性与政策文本融合的困难，同时表明方向预测可能更具实际价值，为后续研究提供可信基准与缺口清单。

Abstract: Carbon markets put a price on emissions, yet that price remains hard to forecast. Work in this area clusters on the EU and Chinese schemes, compresses regulatory text into a sentiment score, and reports accuracy without calibration or explanation stability. We distil ten recurring gaps into an impact-feasibility matrix and propose EPA-CarbonNet, a six-layer architecture that fuses market series with policy text by cross-attention and calibrated intervals alongside policy-attributed explanations. We then build and test it on eleven years of daily S and P carbon index data. The findings are largely negative, and reported as measured: a random walk beats the model on five-day RMSE (0.0365 against 0.0475), SHAP rankings agree at rho = 0.54 across resampled backgrounds, and policy attention never coincides with documented regulatory events. Directional accuracy, at 58.6 percent, leads every baseline. Code, data documentation and all result artifacts are available at https://github.com/Kimalice/Toward-Explainable-and-Policy-Aware-AI-for-Carbon-Credit-Price-Prediction

</details>


### [254] [Emergence of Fibrations, Compression, and Symmetry Breaking in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01768)
*Osvaldo M Velarde,Lucas C Parra,Alireza Hashemi,Hernan A Makse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明深度神经网络学习过程中会产生图论意义上的纤维化与覆盖对称性，并证明覆盖对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子。实验表明该对称性广泛存在于多层、卷积、循环和 Transformer 等主流架构中，可用于将模型压缩至原规模的 17% 而不损失性能；通过受控地打破覆盖对称性，可克服持续学习中的可塑性丧失，取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络常被视为强大但不透明的黑盒，缺乏可解释性和高效推理、终身学习的理论基础。作者希望从对称性角度揭示深度学习的内部结构，为可解释性、模型压缩与持续学习提供新基础。

Method: 从图论视角将网络视为图，识别学习过程中自发产生的局部对称性（纤维化与覆盖）；理论上证明覆盖对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子；并在多层感知机、卷积、循环与 Transformer 架构上验证该现象；利用覆盖对称性实现模型压缩，并通过受控打破对称性改善持续学习中的可塑性。
